Fitowa ta..1⃣3⃣

Tasha matuqar wahala kapin ta isa Hutu kuwa tayishi yafi sau goma dakyar ta iso qofar gidan, ganin gidan a bude ne yatabbatar mata Mal.Habu yana ciki, kasa daga qafafunta tayi domin shiga don haka taxauna a qofar gidan domin tahuta.


Aciki kuwa Mal.Habu ne da Amaryansa a zaune yabude mata Gasashshiyar naman da yasayo mata, inda itakuma yawu yagama wanke mata cikin bakinta gaba daya miyonta yagama tsinkewa, Allah Allah take yagama budewa, aiko bata jiraba ta fusgi ledan hadi da cemishi kawo megida na hutasheka.

Tafara tauna kamar Wanda bata taba cinnama a rayuwarta ba (Wlh ciwo Daya Lami xatai maka sai anyi maka alluran Tetanus😜).

  Har lumshe idanu takeyi don dadi fadi take a ranta yaushe gamo?  Kafin ai haka ai haka ta kammala da naman, mamaki ne yacika zuciyar Mal.Habu saboda baiyi tsammanin xata iya cinyewa ita kadai ba koda wasa, snn bata damu dashi mexai ci ba, maimakon yaji haushinta saima qara burgeshi yaji tayi.


Bayan tagama hutawa ne, tamiqe tayi cikin gidan Hango dakinta tayi da haske saida xuciyarta tabada wani sauti daramdaram, saida ta dafe qirjinta qirjinta snn tasamu kuzarin  matsawa kusa da dakin kapin tayi magana kukan diyarta yarigata miqa sallaman..

Waye ne abunda yafito knn abakin Mal.Habu, nice Zainabu tafada cikin siririyar muryanta, tsintan kanshi yayi da kasa koda motsa bakinsa balle yayi tunanin zai iya tashi ya leqa ta. Ganin ba'a ansa mata bane yasata qarayin sallaman a karo na biyu, Lami ce tamiqe tadaga labule nan sukayi tozali da juna, Zainabu bakinta yana  rawa tafurta L...A...M...I 😳 kece tanuna ta da Dan yatsa, nicepa yadason ranki? Mekikeyi a gidana, dakina kuma atareda mijina? 
Mtswww miji hahhahahhaha miji pa.


Habu tafada kanta tsaye shiko jiki na kyarma yamiqe ya iso garesu, Zainabu ne ta mitstsini jikinta domin tabbatarwa kanta ba mafarki takeyi ba, tabbas a zahiri ne,  tsinkayan muryan Lami tayi tanacewa Habu nabaka minti biyu kacal kayiwa matarka bayanin matsayi na kadawo cikin daki..


Kallon Zainabu yayi rai Abace yace, dama bakida hankali? Xaki nufo dakin mata batareda sallama ba? Megida nayi sallama tafadi hakan tana girgiza NANA datake bayanta, to kibude kunnuwanki dakyau ki saurareni Lami yanxu Matata ce snn wnn dakin dakika samemu a ciki dakinta ne Ayanxu, kinga can? Yanuna mata _kitchen_ bin hannuwansa tayi da kallo tafahimci inda yanuna mata, daga mishi kai tayi alamar tafahimta, to shine dakinki zabi yarage naki inkinga zaki zauna to idan baxaki iyaba ga hanya, yadaga mata kafada irin baruwanshin nan🤷🏻‍♂.


Tinda yafara maganganun nasa take addu'oi acikin zuciyarta shiyasa cikin qarfin guiwa tanupi cikin dakin batare da tanajin qunan zuciya ba, cikin dakin taleqa batasan inda haskenta yakeba, kuma tanatsoron xuwa taroqesu batasan dawani fuska xasu kalleta ba, shiyasa ta lalubi gurin harta yi sa'a taji hanunta akan gado, kwantar da NANA tayi snn tafito waje domin tayi alwala.


Tajima bayan ta idar da sallah, xama tayi tana addu'oi sai a wnn lokacin taji kuka ya kwace mata, tayi kuka tayi kuka hartaji kanta yana amsawa tayi kukan maraici tarasa kafadan da xata kwanta ajiki domin taji sanyi.


A dakin Lami kuwa bayan tagama cin namanta tashare hannunta ajikin zanin gadon Snn tacewa Mal.Habu taso ka hauro mukwanta tafadi tana hawa kan gadon bayan ta tube kayan jikinta yabarta daga ita sai fallan zani, binta yayi da idanu aqasan ransa yarasa menene abun burgewa a jikin Lami, amma kuma bayajin xai iya kusheta kokuma wulaqanta ta.


Lami kikai min ruwa in watsa tukunna bana iya bacci batareda nayi wanka, ba, Zaburowa tayi a gadon tareda tsareshi da idanuwa inda atake yaji yayi la'asar, itakuma tace mishi Mal.Habu dama ka aureni don inbautu maka a gidanka ne? A'ah yafada cikin rawar baki.

    To bari ingaya maka daga gobe Zainabu ce zatana yimana girki da duk wani abunda takeyi maka a da, Amma kasani bakai ba kwana a dakinta domin batada kwana.kaji? Dasauri yace mata Naji, yamiqe yanufi waje domin ya hada ruwa yayi wanka.

Fitarsa keda wuya itakuma tadauko man shafawanta data zuba maganin aciki snn tadansa mishi turare domin kore mishi warin maganin. Bai wani Dade ba yadawo nan ta taso cikin kirsa, ta tarbeshi Dauko zaninta tayi acikin akwati Badon a gaban idonsa ta ciro ba to bashakka xaice yashekara ajikinta baiga ruwa ba, haka yanaji yana gani togoge mishi jiki tsab da wnn zanin, baigama dawowa hayyacinsa daga duniyar wari ba tafara mulkeshi da wnn man.


Ainan yakusa shidewa yaji wani jaraba Wanda tunda ya balaga baitaba jin irinsa ba, ganin yafara binta da wani mayataccen kallo ne, yasata fadawa jikinsa rijib nantake labari tacanxa,inda nafara jiyo gurnanai kala kala, kamar wadanda suke sauke nauyin shekara Dari.

  _Aguje nafito daga dakin har ina tuntube saboda tsoron irin abubuwanda sukeyi yasha qarfin ganin idanuna, Nasan kunason insanar daku to naqi😜)_


Anan bacci ya kwasheta tsakan dare wani firgitaccen kukan NANA ne yatada ta nan ta lalubo ta domin saka mata nono abaki, amma taqi karba tasha, lallashinta tafara, amma taqiyin shiru, miqewa tayi tagoya ta, nanma abun baisauya zaniba, saima qara sautin kukanta tayi, kuncota tayi abayan, duk abunda tasan xatai mata domin yin shiru tayi, amma Sam NANA batasan tanayi ba, aqarshe itama fashewa tayi da kukan ta durqushe a qasa, tana Kiran sunan Allah domin yakawo mata agaji.


Tun Mal.Habu yana fitoda ruwa har abun ya gagareshi bakin mazaqutarsa sai zogi takemishi, qarfinshi yaqare daga yayi yunqurin sauka akanta xata kuma rarumosa,saida suka shafe lokaci me tsawo ta kyaleshi badon tagaji ba saidon taga yana Neman shidewa, gefe Yakoma yanata numfarfashin azaba, itakuma na dadi, sai a wnn lokacin suka fara jiyo kukan Zainabu da 'yarta, Lami tagaji tanason bacci dalilin hakan ranta tabaci tacewa Mal.Habu yaje yasan yanda xaiyi dasu tanason tayi bacci.


Shikuwa miqewa yayi yanufi dakin Zainabu, duk a zatonta taumakonta yaxoyi hakan yasata rungumo 'yarta suka fito aguje, qiqam taganshi a tsaye duk da akwae duhuwa hasken farin wata yanuna mata yanda yahade fuskarsa, hakan yasa tasha jinin jikinta.

Cikin tsawa yake mata magana, ke yanxu Zainabu keda wnn abunnaki yanuna Sadiya, baxaku bar mutane su huta bako? Wlh Zainabu naqarajin duriyar tashin kukanku acikin gidan nan domin hana matata samun isashshen bacci xanyi maganinki xankuma dauki hukuncinda baki taba tsammanin xan iya dauka akanki ba, fuuu Yakoma dakin matarsa.


Tsayuwar ne yafara gagaranta, donhaka tarungumi diyarta dakyau taje tabude qofa a hankali tayi waje, a gindin bishayar qofar gidan  tasamu dutse me fadi taxauna cikin ikon Allah NANA tayi bacci, dadi taji yakamata ganin 'yar tata tasamu bacci, bata San lokacinda bacci me qarfi yayi gaba da itaba


Batasan iya lokacinda tayi wnn baccinba harsanda haske yafara dallare mata idanuwa a firgice tafarka da Ambaton sunan Allah abakinta, tamiqe tayi cikin gidan, batasamu kowa a tsakar gidanba don haka tayi cikin dakinta, alokacinne tasamu damar kallon gurin da kyau, bakin gadonta taje zata zauna idonta yasauqa akan wata qatuwar kunama, miqewa tayi, amma datai tunanin babu mai taimakonta koda tafita asalima saidai kunamar tagudu tashiga wani gurin yasata, Aro qarfin xuciya ta dasa Aranta cikin dabara ta nannade zanin gadon tasauko dashi qasa snn tasamo sanda takashe shi tana kuka


Sauko da 'yarta tayi daga baya ta tube mata Riga kukanta ne ya tsananta a lokacinda tayi arba da kumburarren bayan NANA bayan nata yayi jaa, kana gani kasan tanashan Azaba, cikin kukan tafara tofa mata duk addu'arda tazo bakinta.

Zainabu fitonan daqarfi tayi maganar taji muryan Lami a qofar dakinta, gyara zamanta tayi a dakin don bata tunanin itama xata sarara mata, dukda haryanxu tarasa amsoshin tambayoyin daya addabin zuciyarta, ganin tana bata mata lokacin ne, yasata fadawa dakin...*****



😍😍🤝🏼 
Mummyn Fadyl CE .

 *Ciwon cikin barewa a daji waxai bata kanwa, Allah daya ya halicci barewa a daji  shixai bata magani,
Haqiqa Allah shike iyammini* 


 Fitowa ta..1⃣4⃣

Fadawa dakin tayi cikin fushi, tsayawa tayi a gabanta snn tanunata da 'yatsa Lallai Zainabu wuyanki yayi kauri ya isa yanka, kinsan dawa kikeyi kuwa? Hummm zanbaki mamaki wlh saina maidake abun kwatance acikin unguwarnan, kiran da Sadiya tai mata ne yasata fitowa batareda ta kammala maganganunta ba..

  Menene Sadiya? Umma yunwa nakeji, tuntuni nake jiranki kifito amma kin qule can dakin, snn kinsan munyi maganar komawa gidan Umma Ayashe akan Alhajin nan amma bakicemin komai ba.

   Kiyi haquri Sadiya yanxu xa'a fito ayi abun kari, snn maganar Alhajin kuma kibarshi yanxu pa ba kamar Da bane damuke cikin buqata, dukkan  abunda kike so kiymin magana nikuma zanmiki shi.
To shknn Umma tajuya takoma dakinta.


Lami CE takoma dakinta, samun Mal.Habu tayi yanajin labarai, nanfa tazauna ta shiryamishi qarya da gaxkiya gameda abunda yashiga tsakaninta da Zainabu, shikuma hawa yafarayi kamar farashi, yamiqe a harziqe yayi dakin Zainabun, inda yasameta tana bawa 'yarta nono. Kapin taimata wanka gawani Azababben yunwa dayake addabarta.

Gaxkiya Zainabu baxan lamunci kiraina min mata tah ba, saboda Lami macece da bata qaunar tashin hankali, tana farkawa a gadonta da sunanki ta tashi kan cewa zataxo taduba lafiyarku snn taimiki maganar abun kari, amma kuma kirasa  maganar daxaki gaya mata saina rashin mutunci?

   To wlh baxan lamunci hakan ba, tunda na fahimci kedai ba Diyar arziqi bace, kuma Lami batada isashshen lpy, daga yau kece zaki nayin girki a gidannan, kuma yanxu basai anjima ba kifito kihura wuta...

Har yagama babatunsa, bata daga ido takalleshi ba harsanda taji maganarshi na qarshe, kuma kisani Aurenki yana lanqaye ne a wuyan Lami wlh duk ranar da kika nemi uzzira wa matata to kisani A ranar zamanki atare dani yaqare, A razane tadago jiqaqqun idanunta ta kalleshi shikuma yayi waje abunsa 


(Karfa kumanta Mal.Habu har yau baisan kamannin 'yarsa kwaya daya tilo a duniya ba.😭)

Baqin cikinta da yake cinta ne, yasata miqewa cikin hanzari ta wanki 'yarta snn ta kwantar da ita, don kuwa tadauka wa kanta alqawarin  Mal.Habu baxai taba Ganin 'yarta ba, tunda bainemi hakan ba, xata cigaba da killaceta tareda yimata addu'oin tsari, domin yanxuka abun tafara tabbatarwa kanta akwae surkullen qulli a tattare da al'amarin.

  Fitowa tayi tanupi _kitchen_ domin tahura wuta, amma me Lami ce tai mata tsaye tana zuba mata ruwan bala'i saboda tashiga mata madafi, kifita kihura wuta a qofar dakinki, badon Lami din tafito ba, saidon darajar aurenta, murmushin takaici tayi tafito tanufi qofar dakinta tahura wuta, tsayawa tayi domin tarasa mexata daura.

 Sadiya ce takece mata gashi, dagowa tayi taganta a tsaye akanta tana miqa mata taliya, karba tayi, cikin qanqanin lokaci ta kammala, tana qoqarin saukewa Lami tafito ta iso gareta, karban tukunyar tayi a hannunta, tai wucewarta dashi _kitchen_ .

Miqewa tayi batareda tanuna tafahimci abunda Lami take nupi da abunda ta aikata gareta ba, ruwa ta deba, taje ta watso ruwa tadawo tashirya, snn tagoya diyarta, kikkiintsa cikin dakinnata tayi, acikin kwalemar da takeyi ne tayi Arba da kudaden da Mal.Habu yake bata a da, kudin yakai kimanin dubu shida, lokacin batasan amfaninsu xai risketa a Dan qanqanin likaci haka, ba, hawaye taji yana gangaro mata sakamakon tunowa datayi da ranar daya miqa mata kudin naqarshe.

"Ki karba nidai koda baxakiy amfani dashi ba, ai baxaki rasa baqi masu xuwa miki ba saiki sallamesu dashi, cemishi tayi to ai wadanda kabani mapa sunanan banyi amfani dasu ba, acinyarta ya ajiye mata, snn yayi waje" Allah sarki rayuwa.

A take kuma ta tuno da Babanta, tanason xuwa  taduba halinda yake ciki, donkuwa tanada tabbacin indai yana halin lpy to babu abinda xai hanashi xuwa, hakannema yake qara daga mata hankali, gashi batada Wanda takedashi daxai taimaketa yaje yadubo mata shi, da wnn tunanin tanemi yunwar dake Addaban nata tarasa shi.


Tunaninta yaqare akan taje ta tambayi Mal.Habu izini koxai barta tasamu taje tadubo mahaifinta, amma kuma batajin zata iya shiga wnn dakin saboda zai daga mata hankali, miqin da tasamu ta liqeshi ne xai balle mata.


Fitowa tsakar gida tayi tasamu Sadiya, roqonta tayi akan tayiwa Bbn magana, fitowar Sadiya tasanar da ita Ummanta tace tashiga, Shahada tayi tashiga cikin dakin da sallama.

Da gayya Lami takwanta ajikin Mal.Habu sai shafa shi takeyi, abun babu kyan gabi, shiko saiwani lumshe idanu yakeyi yana tande baki kamar tsohon baki, kawar dakai gefe tayi snn tagaidashi duk da tasan ba amsata xaiyi ba.

Sanar mishi tayi da buqatarta amma yayi tambar ba'ayi ruwan tsiranta a gurinba, tashafe kimanin Rabin awa, harta cire rai akan zaimata magana tana shirin miqewa taji muryan Lami da kece mata kitashi kije Amma kitabbatar baki wuce Rabin Awa ba, saboda xakiyi abincin rana.

   Bata jira taji sauran bayanan nataba tayi dakinta tashirya kanta takuma shirya diyarta, sauda dama takan manta cewar jikinta danye ne, jikin jego, saiyanxu tagane Gata ne yakesa mace rasa qarfin jikinta bayan tahaihu, don kuwa ita yanxu yanda duniya tabugeta kallo daya xakai mata kayi 2nanin haihuwarta goma.

   'Yan kudadenta ta kwasa, tafita tanupi hanyar gidansu, mai adaidaita ta tara, saida taratse shagon dake bakin layinsu tasaya musu kayan buqata itada NANA snn suka qarasa gidan.

    Da sallama tashiga inda tasamu Zuwaira tana yiwa Diyar maqotansu kitso maimakon ta amsa gaisuwarda take mata saima haurata da baqaqen maganganu tayi, fadi take, Kedai wnn yarinyar anyi asararriya kuma tambadaddiya, ubanki ya kwanta jinya kwana dayawa ba'aga qafarki ba bayan duk a dalilin son xuciyarku keda shi yatsinci kansa awnn halin. 
   

   Kedai Zainabu nama rasa da wani suna xan alaqantaki dashi saidai ince miki KARIYA, kirasa dawa zaki kwana saida ubanki, asirinku ya tonu Mutan gari kowa yasan abunda kuka aikata, ace har asibiti Ubanki yabiki kuka kwana.


Tsananin mamaki ne yasa Zainabu kasa koda motsa 'yan yatsunta ne, ayaune tasan kuka ma rahama ne, don kuwa nemanshi tayi tarasa,  saiki miqe kishiga kisameshi yana daki.....

A guje tashige cikin dakin Bbn nata tasameshi baya koda motsawa banda bakinsa datake kallon alamar yana son magana, da sassarfa ta isa gurinshi takai bakinta saitin kunnensa,  ahankali yakece mata, Zainabu na kiyi haquri da duk wulaqancin rayuwa, komai yanada qarshe, kiriqe ibada, Ruwa Ruwa xansha kikawo min ruwa to tace tamiqe hartana tuntube, ruwan tadebo tashigo dashi dakin amma.....

Mummyn Fadyl CE

_Al'ajabi yana gurin kanuwa wacce take tsiro akan kogi, kaidai nemi dacewar ubangiji kaga ni._



Fitowa tah.1⃣5⃣



Ruwan tadebo tashigo dashi dakin amma qarasowanta tasamu yana salati, riqe hannayensa tayi qam a jikinta candai taji jikinsa yasake, duk da batasan meye mutuwa kuma Yaya yakeba, to tabbas xata iya cewa mahaifinta mutuwa yayi, jijjigashi tafarayi amma babu shi. Wani qara tasaki me qarfi Wanda daganan bata sake sanin inda kanta yakeba.


((kullu nafsin xa'ikatul maut))

Qarar ne yafirgito da Zuwairah taiyo cikin dakin bashiri, ganinda tayi hannunsu sarqe Dana junane yasata saurin nufarsu cire hannunshi tayi daga cikin na zainabu snn tafara kiran sunanshi tajijjigashi itama amma shiru nanfa tahau kurma ihu.


Kan kace kobo gida yashika maqil Zuwaira kuwa banda ihu dafadin nashiga uku na lalace ba babu abunda takeyi, ana nunamata fadin sunan Allah a wnn lokacin shixai taimaketa amma inaa bata fahimtar abund aka gayamata.  Ita tasan tafi kowa yin rashi don Mal.Dahiru tsayayye ne akan gidansa, bugu da qari baida tashin hankali, tanada tabbacin bamai iyawa halinta saishi, daga ta tuno irin wadannan abubuwan atake take qara daga hannayenta taxuba akanta tahau ihu tana burgima a qasa 'yan uwa suna dagata.


A cikin qanqanin lokaci jama'a suka taru inda kowa mutuwar Mal.Dahiru yatabashi saboda shina kowa ne, baida abokin tashin hankali bare gaba.

Maqocinsa Mal.Jafaru shikansa yasan yayi rashin Amini saida yamutu nema yaji lbrn Ashe yayi jinya saboda shi rabonda su hadu tun ranarda suka sanar dashi auren Lami.

   Babu abunda yafi tsayawa mishi a rai kamar murmushinda yayi musu bayan yaqi karban kudinda Habu yamiqa mishi na maganar jinyar Zainabu Sam abun yaqi barin idanunsa, wasu siraran hawaye ne suke zirarowa daga idonsa ....
 
  Miqewa yayi yatafi gidan Habu domin sanar da Habu rasuwar Sirikin nasa, yashafe a qalla minti Arba'in a qofar gida amma Habu baisamu fitowa ba gakuma lokacin jana'ixar takusa.

  Shikuwa Acikin gida yana daki a kwance suna hira da Lami akan Jarin da xaibata, akaiyo aikan Kiran da mahaifinnasa ke mishi, harcikin ransa yanason xuwa amma kuma yakasa tashi saboda ba'a bashi umarnin hakan ba.

Can dai Lami tace mishi nasan yanxu haka kudi yaxo kabashi, nikuma baxan lamunci suyi ta tatseka bayan baka wani Tara yaraba, baxai yiwu inxuba musu ido kana basu kudi yanda sukeso daga baya gida qara yimaka nauyi xaiyi, yasuke so muyi, haka nekam abunda ta iya fada kenan. Bani kudaden da ke aljihinka kafita mishi haka, inyaso kace mishi kwanannan kasuwar ba ciniki kuma bana buqatar kawuce minti ashirin kadawo gida, don Nima inada buqatarka.

 Kalmar da tadaga mishi hankali knn wai Buqata,shidai baisan wnn wace iriya bace, bata gajiya shiyanxu haka kunkuminshi ciwo yakemishi, amma ya lura ita ko ajikinta, itadai burinta a kwanta da ita, ai Buttt yamiqe yayi waje don daman yagaji da zaman Sam hirar ba dadi take mishi ba kamar akan qaya haka yakejinshi amma babu damar nuna hakan.


A qofar gida ya iske Bbn nan suka gaisa yake sanar dashi abunda yafaru, baisan dalili ba amma Sam baiji mutuwar tawani ratsashi ba baikuma yi jimamin taba, To saikaxo mutafi aje ayi jana'ixar dakai ko? Saboda lokacin yakusa yi don najima a qofar gidan naka.


Tsintar kansa yayi dacewa mahaifinnashi bari inshiga gida insanar da Lami idan ta Amince sai inzo muje, maimakon mahaifin nashi yaji haushi saima qara mishi qarfin guiwa yayi dacewa, eh wnn gaxkiya ne, mace ta gari irin Lami saida lallabi ae,yana washe baki, shikuma cikin jin dadi yakoma cikin gidan.. 

Sanar mata dayanda sukayi da mahaifinnashi yayi, itakua sai cewa tayi zauna anan nizanje inbashi amsar dakaina, komawa yayi cikin jin haushi bawai don xata  hanashi xua gurin jana'iza ba, a'a saidon baxai samu yaleqa koda qofar gida ba.


  Itako qerere ta tsayawa Mal.Jafaru Aka, shiyafara gaisheta batareda ta amsa ba tafara arattako mishi bayanai kamar haka, Gaxkiya Baba habu baxai samu binka ba, saboda baxaiyiwu ko sati banyi da Tarewa ba, afara dauke min miji ba, katafi kawae alabashshi kapin ayi sadakar bakwai yazo yayi muku gaisuwa, ai gaisuwa bata qarewa..


Maimakon yaji haushinta saima jiyayi a ransa tafishi gaxkiya ai bai kyautaba, yakuma fahimci shine mai laipi  dayazo daukan mijin nata, nanfa yafara bata haquri, akan babu damuwa idan yasamu lokaci yazo yy gaisuwar, bata tsaya sauraran Shiba tayi ciki tareda banko qofar haryadan samu goshinsa. Ko ajikinta.


Acikin Gidan Mal.Dahiru kuwa bayan angama mishi wanka yayi daidai da farfadowar zainabu, ganinta tayi a kwance ga jama'a sai kuka sukeyi kowa idan ya kalleta sai hawaye tareda tausayinta. Ahankali ta isa gurin Goggonta a wnn lokacin babu alamar kuka atareda ita.


Goggo kisanar dani meke faruwa kicemin ba Bbn bane yarasu, kisanar dani mafarki nakeyi, duk tana maganar ne hannayenta a dunqule da juna, kukan goggon nata ne yatsananta, can tai qarfin halin cemata, kiyi haquri kidauki dangana Allahn da yabamu shi yakarbi kayansa, Qarfin hali tayi tace, Goggo ankai Bbn? A'a goggo kirakani inganshi, miqewa tayi, tareda yimata jagora har cikin dakin.


Gashinan cikin suturarsa, amma ba'a rufe fuskarsa ba durqusawa tayi da guiwowinta a gabansa, kallonshi takeyi tarasa metakeji a ranta, saboda batajinta a mutum a wnn lokacin, balle tace tanada damar yin tunani.

Gaf da fuskarshi tamatsa takaranta mishi fatiha, yanda taga fuskarshi da murmushine, yasata sakin murmushin tausayin kanta snn tafara magana. Inafatan kyawawan halayyarka subika mahaifina, Nagode da tarbiyarda kaimin Allah ya kyautata makwancinka, tabbas xanyi maraici, xanyi rayuwar qunci kai kadae dakedashi *TUDUN DAFAWA* yaukuma anwayi gari babu kai

Meyasa xaka tafi kabarni a lokacinda nakeda buqatarka, alokacinda banida kowa saikai,? Meyasa rayuwata batada dadi kamar na kowa? Meyasa aduk lokacinda na samu Abu saiya tafiBanida kowa saikai Baba naxo sanar dakai rayuwata takoma a hagunce, mijina yajuya min baya, waxan gayawa sirrin xuciyata, Bb kai kadai ne kake saurarena kuma katausaya min Baba narasa ka banida *TUDUN DAFAWA* Banida kowa Banida kowa Baba katashi maraicin zaimin yawa nida 'yata Baba katashi tafara kuka tana jijjigashi, nan mutane sukayo kanta aka dagata tana fusgewa akayi waje da ita.


Can dakin Zuwaira aka shigar da ita, kowa yadawo da kukanshi kanta, saboda kapin kowa yasan dadinshi to ita tafara sani, snn kuma batada kowa saishi din.


  Hango mutane tayi anfito dashi a cikin Makara (motar kowa) a gigice tamiqe tayi Kansu tana Ku ajiyeshi kutausayamin karku fitar dashi daga gidannan acikinku waxai mayemin gurbinsa, waxai saurare ni, fitardashi waje sukayi inda itakuma tafadi agurin sumammiya😭😭😭. Kanta aka yiwo aka daga ta aka kaita cikin daki...



_Masu qorafi akan shafina baya yawa plx kuymin uxiri kunsan ni sabon shigane a system din, kunga kuwa sai ahankali, ina iyakacin qoqarina don ganin nafaranta ran fans dina, insha Allah xan gyara, Masoyana u mean alot to me_


😍😍🤝🏼 Anatare.
Mummyn Fadyl CE

Fitowa ta. 1⃣6⃣

Xainab saida tashare kwana uku batare da tasan inda kanta yakeba, a wadannan kwanakin  yarinyarta madara goggonta take bata takesha, A ranar da tadawo hayyacinta abub yadawo mata sabo, intana zaune saidai kaga ruwan hawaye yanabin kuncinta, babu magana a gurinta kam, intambaya ce saidai tabada amsa tareda gyada kanta, sama ko qasa.

 A waje gurin maza kowa yayi matuqar  mamakin rashin ganin Mal.Habu koda a gurin makoki ne, to hakaxalika acikin gida ma, inda akaita tambayarta ina yake? Basu amsa takeyi da cewar yayi tafiya.

****************

Randa akayi sadakar bakwai mutane duk sun watse, Zainabu tana daki tunanin duniya duk ya damu rayuwarta, diyarta take qarewa kallo yarinyar takoma tamkar qashi da rai, sakamakon rashin wadataccen ruwan Nono, ita kanta tanajin canji ajikinta koda tafiya takeyi, don yanda iska yake kadata ma tasan rashin qarfi ne ke dawainiya da ita.


Tun tana tunanin xataga mijinta, to xua yanxu tagama cire ran xaixo, ahalinda take ciki yanxu a rashin madafa ne, zata tattara takoma gidansa, kobanxa a gidansa xata samu gurin kwana batareda an tsangwameta ba.

Zuwairah ce tashigo itada yarta, yayar tata ce tafara magana, ke Zainabu yaukam ai anyi bakwai saiki tattara ki koma gidan mijinki, kin Riga kingama yin kashi a gurin xamanki, kedai Zainabu bantaba ganin asararriya diya marar Albarka irinki ba, kece sanadiyyar mutuwar mahaifinki, snn ga yarinya Wanda ba'ada tabbacin 'yar Habu ce, tunda kowa yashaida dalilin haihuwarta ce mijinki yaqara aure, yakuma cire hannunsa a gabadayan al'amarinki, tabbas qarshenki baxaiyi kyauba.


Zuwaira takarba tace kitashi ana miki magana kin tsaremu da shegen idanun naki irinna Uwarki, kinyi gadon wahala ae, yanda uwarki ta mulki ubanki kinyi tsammanin kema xaki samu wnn gurin a gidan Habu ne?
 
Yanxu gidannan nawane da 'yan uwana kijira randa nayi niyya a raba gado, inyaso idan aka Raba kyazo kixauna a fegin ki. Amma yanxu bakida gurin zama, koda kin zauna baxan ciyar dakeba, kisani ubanki bai bar komaiba sai wnn shegen dan qaramin shagon, Wanda yanxu Yahuza qanina ne xaifara zama aciki.

Banda mugun hali irinnaki ace ubanki yarasu a rasa mutum daya a dangin mijinki daxaixo gaisuwa daga mata har maza, gaxkiya mugun halinki yawuce a misalta.

Duk maganganun dasukeyi dinnan kanta na qasa bata daga ido takallesu ba balle su sa ran xata tanka musu, bahaka sukeso ba, sunso tayi magana, inyaso sutara mata jama'a suymata tofin alatsine.... A qarshe saima durqusawa tayi a gabansu tace. 

Kuyi haquri nasan kuma bakwajin dadin zama dani, Umma kisani Wanda ya hadamu zama tare baya raye kuma kobabu aure tsakaninki da Baba xaki iya share hawayena, insha Allahu xanyi nisa daku baxaku qara shaqan baqin ciki ko kadanne a dalilina ba.

Daga haka ta tattara kayanta Dana 'yarta tayi waje tabarsu a gurin sororo, taxo fitane dab da dakin Babanta kuka me qarfi dakuma tsuma zuciya ya kufce mata batare da ta shirya wa zuwanshi ba.

Nantake taji al'amari da dama yana dawo mata tamkar a wnn lokacin ake yinsu, tayi kuka me isarta snn tafita, tadanyi tafiya kapin tajuyo taiwa gidan Nasu bankwana tagama cire ran zata qara samun farinciki a wnn gidan, Babu uwa babu uba babu kuma *TUDUN DAFAWA* me adaidaita tasamu tabiyashi yakaita har qofar gidan ta, wani mummunar faduwar gaba ne yaziyarceta, batasan yanxu kuma wace rayuwar zasu cigaba dayiba, saidae kawai tasawa zuciyarta indai bai furta mata sakiba to har gaban Abada baxata daga qafarta tabar gidan ba.

Tanashiga bata kalli qofar dakin Lami ba, tayi shigewarta dakinta, saidae kuma abubda tagani yabata mamaki, buhunan abinci ne reras acikin dakin, dakuma sauran kayayyakin abinci.

Kenan nanne yaxama ma'adanin kayan abinci? Tamvayar zuciyarta tayi, ganin batada amsar tambayan yasata kame bakinta gumm.

Fitowarta alwalar sallan mangariba ne yayi daidai da shigowar Mal.Habu gida. Tsayuwa yayi yana kallonta yarasa me yakeji akanta, yanason yaga farin cikinta yana kuma jin al'amari mai girma akanta, amma kuma sashin data tsaneta acikin zuciyarta tafi qarfin rinjaye.

Sunjima suna kallon juna, yarame bana kadanba, inda anashi fannin kuma yake kallon takoma tamkar tsinken sakace saboda rama, tafiya yafarayi ahankali  kamar antuno mishi da wani Abu a take yaji yatsaneta a rayuwarsa bayason kallonta da duk wani abunda yashafeta.  
( karku manta aduk lokacinda yataka wnn layar da Lami ta bunne to komai kwance mishi yakeyi). Lami bata damu da sallah ba amma duk rintsi bata mantawa da yayyafa ruwa a inda layarta yake... Domin sharadinsa kar gurin yabushe.


Lami tana daki tajiyo sallamar Mal.Habu amma shiru  bataji yaqaraso ciki ba, maza tamiqe don ganin meya tsayayi, Tarar da Zainabu tayi a tsakar gidan nanfa ta tsuke mummunar fuskarta tanupe su, Bbn Sadiya meka tsayayi haka na ganka da wnn matar? ( kuji wai Bbn Sadiya😜) kame kame yafarayi itakuwa data fahimceshi nan tahau surfawa Zainabu bala'i ta inda takeshiga batanan take fitaba, itakuwa wucewa tayi tai alwalarta tanupi cikin daki....


Yakasance tun daga wnn ranar Zainabu tayi bankwana da ganin Mal.Habu saidai taji muryarsa watarana kuma taleqashi snn itace maiyin girkin takuma kaiwa Lami domin taraba, intai niyya tabata inbatai niyya ba kuma acemata tajira nagaba, bata taba tanka mataba, tasa aranta baxata bawa Lami damar ganin Lagonta ba...

Abun Mamaki shine Sam Allah yamantar da Lami cewar Zainabu pa tanada yarinya ita duk atunaninta yarinyar tamutu, tunda bata ganinta, ita yanxu damuwarta tasamu ciki...

Bayan shekaru biyu

Kapin xuwan wadannan shekarun abubuwa da dama sunfaru a wnn zaman babu abunda Zainabu ta tsinta na farinciki, saidai ixuwa yanxu tamasamun abinci balaipi takanyi dabara kapin tagama tadeba musu itada 'yarta suci adaki, Nana taqara girma har anyayeta, ta taso cikin gatan mahaifiyarta takuma yi sabo da zaman daki don Mamanta bata taba fita da ita, kullum tana daki abunta, har uwar tagama uzirinta tadawo ciki, dakinsu kuwa yagama zama dandalin ajiye abinci, inda hakan ya haddasa musu alaqa da beraye babu dare babu rana,.

A bangaren Lami kuwa tasamu yanda takeso kudi kan tana samu yanda ta tsara haka rayuwar take tafiya mata, tsakanin Mal.Habu dakowa nashi baqar magana snn Lami bata sararwa kowa amma kuma sunkasa jin haushinta saima qara qaunarta dasukeyi.

Abu daya yake damunta,shine yanda Sadiya take nema tamaida kanta Kariya, bin maza takeyi kamar Yaya, Sam bata zaman gida, dazaran uwar tafice itama zata fice su watse subar gidan.

Hakanne yasa Zainabu tafara sakewa a gidan itada NANA yarinyar tafara sabawa da wasan tsakar gidan. 

 Suna tsakar gida Nana tana kan cinyar mamanta itakuma tana mata kitso, yarinyar kyakykyawa ce ajin qarshe ga gashinta Harkan wuyanta tanada wushirya, fara ce amma rashin jin dadi yaboye hakan.


Lami ce da Ayashe sukai mantuwar kudin Zannuwan da xasu aika dashi gurin wata dillaliyar dasuke sayar mata da kaya don haka suka dawo dauka afujajan.

Babu sallama suka fado cikin gidan sukuma lokacin anfama yiwa Sadiya kitso mahaifiyarta tana tsokanarta itakuma tana tsalle tareda kyalkyala dariya,  Lami ce ta waro idanu waje tana nunawa Ayashe NAnan.............

Futowa ta.1⃣7⃣


Itama Ayashen a firgice take kallon inda ake nuna Matan, Wacece wnn din? Tambayarda taiwa Lami kenan 'yarta CE Lami tafada ahankali kamar meyin rada, hakan yayi dai dai da  juyowar Zainabu takalli bakin qofa, saida gabanta yafadi sanadiyyar ganinsu, amma tayi tamkar bataji komai ba, kuma bata nuna musu tafirgitan da tayiba, asalima yitayi kamar bata gansu ba, cigaba tayi dayiwa diyarta wasa sunata dariyarsu a qarshe kuma sukayi cikin daki...

Ayashe ce tayi qarfin halin Jan Lami cikin daki, xaunar da ita tayi don talura bata cikin hayyacinta ko kadan, Ke Lami ki kwantar da hankalinki indai kina tare dani kisa a ranki ba'ayi damuwarda xata xota gabanki baki kawardashi ba.


Kudi ake buqatan kinsan hakan, maza tattaro abunda kikedashi a dakinnan mutafi gurinda xa'ai mana maganin 'yan iska yanxunnan. Ni kincemin 'yar tamutu shiyasa bantaba kawo lamarinta a gaba na ba, Batun Ayashe ne yasata sakin ajiyar zuciya me qarfi cikin kuzari ta miqe ta tattaro kudadenta da Wanda take tatsa agurin Mal.Habu dakuma wanda take sata, don bai isa yacire wando adakin ba, saita yasheshi. Haka xaisamu babu komai a aljihunsa kuma babu damar yin magana.

......................................................
Tun bayan dawowarsu daga samo maganin da xasu sawa Nana ne, ta fara Jan Zainabu a jiki, Itakuwa  bataji aranta alkhairi ne hakan ba, shiyasa ko kadan takasa sakewa da ita, tsakaninta da ita gaisuwar nan dai dasuka saba. Itakuwa tarinqa janta da hira kenan

A halinda suke ciki yanxu Lami takan basu isashshen abinci, snn ta fara qowarin taga yarinyar tasaba da ita,yanda xata aiwatar da qudirinta batare da Zainabu tazargi komaiba,  amma Nana Sam taqi sabawa da ita, da zaran tanufota take fara ihu baxata taba bari tadauketa ba.

Lami abun yana matuqar qona mata rai, tarasa yanda xata bawa yarinyar maganin,  amma ayau tadauki aniyar saita cimma burinta, don tafijin haushin yarinyar fiyeda yanda takejin haushin uwar.

Alala ta aika almajiri yasayo mata, yaqara gyarashi, ne tanupi dakinsu, sallama tayi kamar mumina snn tace Zainabu kuna cikine? Eh  munanan, shigowa tayi taxauna gefen gadon tana yamutsa fiska, ranta namata xafi, bahaka taso ganin dakin ba, taso ace dakin yana nan kaca kaca ne, gwanin qazanta, amma ko ina Fes, duk da idan taxo deban kayan abinci saita xuxxubar a qasa, amma bataga alamar sunacikin qunci ba, itako mezatai wa Zainabu taji zuciyarta yamata sanyine? Bata qaunarta ko kadan.


Alala ne Ayashe ta aikowa Sadiya, itakuma batanan, idannace xan ajiye mata kuma kartazo taqici, shine nakawowa 'yarki niya sunanta ne? NANA sunanta tabata amsa, acikin ranta kuwa sunayen Allah taketa karantowa, Ayya Nana miqota taci Alalan, miqata tayi da zuciya daya, itakuwa Nana maqe kafadan ta tayi alamar baxata xoba.

Bayanda batayi ba amma NANA taqi xuwa a qarshe miqawa uwar Alalan tayi tace mata gashi kibata da kanki tasamu taci, karba tayi tafara qoqarin gyara Nana domin tabata, itakuma ganin uwar na dagata saitai tunanin miqata gurin Lami xatayi, don haka ta banqare jikinta tafara kuka harta bangaje kwanon Alalen yaxube gaba daya, gashi dakinnasu babu fulo balle akwashe taci.

Kan Uban can! Abunda Lami tafadi kenan cikin qaraji to wlh kisani kun zubar da abincin ranan ku kai harma dana dare, tunda har kina kallo 'yarki ta zubar zanyi maganinku gaba daya fuuuu tayi waje, Uhmmm murmushi Zainabu tayi snn ta tashi ta gyara gabadaya Inda ya baci... 

  Fitowarta tsakar gidan batasamu kowa ba, fita tayi tasamu yaro tabashi naira dari yasayo musu buredi domin suci itada 'yarta.


Itakuwa Lami gidan Ayashe tayi tasanar mata abunda yafaru, itakua Ayashe cemata tayi, ke madai Lami kincika zalunci dayawa tunda kinhana  uban kulasu kifita harkarsu mana, dukfa a qarqashinki suke, kedai kawai karki kuskura kibari ta karkato da hankalinshi gareta, kenipa tsoro take bani, dakewa kawai nakeyi amma dazaran naganta gabana kefadi.

Snn Alhaji nuhu yanata damuna akan maganar Sadiya, meyasa kika hanata kulashi bayan kinsan irin kudadenda yake Baku, humm ni wlh yanxu damuwata dayace nafison Sadiya tadaina bin maxannan tunda pa kinga zamaninnan cututtuka sunyi yawa ina tsoro wlh


Hahhahhahha lallai Lami amma kinsan nima ta dalilin Sadiya Alhajin nan yana sakemin kudi snn karo daya kicemin xaki rabasu, nikuma Yaya kikeso inyi, rasa abunda xata gayamata ne yasata yin shiru.

Kedai kiyarda dani babu abunda xaifaru da ita kawai kiturota gobe snn karki manta da maganar xuwa bikin 'yar goggon nawa kinsan jibi nepa, kitanada mana kudi isashshe.
Jiki a sanyaye Lami ta baro gidan.

Tana shigowa tayi dakin Sadiya itama kuma isowarta kenan taciro kudi acikin Jakarta tana irgawa, Sadiya wadannan kudaden pa? Cikin gautsin baki tace Bello ne yabani,  nashiga uku ni Lami, yanxu Sadiya baxaki daina bin maza ba, gaxkiya Umma kiyi haquri nipa yanxu badon kudi nake binsu ba, inabinsu ne saboda inji dadi.

😳Kiji dadi Sadiya nikike gayawa wnn maganar? Tashi tayi daga gurin uwar tayi waje tabarta a zaune baki bude, takuma kasa rufewa tsabar mamaki, gaxkiya yakamata innemo wa Sadiya taimako, da wnn tunanin tafito.

Itako Sadiya jininta ne yahadu da NANA har yarinyar kuma tasaba da ita, indai tafita yawon gantalinta to a hanyar dawowa saita sayo wa NANA tsaraba. Tun Zainabu tana dari~dari da hakan harta sake.

Lami tasanar da Sadiya maganar Ayashe, itakua cikin zumudi tafara shiryawa, Sadiya bakya tsoro? Lami ta tambayeta, tsoron me Umma? Wlh banajin tsoron komai kinsan yanda Alhajin nan yake kuwa hummm, Sadiya nikam inkinje karki amince kinji, lallai ma Umma ke akwae Wanda yataba hanaki yin abunda ranki yakeso da Bbn Nana ne? Wlh gaxkiya baxan boye mikiba banajin dadin abunda kikemin inkinga kinatsoron injamiki Abun kunya ne, to kiymin aure kawai, tanagama fadin hakan tai waje tayi tafiyarta.

Taje tasamu Alhaji yana jiranta don haka tana shiga basu bata lokaciba, suka dira akan gado, suka fara aiki ba kama hannun yaro, babu abunda kakeji sai nishinsu dakuma qaran gadon qarfen Ayashe.

Bayan tafiyar Alhajinne Ayashe tashigo tana washe baki, snn tacewa Sadiya, kinsan meye ne Sadiya? wlh kinada baiwa king a Alhajinnan? Gyada kanta tayi alamar ganewa, yacemin gaxkiya ya yaba da ni'imarki yakuma bawa uban dakinsa na cikin birni labarinki, don haka inason kibani hadin kai, nikuma zanshige miki gaba. Baqaramin kudi zamu samu daga gurinsu ba.

To ai Ummana baxata yarda ba, dariya tayi kapin tace karki damu nixan shawo kanta, dahaka takarbi 'yan kudaden hannun nata snn tace mata ta sanar da uwarta maganar tafiyar tasu da sassafe ne.
 itakuma ta tafi gida babu ko sisi sai uban tsamin jiki.

_WASHE GARI_

Dasassafe Lami tashirya Mal.Habu ta tayar snn tacemishi yawuce yatafi kasuwa, da sassafennan? Ehto yayi sassafe kam amma katashi mufita tare inyaso kaje yaukam kagaida su Baba dagacan kuma kawuce kasuwan, miqewa yayi yaje yadebi ruwa yayi Wanka, kapin fitowarshi a wankan itakuma ta debo ruwa cikin bokiti ta jiqa gurin layar ta, shikuma yafito suka kama hanya, duk abunnan dasukeyi akan idanun Zainabu, tajima tana mamakin meyasa Lami ke yawan jiqa wnn gurin? Tabbas abune me muhimmanci saboda ta lura da ita bata taba mantawa da jiqa gurin, Allah ya kyauta tafadi tamaida kanta cikin daki. 

Kapinnan sufita saida tashiga dakin Sadiya tarar tana baccinta harda minshari, daka mata duka tayi acinya itakuma afirgice ta miqe tana Sosa gurin, kisaurareni dakyau, karki kuskura kitapi gantalinki kimanta da jiqa gurin can, kinajina? Naji tafada da bacci a idanunta, itakuma tayi waje.

Rana akeyi katakau hakanne yasa basu zauna a tsakar gidaba , sunacikin dakinsu don basuda runfa, kasancewar gurin daman a mazaunin kitchen yake.

Sadiya kuwa tun bayan fitan ummanta bata jima cikin baccinba tafarka ta wanke fuskarta takuma wanke bakinta tadawo daki ta cancada kwalliya tawuce yawon gantalinta, bata tsaya ko inaba sai dakin Bello anan tayada zango, don ayanxu tagama gano dadin abun, kobai nemetaba tana iya kai kanta, wataran yaqiyi yi dakyar take lallabashi ya yarda, yau dinma hakance takasance.

Bayan isarta a qofar gida tasameshi, alama yayi mata data shiga ciki yanaxuwa, itakuwa taje tabaje akan gado tana jiransa, tashafe yafi tsawon awa guda snn yashigo fuskar nan atamke, can nesa da ita yatsaya snn yace waike Sadiya intambayeki bakya gajiya da abunnan ne? Ni wlh ina mamakin jaraba irintaki, Abu ba abinci ba amma kinkasa gundura dashi wace iriyar mace ceke? Babu Jan aji babu komai yafadi hakan yana tsareta da idanunsa na masu shaye shaye.

Kame kame tafarayi snn tace kayi haquri don Allah Bello nibama saikaban kudi ba nayarda wlh abunne dadi yakemin kuma baya isata, Tab gaxkiya duk Wanda ya aureki kasheshi xakiyi batareda lokacin mutuwarsa tayiba, itadai tananan tayi zugum tanajiransa yayi mata abunda taxo dominshi.

Haka yaxo yafara tumurmusan ta, shikanshi yanajin dadin Tarawa da Sadiya saboda tanada ni'ima amma matsalarsa daya ne da ita bata gajiya, tadinga cakumoka knn baxata gaji ba.


Bayan ya samu  nitsuwa ne, yakwanta a gefenta yahau baccin wahala, itakuwa jitayi kamar basuy komaiba kuma tana tsoron yimai magana karya tsigaleta yace xai daina ma gaba daya, a ina tasan zata samo wani. 


Bacci ne mai nauyi yadauketa, kamar a mafarki tafara tuno maganar Ummanta, afirgice  ta miqe har yamma yayi, gabanta ne yafadi agurguje ta farara sa kayanta, gyalenma a hannu tariqe shi aguje taiyo waje tanupi gida........

Fitowa ta.1⃣8⃣


Kamar Anjehota haka tafado cikin gidan gurin ajiyar mahaifiyar tata, amma abushe tasamu gurin, cikin gigita  ta antayo ruwa cikin bokiti ta kwaza agurin taqara taxuba sau uku tana zubawa, duka akan idon Zainabu, Sadiya na juyawa suka hada idanu, cikin borin kunya tafara magana.


Maman NANA ina qanwata take? Qanwarki tana bacci, murmushi tayi taqarasa gurinta kawo intayaki wanke wanken, Mama tace a'a Sadiya bari jeki huta abunki.

Tanaso ta tambayeta meyasa take zubawa gurincan ruwa kullum, saikuma xuciyarta ta kwabeta da cewar tafita daga abunda babu ruwanta, da wnn shawaran na zuciyarta takama bakinta tayi gum tareda bin Sadiya da idanuwa.

Itakuwa Sadiya tana Shiga daki ta sauke nannauyan ajiyar zuciya, tareda fadin Allah yasa kar Umma tagane,nan tacire kayanta tanupi waje domin yin wanka Duk da bawai wankan tsarki takeyi ba, itadai wankanta kawae takeyi na sabulu.


Sai bayan sallan isha ne Lami ta iso gidan, gurin ajiyarta tafara dubawa, ganinda tayi gurin a jiqe ne, yasata sakin murmushi taleqa dakin Sadiya ganinta tayi tana sharara baccinta sakin labuleb tayi tanupi nata dakin.

Bayan Mal.Habu yashigo cikin gidanne daidai gurin ajiyar Lami, jiyayi kamar yatuno dawani abunda yajima da mantawa amma yarasa gane menene, ganin zai takura kansa da tunani ne yasashi barin qoqarin tunawa.


Dakin Lami yashiga kamar kullum karban ledan hannunsa tayi batareda tambayarsa labarin kasuwa ba, dukda yanxu al'amura sungama kwabe masa, shagonsa ta koma baya bana wasa ba, amma kosau daya tun aurenshi da Lami bata taba tuntubarsa akan harkokin kasuwancin nasa ba, itadai yabata kudin kawai, Bashi yanason yai yawa akansa.


Saida tagama duba ledan snn tai murmushi tace Ashe baka manta da saqon kifinnawa ba, gaxkiya Idi duk fadin unguwarnan bawanda yakaishi iya   suya, shiko binta kawai yakeyi da kallo qarshe yace mata bari yaje ya watsa ruwa yadawo. To shknn nima bari intashi inmiqawa Sadiya nata.


Dawowanshi daga wanka ne kamar kullum yauma saida ta mulkeshi da wnn man nata me uban qarni, Sam wnn man bayajin dadinsa amma kuma ko xa'asa mishi wuqa a wuyansa baxai taba yunqurin fada mata ba.


A wnn Daren bacci baiyi gigin daukan Habu ba, saboda yawan tunane tunanen daya addabeshi, kamar Wanda aka tsikara da allura haka yatashi, kallon Lami yayi, yaga tabude qaton bakinta tana ta sharara minshari,  cikin sanda ya lallaba yafito waje baitsaya ko inaba sai qofar dakin Zainabu, tura qofan yayi a hankali, yatura kansa ciki, tanakan darduma ta idar da sallah tana addu'oi qaran kamar shigowan mutum ne yasata saurin lalubo tocilan ta, kamar daga Sama taga Habu, Rasa ta inda xaifara yayi, kawai Saiya durqusa a gabanta tareda kafeta da idanunsa masu kashe mata jiki aduk lokacinda tayi tozali dasu.

Mekake buqata, tayi tambaya cikin rawar murya, toshe mata baki yayi, snn yafara magana a hankali, Zainabu bansan meyake damuna ba, Sam banida tunani aduk lokacinda nace xanyi tunanin wani Abu atake nake tsintar kaina cikin mawuyacin hali, bansan Yaya zan misalta miki yanda nakeji ba, inama da inada iko, toda babu shakka xanbude miki zuciyata koxaki iyamini wani taimakona akai, rufe bakinta tayi da tafukan hannayenta sakamakon kukan da yake qoqarin kwace mata, miqewa yayi tareda karban tocilan hannunta, ya qarasa bakin gado haska NANA yayi yanata kallonta amma yakasa jin komai akanta, zama yayi tareda tallafe kansa, saboda wani azababban ciwo dayake barazanar raba mishi kai gida biyu.


Can kuma kamar walqiya yafita a dakin, hakanne yasa Zainabu kokonto kodai Mijinta yafara mata gizo ne, tabbas gixo ne don babu yanda ma xa'ayi yayi tunanin xuwa gurinta harya duba 'yarta.


Juyawa gurin NANA tayi tarungumeta tana kuka, fadi take aranta, 'yata kiyi haquri tamu kalar qaddaran kenan nataso bansan gatan kowa ba saina mahaifi, daga baya nasamu gatan miji, tun tafiyar batai nisa ba narasa kulawar mijina, snn narasa mahaifina rasawa ta har abada, yabar duniya bamuda *TUDUN DAFAWA* ina roqon Allah kobayan raina ya *DAFA* miki banson kiy rayuwar qunci irinnawa ke kadai kika ragemin da wadannan zancen zucin bacci yayi Awon gaba da ita.

     
   ~BAYAN SHEKARA GOMA SHA UKU~

Rayuwarsu haka tayita tafiya babu wani sauyi acikinta, Habu yacigaba da satar dare yana Shiga dakin Zainabu batareda Lami tasani ba, amma abunda yake bata mamaki shine baitaba yunqurin koda kama hannunta ba, Sadiya tasan shi a matsayin mahaifi amma babu shaquwa balle soyayya tsakaninsu, hakan sayo musu Abu ya turo musu ta _window_  da wadannan kudaden take samu Duke saida 'yan kayan miya take kuma biyawa NANA kudin makaranta, amma a tsakar gida tamkar baisan da zamansu ba, tun NANA tana bibiyarshi harta daina.

Nana tagirma yarinyar me kyau,kwarjini dakuma cikar haiba, ga ilimi, samari sunfara cicirindo akanta amma bata taba basu fuska ba, kyanta yanata qara fitowa batada shishshigi ko kadan, snn tana ajiye kanta inda Allah ya ajiyeta.

A Bangaren Lami kuwa duniya tagama saita mata, tagama samun yanda takeso a rayuwa kuma duk abunda Mal.Habu yakeyi tasani sarai don MAYE yagaya mata wnn ajiyar nata anbarshi ya bushe dole asirin xaiyi rawa, tasan komai amma  taxuba musu ido ne don tanada tabbacin babu abunda xasuyi saboda qullin da taimusu ba qarami bane, saidai haryanxu burinta naganin taraba Zainabu da gidan haryanxu baicika ba.


Sadiya kuwa likafar iskanci yagama Jan gaba, saboda a yawon iskancinta tahadu da qawayen banxa inda suka bude mata idanu Wanda xuwa wnn lokacin harcikin gari takexuwa, tagirma sosae sa'anninta duk sunyi aure harda 'ya'ya, gashi bata  ganin kowa da gashin ido,ciki kuwa harda uwarta, batajin maganar ta koda wasa, don inta matsa mata da magana topa watsewa sukeyi baram baram, harta gaji taxura mata idanu.



Da Safe Mama ce tafito hada kayan kumallo a qofar dakinta inda tahura wuta tanajiran dawowar NANA daga markaden qosan da xasuyi na karyawa, itakuma tadaura ruwan da xata Dana kunun tsamiya akan wuta.


Sadiya ce tafito a guje ko qarasowa bakin makwarari batayiba aqofar dakinta tafara kwarara amai ba qaqqautawa, idanun nan sunyiwo waje kai dagani kasan tanashan azaba, Mama ce taisaurin debo ruwa a buta, tanufo ta amma kapin ta qaraso Lami tayi saurin bangajeta Meye haka Zainabu, kindauka bansan abubuwanda kikeyi bane?


Kin asircemin yarinya batajin maganar kowa sainaki, kinshayar min da ita guba, tun farin rashin lpyrta nan dama nasan da hannuwanki a ciki, to wlh kifita a idona idan kika kuskura kika cigaba da shiga tsbgata wlh xanmiki abunda bakya tunani.

Sallamar Nana ce yahana Mama furta komai tayi gurinta tana tambayarta kindawo? Saboda tasan yanxu Nana tagirma takuma fara Ankara da irin zamanda sukeyi agidan, baxataso yarinyar ta taso da wadannan tunane tunanen ba don sungirmi shekarunta, ki ajiye qullin ki karbo kayan hadi gurin Ummanku kikawo.


Itakuwa Lami kama hannun Sadiya tayi suka nupi dakinta da ita, kwantar da ita tayi snn tafara tambayarta, Sadiya nipa ina lura da yanayinki a kwanakinnan meke damunki ne? Saida ta yamutsa fuska snn tace wlh Umma xazzabine saikuma kasala, Nashiga uku na doka lullubi ni Lami, yaushe rabonki da ganin Al'adarki? Yanxu wata na biyu kenan! Hannu tafara tafawa tana salati, kinga abunda naketa gujewa knn oh ni yanxu yaxanyi dake Sadiya? Kodayake ta inda aka hau tanan ake sauka, dare nayi idan qafa yafara daukewa a layinnan xamu nupi gidan Ayashe tasan yanda xatayi dake.
 

Fitowa tayi daga dakin snn tafara yabawa Mama baqaqen maganganu itakuwa kamar batasan da ita yakeyi ba, saida ta tabbatar takai aya snn tace mata kibamu kayan hadin qosan Rana tafarayi kuma ba'a fara suya ba, Baza'a bayarba tafada cikin hargagi wlh yau sakamakon baqantamin rai dakukayi saidai kuwuni da yunwa atoh, tawuce dakinta ta barsu baki bude.

Tana shigewa ciki Nana takalli magaifiyarta da hawayenda suka fara cika mata idanu tace, Mama Ke meyasa Baba baya baki kayan hadin ki ajiye,  kuma adakin Umma babu kayan abinci? A gidansu hauwa'u ba suma Matan Babansu biyune amma kowa tanayin girki, meyasa Umma batayi?


Kai Nana kincika tambaya yanxu wanne kikeso in amsa miki? Banason kinasa damuwa a ranki, komai kikaga yafaru tofa yanada sila kuma xaiwuce babu abunda yazo domin zama, dadi ko baqinciki xai wuce kinji, kisaki ranki 'yata kinji? Murmushi tayi...
 
   Kishiga daki. A qasan filo akwai naira goma kisayo mana gishiri mumaida qullin na Alale, kapin kidawo xan wanke mana gwangwani kinji? Umma babu manja da yaji pa, karki damu bakullum akecin abinci don dadi ba, kedai kiysauri kidawo kisamu kici kitapi makaranta ,yanxu xakiga Hauwa'u ta turo.


*DA DADDARE*
Bayan Lami ta tisa qeyar Sadiya suka nupi gidan Ayashe shikuma Mal.Habu dawowarshi yasamu babu Lami hakan yasa cikin sauri yanupi dakin Zainabu, yaci sa'a itakadai ce, don Lami tasaka Nana gyaran dakin Sadiya inda tabata shida Amai.

Cikin sassarfa yaqarasa gurinta kamar wani mayunwacin zaki, cikin mamaki takedubanshi da irin sauyinda yaukuma aka samu, bata bata mishi lokaci ba, don idan tace batai kewar mijintaba tayi qarya shekara nawa?  
         (Zainabu tsohon tsumi ya motsa😜)

Nan suka shige jikin juna batareda fargaban komai ba, cikin shauqi suka raba junansu da kayan jikinsu, suna gab da isarda saqonne pa, Mal.Habu yamiqe a firgice sakamakon............

Fitowa ta.1⃣9⃣

Miqewa yayi a firgice sakamakon jin Abunashi kwata kwata yakoma yakwanta luguf kamar lagwani, itama Zainabun miqewa tayi tana tambayarsa lpy? Kasa amsamata tambayar yayi snn yafara qara qoqarin ganin ya cimma burinsa.

 Haka yaita fama gurin sau biyar, xata tashi tsab amma dazaran yayi yunqurin shigar da ita zatakoma takwanta, haryakai Zainabu tafahimci halinda ake ciki, kifewa a gurin tayi tafashe da kuka maiban tausayi, inda shikuma yadafe kansa cikin takaici dakuma al'ajabin wnn sabon al'amarin da yakuma dunfaro shi, to Kodai banida lpy ne? Tambayarda yayi wa kansa kenan amma kumapa jiyama yakwanta da Lami lafiya lau, pa.


To menene manufar hakan? Abubuwa sunmishi yawa a kwakwalwarsa yarasa dawanne xaifara, juyawa yayi da niyyar lallashin Mama amma alamun ana taba qofar gida da niyyar shigowane ya hanashi, cikin sauri yakintsa a hargitse ya fita haryana tuntube, yayi dakin Lami itakuma Nana lokacin xatayi dakin Mamanta ganin mahaifinnata cikin wnn halin yasata saurin shiga dakinnasu.

Mama daganinta taisaurin gyarawa snn tafara qoqarin qaqalo murmushi Wanda kana gani kasan iya fatar bakinta ya tsaya, Mama menene, meye Baba yayi miki? Babu abunda Yayi mini Nana kaina kemin ciwo kuma yakawo min magani ne nasha, to kuma naga yafita haryana tuntube yashiga dakin Umma?

Hoo ni Nana ban hanaki shiga abunda babu ruwanki ba? Kiyi haquri Mama baxan sake ba, shikenan maxa yiki kwanta karki makara sallan Asuba, kwanciya Nana tayi, itama kwanciya tayi amma bataga alamar bacci xai iya kusantota da garaje ba, hakan yasata miqewa tanupi bayi wanka tadauro alwala tadawo ciki.


Itakuma Lami  can gidan Ayashe kwashe mata halinda Sadiya keciki gaba daya tayi snn taqara da cewa kinga pa Ayashe abunda naketa gujewa knn, niyanxu babu abunda nafi qauna kamar Sadiya tayi aure, harga Allah nagaji da zamanta haka kiduba pa kiga sa'anninta duk sunyi aure.

Ayashe kuwa acikin ranta cewa take ai aure kam bayanxu ba, don nalura ajikinta xanpi samu fiye dake, abunda Lami bata saniba Ayashe CE taiwa Sadiya karfa da auren snn kuma akasa mata jaraban maxa,

A fili kuwa cewa tayi Lami yanxu dai dukba wnn ba, kibari Alhaji inyazo insanar dashi gobe dasafe nasan xaisan yanda xa'ayi da cikin, Yo wani Alhaji kuma Ayashe?

Nida nakeson muxubar dashi, yabi rariya batareda kowa yaji me ake ciki ba, a'a karki sanar dashi muje dai chemist da ita kawai mubiya, ayita taqare a darennan, tunma kapin yacigaba da girma,yanxu ma bansan watannin sa ba, inagadai yayi wata biyu.

A'ah yanxu kam dare yayi sosae snn idan muka tafi da ita, aimunso tonon asirin kenan, kibari gobe dasafe xan biyo miki muje mu biyu mu amso maganin tasha agida kawae kinga bawanda yasan anyi koya kikagani? Eh hakanma yayi duban Sadiya tayi wacce tun isowarsu ta bingire a gurin taketa sharara baccinta, daka mata mari tayi tareda Daka mata uban tsawa tamiqe suka kama hanyar gida.

_WASHE GARI_

kamar yanda suka tsara haka Ayashe taxo, iso taimata har cikin daki, snn tamiqe xuwa gurin Mama karbo mata abun karyawa,  tana fita Ayashe tacigaba da bin dakin da kallo kamar kullum tareda raya abubuwa da dama aranta, kwafa tayi a qarshe  snn tagyara xamanta.

Shigowa Lami tayi bayan ta'ajiyemata takuma hada mata shayi snn tace mata bari taje tayi wanka, bayan fitarta nanma Ayashe miqewa tayi taduba cikin jakar Lami Wanda takeda tabbacin acikinsa take ajiya, don tana lura da ita kullum acikinsa take daukan kudi, 😳😳zaro tayi sabodan yawan kudadenda tagani a cikin jakar, lallai Lami shine idanna tambayeta kudi taita karantomin babu? Xanyi maganinki, saurin komawa mazauninta tayi. Saboda tajiyo qarar tahowan Lami, don ita tafiyarta baya buya yanda kasan doki haka take😜

Dakyar Ayashe ta daidaita nutsuwarta sabada ita kadai tasan yanda xuciyarta kemata suya, dakuma abunda take qudirtawa aranta, Lami tagama shirinta tsaf. Snn tabawa Ayashe baya tadauko jakar a fakaice kamar babu abun arziki a ciki, zaro kudi tayi kadan aciki snn tafara cewa, wlh Ayashe yanxu Sam babu ciniki a kasuwar kudi sunfara yankemin, Allah sarki kawai ta iya fadi tareda tabe bakinta. Tagama shirinta suka fito domin tafiya, Mama ko tana dakinta Nana ce a tsakar gidan taketa kaikawo a dakin  Sadiya, tasakata taimata fate, gashi kuma ta malala Amai a tsakar dakin, Nana tanashan wahalar jinyar Sadiya, duk da haka tana mugun tausayinta don koba komai Sadiya tana qaunarta, sau da dama takan bita idan xata tafi makaranta tabita waje domin tadan bata na kashewa, itako bata saba kashe kashen kudiba haka take dawo dashi gida tabawa Mmn ta.


Ganinsu yasata ruaunawa tace Umma Ayashe ina kwana, bankwana ba don gidanku shegiyar yarinya wacce tai gadon kissa, kyaleta Ayashe mutafi abunda ke gabanmu yawuce mutsaya muna kula wnn me idanun magen, 

Saida suka fara tafiya snn Lami tace Ayashe nipa da akwai yanda xa'ayi ma zanso amaida cikin nan jikin wnn yarinyar Nana, wlh tatsayamin a maqogoro yanda kikasan qashin kifi, wlh nafi tsanarta akan uwar, duk abunda nashirya akanta bayaci, kinaga badon Maye ya ankarar dani dawuri bafa da aikin gama yagama xuwa yanxu.

Ayashe bata iya cemata komai ba don xuciyarta acike yake da haushinsu gaba daya......

Mai shagon yagama yin sabo da Ayashe don takan kawo mishi _customers_ sosae don haka yana ganinta yasan tasamu, gaisawa sukayi snn sukai mishi bayani, tambayarsu yayi watannin cikin, bayan sun sanar mishi ne yabasu magunguna Lami tafito da kudi tabayar, miqo mata canji yayi rige rigen karba sukayi da Ayashe amma Lami ce tayi Nasarar karbewa abunda yaqara cin ran Ayashe knn takuma qara tamke fuska tareda qara jaddada qudirin zuciyarta....


Yanda sukaga Rana haka sukaga dare a bayan Sadiya tayi amfani da magungunan da suka kawo mata, ciwo harda fadowa a gado, har Mal.Habu a dakin Sadiya ya kwana, yanason ya tambayi cuwon menene amma yana tsoron Lami da amsar da xata bashi, suna tsaye cirko cirko, ihunta ne yatado Mama da Nana suka nufo dakin a guje, amma suna shiga Lami tafara jefansu da baqaqen maganganu tareda cewa duk itace silar ciwon 'yarta kuma a sannu xaikoma kanta.


Itakuwa Mama ganin cin xarafin yafara yawa ne yasata Jan Hannun 'yarta sukabar musu dakin sum sum da Harara tabisu, shikuma yatsaya yanda kasan hoto saibinsu da idanu kawai yakeyi.

Sai gab da Asuba snn Sadiya tasamu bacci me nauyi ya dauketa, ananne suma suka nupi dakinsu Mal.habu kuwa ganin asuba ya qarato shiyasa baikoma bacci ba, itakuwa Lami kan katifa tafada, acikin abunda baifi minti biyiba sai tashin minsharinta takeji, kamar wata kwado.



*BAYAN KWANA ASHIRIN DA BIYU*

Sadiya ta warke tsab takoma harkokinta harma yafi nada, tunda yanxu Alhaji yana hadata da ubannin dakinsa, kudi kam sai dai tabawa Ayashe don batada buqatarsu itadai taji dadinta😋..

Ayashe ce tazo gidan Lami saboda Alhajin yayi mata maganar wani uban gidansa, kuma kwana yakeson yayi da Sadiya, yabata maqudan kudade akan indai takawo mishi ita toxai ninka mata ninki baninkin wnn kudin,  ai jiki na kyarma tazari gyale tayi gidan Lami ba shiri...


Lami tasamu tana tsakar gida, filet din dafaffen kwai ne aganta tanaci hankali a kwance, ta mimmiqe qafafu Nana tana daddana mata, harwani lumshe idanu takeyi.

Maraba da Ayashe tafada tana fadada fara'arta, kina hutawa qawata, kekuma tashi kibamu gurin munafuka Ayashe tafadawa Nana cikin kidima tayi dakinsu.

Ayashe kuwa cikin iya tsara magana tasanar da Lami abunda yakawota tareda kitsa mata alheran da xasu samu, cikin tsuke fuska Lami tace gaxkiya Ayashe baxamu shirya dakeba matuqar kina kawomin irin wadannan maganganun yaushe ma sadiyar ta warke? Kuma shknn takoma kamar shaddar kasuwa?

To baxiyiwu ba, inma kudinsa kika karba kimayar mishi ni 'yata aure nakeso tayi yanxukam nima ina burin ganin zuri'ata, keda yakamata kitaimakeni mu cetota cikin halinda take ciki kuma, kece ke qara ingixa abin?
.
Sororo Ayashe tayi amma tayi qoqarin danne xuciyarta snn tahau bawa Lami haquri, dayake abokan cin mushe ne a take suka shirya amma Ayashe  a qarshe xaman gagaranta yayi sukai sallama tafita.
 
Isarta gida a tsakar gida tayi wurgi da gyalenta cikin fushi tafara magana lallai Lami kintaboni yau tunda kika hanani samun nawa tokema kina gab  da rasa duk wani jindadinki, dani kikeyi tafigi gyalen ta taiwaje kamar meshirin tashi sama.......

Fitowa ta.2⃣0⃣


Harta fito waje ta tuno da kudadenta dawowa tayi dasauri tadauka snn tanupi waje, adaidaita ta tara wani qauye taimishi kwatance can bayan gari baxaka taba tunanin akwai wani halittan da xai iya zama acikin wnn kungurmin dajin ba,

Tsaidashi tayi snn taciro dubu uku tamiqa mishi takuma jaddada masa akan yajirata xata fito sutafi yanxunnan.


Kutsawa take tayi yayinda qayoyi keta sokinta amma tsabar tijara batamaji burinta daya ta qarasa inda takeso don yanda takejin ranta xata iya komai akan qudirinta ciki kuwa harda yawo tsirara.

    ( *SUBHANALLAH*) 

Dakyar ta isa hango layi tayi kamar anacin kasuwa hakan baisa taji takaraya ba domin tasan tana isa xata samu shiga.

Hakanne kuwa yakasance domin tana isa naji wata murya data raxanani harsaida wayar tafadi aqasa, sai fadil ne yamiqo mini ita😜

Murya ce me matuqar firgitarwa qara neda muryar cikin kururuwa akace da Ayashe kumatsa gaba daya tashigo, haka aka daddare a layin ita kuwa cikin kwanciyar hankali tanupi ciki.


Agabanshi ta durqusa tayi sujjada snn tamiqe tahau xuba kirari harsanda yadaga mata hannu alamar dakatarwa, dafa kanta yayi na 'yan daqiqu snn yace mata kije dakinki a qarqashin filo xakisamu magani shixaki bata tayi amfani dashi  xaku kori Zainabu a gidan amma bayanxu xaki bata ba, sai kudinda take gadara dashi yafara qarewa.

 Snn xuwa yanxu nagama bata duk aikinda Maye xaiyi mata a gaba, duk 
maganinda xaibata na banxa ne, baxai amfaneta da komai ba, kuma xai cigaba da moreta tana karban kudadenta.

Shaaradin aikinki shine daga yau xaki farayin sallah batareda alwala ba, 

   (Aigwara kahanata sallar gaba daya, 😏 sallar  da babu alwala gara bakayishi ba ae.)


Bayan kinbata maganin xakixo Aljani xai kwana dake na tsawon kwana biyar, bayan kinkoma Habu xaikawo kansa gurinki.

Cikin jin dadi tafara mishi godiya tareda miqewa taciro gaba kudin da yake jikinta gaba daya taxuba a wani kwarya, Aikinki yana kyau Ayashe duk mutanen da kike 2rowa suna xuwa Aljani yace kiqara qoqari, washe baki tayi snn tanupi waje domin ba'ayin magana bayan yayi saidai kapin yayi, don haka tun kapin tasaba umarnin tai waje tabarshi yana surkullensa.


Tundaga wnn ranar tadauke qafarta a gidan Lami tafita harkanta kwata kwata takuma kwantar da hankalinta saboda lokaci kawai take jira yayi takafa nata gwamnatin.

A gida kuwa Lami abunna Sadiya yafara damunta don a 'yan kwanakin nan tama fara kwanan wani guri ba'a gidaba,  snn gashi Mal.Habu yacigaba da satar hanyan xuwa dakin Zainabu duk da Maye yana tabbatar mata cewa babu abunda xasu iya aikatawa koyaya xasuyi amma duk da haka hankalinta baya kwanciya.

Kamar kullum yauma cikin dare ya sidade yatafi dakin Zainabu don tun jinyar Sadiya Nana tafara tayata kwana to haryanxu acan take kwana shiyasa kanshi tsaye yake nufarta.

Duk da yanxu tafara Nuna mishi su haqura sudage da addu'a babu abunda yafi qarfin Allah,amma yaqi, Bacci yasameta tanayi cikin nitsuwa kan gadon ya haye ya kwanta ta bayanta, shafa jikinta yafarayi, bude ido tayi, batayi mamaki ba don tasan xa'a Rina hakan...

Bbn Nana mexai hana baxakabi shawarata bane, munemi Agajin mahaliccinmu shifa masanin komai ne, mudage da addu'oi insha Allahu xa'a dace bai kulata ba domin wani jaraba datake ciciyarsa, don haka yafara aiwatar da abunda yakawoshi cikin tsoron abunda xai biyo baya.

Gashinan dai yaukam bata kwanta ba tananan sambal da ita, cikin farinciki yafara qoqarin shigar da ita jikinta amma me? Saiyaji kamar ruwa yana gangaro masa, gabansa ne yafadi ruwan kuma tanayimishi kamar qaiqayi tareda mutstsinin cikin jikinsa bashiri yamiqe itama miqewa tayi a raxane tareda tambayarsa menene kuma? Ina Haske yafada cikin raunananniyar murya, lalubo tocilan ta tayi ta haska gani yayi wani ruwa me yauqi hade kuma dawasu qananun tsutsotsi sunata yawa a cikin ruwan abun babu kyan gani.

Saurin runtse idonta tayi, inda shikuma yakasa komai sakamakon yanda zuciyarsa dakuma kwakwalwarsa ta tafi hutun wucin gadi, Candai yadawo hayyacinsa , haquri yafara bawa zainabu tayi saurin toshe mishi baki da tafin hannunta.


Bbn Nana kadage da addu'a lamarinmu yana buqatar hana idanuwan mu bacci mu raba dare muna roqonshi yakawo mana sauqi cikin al'amarin mu.

Jiki a sanyaye yace mata Magode yasakai yafice, itakuma kasa kuka tayi, yau kam, idanunta bushewa sukayi, tashi tayi ta kimtsa jikinta, tareda qudirtawa a xuciyarta xata daina yarda dashi matsawar bazai tsaya kan ibadarsa ba, don tagaji tanakuma cutuwa, tunda xa'afara abu rimi rimi amma daxarar ankai matakin zama Abu daya labarin xai sauya, saikuma yabarta da matsan cinya da ciwon Mara, tunda shi yanada inda  xai sauqe nauyinsa.


Shikuma shigarshi Lami tana kallonsa damuwa fal a fuskarsa, hakan yasata jin dadi harta kasa danne farincikin ta, tafashe da dariya me qarfi, juyowa yayi ya kalleta itakuma tamiqe ta qaraso gareta da uban tsamin jikinta, lavarin ne yasha banban hakan yasani barmusu dakin.

Haka rayuwar tacigaba da tafiya inda Lami tafara ganin sauyi a gurin Habu don har wasa da tsokanar Nana yakeyi duk da baya kula Zainabu ko kadan, taje gurin Maye yabata magunguna tabasu sunci sunsha amma duk a banxa, don haka tayanke shawarar xuwa gurin Ayashe...

Samun Ayashe tai kamar kullum gidanta kamar dandalin karuwai kowa na abunda yakeso, wasuna shan taba, wasu na Caca, wasu xaune anata hira, itakuma uwar gayyar tana gefe tana busa sigarinta.


Daganinta tafadada murmushinta  ta tarbi qawartata da farinciki suka gaisa tareda wasan barkwanci kamar yanda suka saba, can Lami ta nisa taiwa Ayashe bayanin abunda ke tafe da ita.


Wanin wawan dadi ne ya ziyarci xuciyar Ayashe  aranta tace Anzo gurin amma ta danne tareda saka fuskar damuwa a fiskarta, tai shiru kamar me tunani karki damu indai kina tare dani kije gida Gobe zanzo ina Layar kwankin bayan nan? Eh yananan ai kinsan ba'asamu mahadinsa bane.

 To ki kwantar da hankalinki ki koma gida gobe xantaho miki da maganin damuwarki, xan samo miki mahadinsa ayita taqare kawai yanxukam, a yasar da kwallon mangwaro ahuta da quda.

To nawa xan bayar na karbo maganin? Ah habadai karki damu aini Lami kinfi qarfin haka a gurina kece bakidau keni da muhimmanci ba, akwai isashshen kudi xankarbo miki karki damu, bahaka bane Ayashe wlh kudinne yanxu sun yankemin, wlh ingaya miki Mal.habu yanxu baya dawowa da kudi gida, danai mishi magana wlh abun tsoro kai tsaye yacemin Babu kudinne.

Tafadi hakan tareda tagumi don tana cikin tashin hankali matuqar tayi sake asirinta yagama tonuwa, Nace miki ki kwantar da hankalinki kekam me gabadaya xa'ayi kowa yahuta.


Lami Kuwa dadi taji ya mamaye ranta, taiwa Ayashe bankwana tareda fatan ganinta washegari kamar yanda Maganin........

Fitarta keda wuya Ayashe takwashe da kafurin dariya me rage imani, saida tayi me isarta snn tayi shiru tace Lami dani kikeyi yanxu ne xakisan kintabo Ayashe hahhahahahhahaha kindauka ke kadai ce xaki dauwama a cikin dadin?

Har xakiymin wulaqanci akan kudi, kicimin mutunci akan 'yarki? To muxuba mugani, 'yartakin ma kinja mata baxatai auren ba kuma xata dauwama a iskanci, snn kekuma xan kwace Habun daranki saikin zama 'yar kallo kintaro ruwa  bakida Leman karewa takece da dariya snn tacigaba da xuqar sigarinta..


*WASHE GARI*

Shiryawa tayi tsab tanupi gidan Lami da qullin Maganin.

WNN SHINE ABUNDA YAFARU A FARKON LAVARIN IDAN XAKU TUNA MUJE XUWA. (Ayashe takawo magani itakuma Lami ta aika kiran. Mal.Habu akasuwa).

Fitowa ta.2⃣1⃣


Sai da ta isa gab da layinda xai sadata da shagon Baban natan snn tai qoqarin saita nitsuwarta, taqarasa cikin shagon.

Yaron shagon nasane tasamu yanata hada hadar sayarda kayan,  gaisheshi tayi snn tace mishi tana Neman Bbn tane, to kijirani bari nai mishi magana yanacan cikine.

Nana lpy daiko? Taji muryan Bbn ta, murmushi tai mishi kapinnan tabashi amsa da cewa Umma ce take inkiraka, saida gabansa ya yanke ya fadi, dakyar ya iya cemata kije ina xuwa....

Komawa cikin shagon yayi yaxauna, baida alamar tashi, haka kawai yaji yaukuma baxai iya bin umarninta ba, wnn shine karo na barkatai tana yimishi irin hakan, wataran ma shine yafito Amma haka xata danno mishi kira, shikuma yakoma gida, kuma bb abunda xata mishi.


Haka yacigaba da zamansa batareda yaje garetan kamar yanda tabuqatan ba..
Haka kawai kuma yaji buqatar matarshi yana qara qaruwa acikin ransa, duk da yana tsoron tinkararshi bashida tabbacin yanda xata karbi abun, snn bugu da qari baisan mexai faru ba kuma..


Itakuwa Lami a gida tacika tagama yin fam axuciyarta kuwa tsoro ne yake qara kamata, tana Allah Allah dare yayi tasamu tasamishi maganin a ruwan wankan.

*DA DADDARE*

Bayan dawowan Mal.Habu daga qofar gida bai kobi takan dakin Lami ba, dakin zainabu yawuce kai tsyaye ledan hannunsa ya ajiye cikin Mamaki takebinshi da kallo fa ido taiwa Nana magana, a take tamiqe tabar dakin.

Menene wnn Bbn Nana? Nakune keda Nana yabata amsa a taqaice, takasa boye mamakinta hannu tasa ta matso da ledan gabanta tareda budewa, Nama tagani dakuma kifi soyayye hade da lemun gwangwani, bai saurareta ba yai fitarshi.

Miqewa tayi takira Nana, miqa mata tayi tace mata Nana nah zauna kici kiqoshi, duk da yawunta ya tsinke babban burinta tajishi acikin bakinta, hakan bai hanata tambayar mahaifiyarta ba.

Mama yau kuma Baba ne yashigo gurinmu harda tsaraba irinna Umma? Dariya tayi snn tace mata Nana knn aina gayamiki komai idan kikai haquri yanada qarshe, kullum ina tunatar dake muhimmancin haquri, kiriqeshi xakici riba, murmushin jin dadi ta sakarwa mahaifiyarta snn tafara cin naman cikin jin dadin sauyin rayuwar da suka samu.


Dare ya tsala, gari yayi duhu, guri yayi tsit, babu abunda kakeji sai kukan tsuntsaye, saukowa yayi a gadon kamar kullum cikin hikima yafito daga dakin, Lami kuwa a ankare take don haka yana fita taiyo waje cikin Sauri, maganinta tadauka takuma lalubo boyayyiyar layarta..


Cikin hanxarinta ta fito waje, qofar dakin Zainabu taje, cikin sanda inda ta tsuguna ta toni guri kadan ta bunne layar snn taxuba ruwa a gurin, ruwa takoma da deba bokiti, ta raba maganin kashi biyu, ta xuba Rabin acikin Nata, kai tsaye tashige bayi ta watso ruwan, wani qaiqayi takeji a jikinta ta ko ina tareda wani wari marar dadin ji, a haka taketa daurewa, itadai indai burinta xai biya tofa xata juri abunda yafi warima.


Cikin Hikima Mal.Habu ya lallashi Mama itako Sam bata iya yimishi musu, Don haka baisha wuyan shawo kantaba, yafara sarrafa duk wani sassa na jikinta cikin xaquwa da rawan jiki, abunda ya bashi mamaki bai wuce rashin samun matsalar da akayi yau ba, Don kuwa yau yaji abunda baitaba jiba, gaxkiya yayi kewar matarshi, inda itama a fanninta take farincikin hakan, yau taji mijinta ajikinta abunda tai rashinshi tsawon shekaru masu dinbin yawa.


Sunshare Awanni suna Abu daya batare da qosawa ko gajiyawa da juna ba, wnn Daren yashiga cikin darenda yakasance me tsayawa a rayukansu,  jinta yayi ayau kamar sabuwar budurwa  baxai taba cewa ta haihu ba ayanda yajita,  (Yo ba doleba shekara nawa ba service😜, Donna ba ai gyara ba tun haihuwa)


Dokinta yacigaba daji, xai barta ne badon gajiyaba sai don tausaya mata. Yau ne yatuno da wani Abu waishi nitsuwa a kwanciyar aure.

 saidaga baya suka samu nitsuwa, shine yafara magana cikin not tsuwa, tareda mannata da jikinsa  yafara ce mata, kiy haquri da duk abubuwanda suka faru a shekarun nan Zainabu idan xance miki baxan iya tuna komai ba wlh kiyarda dani, banida tunani nawa nikadai, bansan menake ciki ba inabuqatar addu'arki ina muradin mudawo cikin rayuwarmu na da..

Itako tsabar dadine yamantar da ita komai, jitakeyi a ranta kamar yau tafara jin dadin kasancewa dashi, itako mexai hanata karban uxirinsa, dama can tasan balaipinsa bane, kodama laipinsane tasan dole xasu sulhunta junansu tunda a iya saninta bata bata masa ba. Ashe xataga wnn ranar a 'yan kwanakin nan? Haka yayita gaya mata maganganu masu tsayawa a rai itakuwa binsa kawai takeyi da murmushi dakuma godiya, har sukakai matsayinda kowa ya gamsu da batuttukan dan Uwansa.

Bai gusheba yaci gaba da bata labarai Amma me? gani gani yayi tajima da bacci duk a tunaninsa baccin gajiya ne, yajima a haka yana kallonta, sai can yamiqe da niyyar fita amma jiyayi baigaji da kallonta ba, shafa fiskarta yayi yace baxan taba mantawa da karamcinki a gareni ba, tabbas ke 'yar halak ce, Allah yaqara miki haquri, yakuma fiddani acikin qangin da nake ciki, da hakan yasamu yafita yana waiwayenta.

Lami a dakinta tayi mamakin jimawarsa na yau, kasa haquri tayi tafito tsakar gidan, jitakeyi kamar tashiga dakin taga mesukeyi kokuma tashiga tarufesu da duka koxataji sauqin yanda xuciyarta ke tsalle kamar xai fado qasa, saida tahango alamar  fitowarsa snn tashige dakinta da gudu tai likimo akan gadon kamar me baccin gaxke.

Yana dawowa kuwa ta turmusheshi kamar kullum duk da nayau buqatar ta dabance, burinta sugama yayi wanka a Daren, shikanshi yayi mamakin yanda ta gyaleshi da wuri haka, duk da yaji dadin hakan don kuwa a matuqar gajiye yake da ita.

Ba'anan mamakin nasa ya tsayaba, ganinda yayi dakanta, tafita tahada mishi ruwan wanka,  harda cemishi rayi muje intaimaka maka kayi wankan, baki bude yake kallonta cikin duniyar mamaki, tareda cemata a'a barshi kawai yamiqe yafita yayi bayangidan.


Fadi tayi a gadon cikin farinciki, yaukam xan huta da damuwarki Zainabu saura kuma 'yarki, fita tayi dasauri ta leqa dakin Zainabu ta tabbatar tanakan baccin dadi ne yaqara cikata tadawo dakinta taxauna dirshan tana jiran sakamako.


Shikuwa Mal.Habu a bayi yana watsa ruwan ajikinshi yaji kamar anjuya mishi kwakwalwarsa gaba daya, kamar mutum mutumi haka yakejin kansa,  tamkar anxare komai axuciyarsa yakeji saikuma wani baqon al'amari dayake shigarsa Wanda baida tabbacin daga inda yake.

Jiyayi gaba daya ankwashe tunaninsa na baya anshigar mishi da sababbi, a wnn yanayin yake harya kammala wankan yafito.

Baiyi yunqurin shiga kowani dakiba, a qofar dakin Sadiya yasamu wani dutse, akanshi yaxauna tareda dafe kansa.. Lami ce taleqo bahaka taso ba, batason abunda xaifaru, yafaru akan idonsa, don batason yashiga wani halin baqinciki ko kadan don tanason mijinta.

A bangaren Mama kuwa Gab da asuba ne baccin nata yacanxa akalarsa xuwa  marar dadi, inda takeyin wani mafarki mai ban tsoro, wani halitta take gani razanar da ita yake kuma sanarda ita abunda xatayi daga zarar tafarka, cikin murya me firgitarwa a qarshe yabuga mata wani qarfe a tsakar kanta, farkawa tayi amma cikin dakin gaba daya juya mata yakeyi snn gashi sai hankada ta akeyi waje...

Tana tako qofar dakin ta taji wani qara acikin kanta, batasan lokacinda tafita a gidan da gudu ba.............

Fitowa tata. 2⃣2⃣

Tagabanshi ta wuce amma Sam bai luraba baikuma ganta ba, saboda duniyar da kwakwalwarsa ta Lula duniyace me matuqar nisa, Wanda yataba xiyartar ta shixai gane halinda yashiga.

Ganin shirunne yasa Lami fitowa domin ganin ko lpy, duk da yanda gabanta yake tsananta faduwa Wanda tarasa dalilin hakan, cikin rashin kwarin guiwa ta tinkareshi magana tafarayi amma ko kadan hankalinsa baya gurin balle yasan taxo.

Ganinda tayi baya cikin hayyacinsa ne, yasata taboshi firgigit ya miqe kamar Wanda aka tsikarawa allura, Lpy dai Bbn Sadiya? 

Runtse idanunsa yayi sakamakon wani dafi daya soki xuciyarsa a dalilin tabashin da tayi, lpy qalau yabata amsa batare daya kalli inda take tsaye ba yayi wucewarsa masallaci domin yaji amfara kiraye kirayen sallan Asuba.

Sororo ta tsaya agurin kamar an dasata sai bin qofar gidan tayi da idanu, meye hakan tafada bahaka tayi tsammani ba, don a yanda maganin yake indai tayi yanda akace to xai manta da Zainabu har abada baxai kuma tambayarta ba, xai koma qarqashin ikonta sai yanda takeso xai nayi, to Yaya haka kuma acikin qanqanin lokaci kodai abun baigama shiga jikinsa bane, da wnn tunanin tasaki murmushi.

Tunawa datayi da lbrn Zainabu ne yasata nufar cikin dakinnata, wayam ta taras bakowa hakan yasata jin matuqar dadi tareda fadin Angama da wnn, tayi dakinta cikin farinciki marar misaltuwa.

NANA ce tafarka kamar kullum domin ita Sadiya dama can Sallah bai dameta ba, sai randa taga damane takeyi kuma nanma sai Rana tafito takan dauki tsawon watanni kapin tayi, ahakan kuma Asuba tafiyi domin sauran kam a gurin gantalin ta yake isketa.

 Fitowarta ganin dakin mahaifiyar tata babu haskene yasata fadin Lallai Mama yau tayi bacci me nauyi haryanxu bata farka ba? Don tasaba itake tadata daga bacci tayi alwala, suy sallarsu a tare, hakan yasata nufar dakin domin tadata.

Mama na nima yau narigaki tashi kinacewa ina bacci inmanta jikina, yau nima naxo tashinki, jitayi ba'a ansata ba hakan yasata lallabawa ta isa gurin da suke ajiya tocilan su kunnawa tayi amma bataga Maman akan gadon ba, tayi tunanin tazaga bayan gidane, hakan yasata fitowa bakin bayan gidan tana jiran fitowarta itama tasamu tashiga.

Lami ce ta fito tasameta a tsugune don tagaji da tsayuwar sosai qafafunta sunfara tsami, Nana mekikeyi a hanyar bayi kamar wata aljana, Mama nake jira haryanxu kuma bata fitoba.

Dariyace ya kwacewa Lami Wanda Nana tarasa ta mecece dariyar tunda itadai bata fadin maganar da xaisa mutum dariya ba a gurin.

Saida tagama dariyar muguntarta snn tai shiru kana tadubeta tace, ai da dauko kujera kikayi kika zauna don anjima kadan tsugunon ma gagarar ki xatayi,
Nana kuwa bata fahimci maganar nata ba, asalima ita bata dauki maganar nata da muhimmanci ba, don sau da dama Lami takanyi mata maganar da bata ganewa intajewa mahaifiyarta da maganar kuma saita cemata bakomai hakan ke nupi ba, duk da tasan ba gaxkiya maman nata ke gaya mata ba, amma takan bar abun a haka.


Wasa wasa tana gurin har gari yafara haske, hakan yasata matsawa dab da bayin snn tafara kiran Mmn nata ahankali bataji ta amsa ba snn bataji alamar akwai mutum a ciki bama kwata kwata, hakan yasata saurin leqawa,  Aiko bakowa.


Fitsari takeji amma a take tanemishi tarasa a gurin, dawowa dakin tayi nanma dai batanan da gudu tanufi qofar gida duk da tasan Mmn ta bataxuwa ko ina, ballema da asuba haka amma bata daddara ba, saida ta fito wajen layinsu tana dubawa, nanma shiru hankalinta ne yaqara tashi taiyo gida cikin Sauri kamar xata kife a qasa.


Sallama tafaryi a qofar Lami, itakuwa tana daki tanashan shayinta hankali kwance saice mata tayi kiriqe sallamar taki xai iya yimiki amfani wataran amma yanxu bashida muhalli anan kedai fadi damuwarki, cikin rawan murya tace Umma Banga Mamana ba, bata daki kuma bata bayangida, har waje naduba batanan.

Toooo Mamar taki jaririya ce daxakixo gurina cikiyarta? Kenake tamvaya kin tsareni da idanu kamar mayya, a'a tafada tareda fashewa da kuka tabar qofar dakin tayi waje.


Fitarta yayi daidai da shigowar Bbn nata, durqushewa tayi a gurin cikin kidima dakuma kuka take sanar mishi da halinda ake ciki, cikin fada yace mata karta qara tinkaranshi da wnn maganar duk inda taje ingaji xata dawo yayi shigewarsa cikin gidan yabarta a tsugune kamar maroqiya, kuka takeyi kamar ranta xaifita. Daga qarshe tadawo gidan tashiga dakinsu.

Sadiya tasamu tana yamutsa fuska alamar yanxune take tashi daga bacci, gaisheta tayi, snn tagaya mata, 😳 zaro idanu Sadiya tayi atake taji baccin ya watstsake mata, tanemi tarasa snn tace mata dagaxke kikeyi Nana?

Wlh dagaxke ne, Yaya kitaimakamin ke kadaice xaki iya taimakona, jawota tayi taxaunar da ita abakin gadon tareda cemata kidaina kukan kijirani ina xuwa karfa kifito, To tacemata tana goge idanu don tanada tabbacin Sadiya xata tayata nemo Mmn ta.


Dakin Ummanta tashiga kai tsaye inda tasamu tayi tagumi takasa shanye shayinda ke gabanta, zama tayi ta fuakance ta, snn tafara magana cikin qaramin murya.

Umma ina Mmn Nana take nasan kinada masaniya akan komai bana raba dayan biyu kece👉🏼 tanuna ta, Sauke hannayenta tayi tareda bata lbrn gaba daya abunda yafaru dakuma abunda yake faruwa na bayan shigowan Mal.Habu baiko kulata ba tana kuma yimishi magana yayi banxa da ita, yayi waje abunsa, abunda baitaba faruwa ba arayuwar aurensu tun farko sai yau.


Sadiya tai dagalo da baki gamida tafa hannayenta aqarshe tai salati snn tace Umma? meye hakan? Gaxkiya baki kyautaba wlh matarnan batai miki komai ba, kinhanata farin cikinta, mijinma bakulata yakeyi ba balle kisa ran xata samu wani abinsa,  duk da cin mutunci da kikemata aikin gidannan gaba daya itada 'yarta sukeyinsa, amma ko sau daya bata taba nunamiki alamar gajiyawarta kokuma kiji muryanta ba, yanxu haka bakisan halinda takeciki ba dawanda xata fada a gaba ba.

Lami kuwa ba nadaman abunda ta aikata takeyiba ko kadan damuwarta dai sauyin da yake qoqarin tunkarota, can tacewa Sadiya kitashi kibani guri inji da damuwarda take damuna, ai ance ka kashe macucinka tun kapin ya cuceka, mexanyi da kishiya koban cutar da itaba a ranta nasan tanada aniyar cutata.

Bansan meke damunki ba Sadiya Sam yanxu bakya kishina kamar da, kinaga ko maganar rashin auren nan naki, babu hannun Zainabu ne aciki? Hummmm bakida wayo ne ke arayuwarki, snn kinaga nima yau shekara Biyar da komawar Isah Burni haryau babu lbrnsa Dan wayarda yake mananma yanxu yadaina tunda naimishi kaca kaca akan nuna Zalamarsa akan yarinyar nan NANA taqarsa maganar da hada wani tagumin.

Itadai Sadiya tabe bakinta tayi tace mata yanxukam dai yarage naki, Donni yanxu inagab da barin garinnan tai ficewarta tabarta azaune cikin Zulumi.


A Fannin Mama kuwa tun fitarta daga gidan takejin kamar ana qara hankada ta don haka taita tafiya babu takalmi babu dankwali cikin qanqanin lokaci tai tafiya me Nisa, haka intana cikin tafiyar xataji ambata umarnin fara gudu, nan xakaga tafara cilla qafafunta tana gudu.....


Nana kuwa ganin datayi babu maimata maganar mahaifiyarta ne, yasata fita tayi gidan marigayi Mal.Dahiru inda tasamu Zuwaira duniya ta saita mata don tasake sabon aure, kuma a wnn gidan suke zaune, harma da qannenta, sallama tai musu, suka amsa fuskarnan a gimtse kamar sunga mutuwa..

Dama...dama.....dama.... tafadi don gabadaya ta rude, sakamakon hararar da take karba ta kowani fanni daga cikin su, Dame me? Zuwaira ta daka mata tsawa, a daburce ta sanar da su halinda ake ciki, dariya zuwaira tayi kapin tafara magana.


Allah me mutantani wai jaba taga bakin surukarta, Lallai Zainabu wato likafar karuwancin nata ya tabbata knn? Harta San ta tsallake mijinta da 'yarta tashiga duniya, yanxu mekike so muyi miki, mukai shela gidan radio ko telebijin? Inxaki kwantar da hankalinki ki kwantar  don kuwa Uwarki tashiga bariki.

Maganar ya matuqar yiwa NANA zafi amma haka ta dake tunda Neman agaji taxoyi tundaga haka bawanda yakulata, saican ne zuwaira tace daga gani ma yunwa ce kecinki gacan qanxo kidauko ki daka kisa gishiri kici, iyakan abunda xan iya miki knn!

Naqoshi tafada hadi da miqewa taimusu sallama bawanda yakulata tayi waje, tsugunawa tayi a gurin tafashe da kuka...

Mummyn Fadyl CE
