Fitowa ta. 2⃣3⃣



A hanyarta ta dawowa gidansu ne tatuna batamayi sallar asuba ba, cikin Sauri taqara gidan, batabi takan kowa ba, tanufi bayan gida tayi alwala tashiga daki, bayan ta idar da  sallan ne tajima axaune tana addu'oi cikin kuka, tana roqon Allah ya bayyanar mata da mahaifiyarta cikin qanqanin lokaci, domin batasan yanda xata cigaba da rayuwa a cikin gidan Babu Mamanta ba. Itace taxanma *TUDUN DAFAWA* nta.


*BAYAN WATA HUDU*

Haka dai rayuwartasu taci gaba da tafiya kowa a gidan quncinda yake fama dashi na dabanne, Nana tana fama da maraicin rashin uwa, uwa uba kuma duk wani haushin Lami akan ta take sauqeshi, Dan makarantan ma datake xuwa yana rage mata qunci anhanata xuwa wuni takeyi tana aiki, da daddarene kawai take samun sukunin yin kukanta a makwancinsu.

A fannin Lami kuwa duk yanda kake zaton abun yawuce tunaninka, don ynxu Mal.Habu ma yadaina kwana guri daya da ita, kuma bayajin maganarta banda kallon kyama babu abunda take samu agurinsa, don idan yamazaune intaxo gurin a take yake barin gurin, shiyasa take quntatawa NANA tanan kadai takesamun sauqin yanda qunci ke cinta a ranta.


Taje gurin Ayashe, tasanar da ita matsalar da take fuskanta, itako Ayashe, haka tayita karban kudadenta dasunan tana karbo mata magani, haka zataita zana mata yanda zatayi amfani dashi amma duk a banxa, a qarshe ma xuwa yanxu batada kudi a hannunta Dan kadarorinta ne daya ragemata, taketa dauka daya bayan daya tana saidawa.

Sadiya kuwa ahalinda ake ciki ba kasafai take zaman gidan ba, bin maxa takeyi na fitar hankali Wanda ita kanta mamakin abun takeyi takanji takaicin yanda tamaida kanta kariya, jakar gayu, har kuka takanyi wani lokacin idan maxa suka wulaqantata, da kudinta take bin wasu daga cikinsu amma suqi kulata..


Wata mata nahango a tsugune tanata kelaya Amai tamkar xata amayar da kayan cikinta, saida naleqa na hango fuskarta Zainabu nagani tagama fita a hayyacinta, tayi wujiga wujiga da ita, fuskarnan fayau kamar batada jini a jikinta, datti kuwa kamar xa'a kankara a jikinta, kannan babu dankwali gashin da kallonsa kaga alamar kwarkwata sunsamu gidan zamansu.


Tagama galabaita matuqa, komawa tayi taxauna tana dafe cikinta, fashewa tayi da kuka meqarfi a qarshe tamiqe dagudu tabar gurin tana gudu tana fadin Menai maka da qarfin gaxke.

Wnn sunan yariga yabi ta idan yara suka ganta binta sukeyi da gudu suna ihu sunacewa da ita Menai maka, itama tana nanatawa. Suko suita dariya...


*Bayan kwana Biyar* 


Kamar yanda yasaba nufo gidan haka yauma abun baicanxa zaniba, fuskar nan babu annuri yashigo tare dayin sallama murya can ciki, bakowa a tsakar gidan sai NANA da take ta aikace aikacenta, amsawa tayi tareda qarasawa gurinshi cikin Sauri domin takarbi kayan dake hannunsa.

Daga mata hannu yayi alamar karma tafara qarasowa gurinsa, turus tayi ta tsaya, shikuma yanufi dakin Lami kai tsaye, A kwance yasameta tayi dai~daya akwance tanata baccin asaran data saba..

Qafarta yadan zungura tamiqe a firgice ganin shine yasata saita nitsuwarta don tana mugun tsoron abunda xai hadata dashi, don da dukkan alamu qiris yake jira ya saketa, itakuwa bata fatan hakan, baxataso abunda xaimaidata rayuwarta ta baya ba, itakadai tasan wahalan da tasha, yanxu kuwa ko banxa tanasamu taci sau uku. A rana.


Kallon wulaqanci yay mata kapin daga baya yafara mata magana cikin salon umarni.

Lami Aure zanqara bangayamiki domin jin shawarar ki ba, nagayamiki ne donki tattara ki koma dakin Sadiya da kwana dagake har Nana kuhadu a can.

😳🙉 Nashiga uku tafada da qarfi Aure kuma? Meya faru, menai maka, waxaka aura, duka tambayoyinnan taita jero masa su, shidai baiko bi takanta ba yayi waje tareda tabbatar mata karya dawo yasameta a wnn dakin.


Bashiri bakinnan duk miyon bacci tafito da gyale a hannu tanupi gidan Ayashe hajaran majaran.

Samun Ayashe tayi taci uban kwalliya harda qunshi a qafarta abunda tajima bata gani ba ajikin Ayashe, amma ba wannan bane a gabanta, damuwarta asamo mata mafita.

Ko gaisawa bata jira sunyiba tahau arattako mata abunda yake tafe da ita, cikin ran Ayashe fari tas, amma fuskarta a murtuqe tace abunda yay miki knn? Eyyi wlh tabata amsa

Lallai mal Habu ya tsokalo tsuliyar Dodo, kinsan mexamuyi? Inajinki tace mata, kikoma gidanki kiyi zamanki a dakin Jibi insha Allah xan xo miki da wani maganin..

Haka xa'ayi Ayashe? Mexai hana muje yanxu gurin sabon malamin da kikace min kinyi? To kikawo dubu Ashirin yanxu mutafi, dubu ashirin? Tafada tareda dafe qirjinta, wlh Rabona da samun irin wnn kudaden harna manta, ni yanxu kudina ciki da waje dubu uku ne.

Ayashe tace to kinga dole kiyi haquri ki saurarre ni, jibi xan kwashi Adashena inyaso sai inmiki amfani dashi, nasan idan lamuran suka daidaita kya dawomin dasu.

Washe haqoranta gaba daya  tayi snn tace wlh harma da qari xanmiki Aminiya ta, kinmin komai arayuwa kece silar shigana jin dadi, Niko mexan miki in biyaki alheran da kikamin na rayuwa?

Kibari sai Jibi innaxo xan gayamiki, abinci Ayashe tamiqo mata, qinci tayi saboda tsabar murnan da take ciki, taimata bankwana tadawo gidanta.


Dawowanta gida hankalinta kwance tadauko dubu ukun da yarage mata adakin, takira Nana ta aiketa taiyo mata cefanen kayan dadi, tunda yanxu tanada tabbacin nanda jibi duk al'amarin nata xai daidaita kudi xaidawo hannunta yanda takeso.

Washe gari da safe tafito domin baiwa Sadiya kayan abinci don haryanxu saita gwada mata takeyi, ganin qartin maza tayi suna shigowa kai tsaye  Aqarshe kuma maigidan yabiyo bayansu.

Cemusu kushiga wnn dakin kufito da duk kayan ciki kushigar wnn yanuna dakin Sadiya da hannunsa.


Ashar lami tafariyi shiko baiko kalleta ba, nan ta ari hauka ta dorawa kanta taita surutai da zage zage, harta isa gabanshi da niyyar cakumo Wuyan rigarsa, wani kallo dayai matane yasata tsayawa cak, batai gaba ba batai bayaba, nanyabada umarnin a wullo mata da dukkayanta waje, intai niyya ta shigar inbatai niyya bakuma tabarshi a gurin..

Snn  yacewa Nana takwaso kayan dakinta tashigar dashi dakin Sadiya, sauran na mamanta kuma tabarshi yarato da dillali xa'ai gwanjonsu,  dasauri tamiqe tashiga dakin tahau tattara kayanta inda a can qasan akwatin mamanta tacikaro da kudade masu yawa a qalla xasukai dubu shabiyar,  dube dube tayi taga bawanda hankalinsa yake kan dakinnasu, cikin Sauri taninkeshi a bakin zaninta dakyau, snn tafara daukan kayan taiyo waje dashi..


Sukuma maxan basuy wata wataba suka fara kwasomata kayayyakinta suna watsowa waje ganinda tayi dagaxke nepa, yasata Shiga ciki dasauri tafara tattare kayayyakinta na sirri Wanda baxataso kowa ya ganiba, dayake majiya qarfine cikin mintuna qalilan suka kammala snn suka fara shirin gyare gyarenda yakawosu.


Mal.Habu bayan yagama nunamusu aikinne da yanda yakeson fasalin gurin yakasance, dakin Lami ne yakeson ahade mishi itada dakinsu Nana suxama ansamu ciki da falo, akuma yi rumfa ahado shi ta cikin gurinnata.
Nan yafita yy wucewarshi kasuwa.

(Sukuwa su Lami damacan dakin sadiyan gurin ajiyace batada girman zuwan Lamine yasa aka mayar dashi daki., zamuga yanda zaman dakin xai kasance)

 itakuma lami juyowa tayi taga Nana tana shirin shigarda sauran kayanta ciki, firgitaccen tsawa tadaka mata tareda cewa karki kuskura kikai kayanki cikinmu kidauki tsummokaranki kikai cikin Madafi, kidawo nikuma kishigarmin dakin Sadiya kishirya mini, nabaki minti Ashirin ki kammala kidawo kicigaba da girki.

Haka Nana tacigaba  a aiki baji babu gani, Lami duk da ranta babu dadi, amma tabar komi a hannun gobe, jiran Ayashe kawai takeyi ta iso mata da maganinta.

Har dab da mangariba masu aikin suka kammala snn suka tafi, Nana bata jira aka gayamata abunda yakamaceta ba, tafita tahau kintsa guraren cikin qanqanin lokaci tagama  bayan ta kamar sara mata shi akeyi tasan al'adar ta ne yake shirin xuwa, bayan tagama tadebi ruwan zafi tashiga bayan gida domin wanka.

Amma amaimakon wankan saita buge da kuka, kullum rashin mamanta sabo yake agareta, takasa sabawa da rashinta, tasan inda Mamanta tananan duk wuya aikin baxai kai hakaba, snn xataje makaranta, amma yanxu bata leqa koda qofar gida Lami tahana.

_WASHE GARI_ da safe wasu mutane sukaxo akaita jeren kayan Amarya lokacin Lami bata gidan tashiga maqotansu anyi haihuwa kapin tadawo harsun gama sun kulle gurin, fitowanta daga maqotan sukuma sunshiga adaidaita bata samuganin ko daya daga cikinsu ba, Sauri tafarayi ta iso cikin gidan, nan ta Taras da abunda sukayi Yakuma bata amsar tambyarda Zuciyarta keyi, don ta tabbatar Jere akayi.


Haka taxauna aranar ko abinci takasa ci, tagama qaguwa daga shigowan Ayashe amma har yamma shiru, haka ta haqura akan gobe da sassafe xatai mata Asubanci.


Guraren qarfe takwas tafarjin hayaniyar mutane batai yunqurin leqa wa ba, don tafison sai an watse ta iskota har dakinta tacimata zarafi kapin tacigaba fuskantar baraxana tunda tahada kishi da ita.

Jin hayaniyan ya lafa ne yasata miqewa tayi dakin Amaryan.............

*WNN SHAFIN NAKUNE MASOYAN  LAMI QAYA SOKI UBAN KOWA😜*



Fitowa ta. 2⃣4⃣


Sai da ta isa gab da dakin kuma taji faduwan gaban xuciyarta ya tsananta, amma haka tadaure saidai batasan meyasa ba taxo daniyyar hankadowa dakin batare da Neman izini ba, saikuma taji dakin yamata wani mugun kwarjinin da baxata iya aikata hakan ba.

Sai gata ta bige dayimata sallama cikin qasqantar da murya, A'ah Lami dadina dake rashin haquri taji muryan Ayashe ya doki kunnenta.

Wanakeji kamar Ayashe? Bata tsaya bata amsa daga ciki ba, taiyo waje.

Ayashe kedai naita jiranki tun safe bangankiba snn yanxu kishigo gidan baki isoni ba kishiga dakinta, tafada da alamun bacin rai a fuskanta,

 Itako bata tsaya bata amsa ba tadai kafeta da idanuwa kawai hakan yabata damar cigaba da magana.


Haba Ayashe banyi tunanin xakiymin hakaba, keda xamu  hada kai mu yaqi matar kuma Sai in sameki a dakinta mekikeyi to? Tafada murya qasa qasa.


Saida tajira takai Aya snn tace mata ai ba tayata nakeyi ba, asalima nice nakewa kaina yaqin aiba kowa a dakin nikadai ce. 

Lami kuwa bata dauki maganar da wata manufa ba, saice mata tayi, to ina Amaryan taje? Kodai maganin kika sa mata? kai  bangane nupinki ba.

Ayashe kuwa saida ta qanqantar da idanunta snn tace mata,
 kedai Lami bansan daqiqiyar ina bace Akwai wata Amaryan da tawuce nine? Kibude kunnenki dakyau kisaurareni nice sabuwar Matar Habu Baban Nana ba wata ba, yanxu shawara daya xanbaki tunda mutunci da arxiki kinemi takaddan sakinki a hannunsa tun dare bai miki ba, don da shirina naxo.

Lami kuwa Sai cewa tayi Ayashe yaufa ba ranar wasa bace, kinsan yanda xuciyata take min kuwa, kirasa ranar da xakimin wasa Sai yau?

Ayashe bata tsaya wata wataba ta tsinketa da wani gigitaccen mari Wanda yasa kwakwalwarta tafiya aiki na wucin gadi, idanunta kuwa duhu tagani tareda wasu taurari Wanda hakskensu yafi na Zara tauraruwa..


( maqiyan Lami nasan kunji dadi to wlh xan rama mata ne ehen 😜)


Muryan Ayashen ne yadawo da ita daga wnn duniyar inda takece mata wnn shine somin tabin uqubar Dana tanadar miki a wnn gidan, ina fatan kin farfado daga suman da kikayi...


Lami kuwa mamaki ne yake Neman halakata a gurin tamkar mutum mutumi haka ta tsaya, takasa fadin komai tsabar mamaki, Tsawa Ayashe ta daka mata Wanda yasata kusan kifewa batareda tashirya ba, itakuwa Ayashe ganin haryanxu lami bata dawo daidai ba yasata komawa cikin dakin inda tadauko ruwanta me sanyi data Adana don xubawa mal.Habu magani aciki.


Taiyo waje dashi, Lami tananan tsaye a 
Gurin, Sai sauqan ruwa me shegen sanyi tayi a jikinta, firgigit tayi nan tasamu tadawo hayyacinta snn gaba daya maganganun suka tarayo xua cikin kwakwalwarta tareda sarrafa mata manufarsu...


Aiko nan itama jarabar masifarta ta motsa tafara xuba mata manya manyan Ashar, snn tace ke harkin isa ki munafurceni in kyaleki? Ki auremin miji, kina tunanin xan barki ne Ayashe, to kinyi qarya wlh baxai yiwuba dole kibarmin gidana.


Sheqewa Ayashe tayi da dariya kapin tace wai miji hahahhaah idan Ana maganar miji aikema baxakisa bakiba tunda a hannun wata kika kwaceshi, nikuwa kinsan karban goruba a hannun kuturu abune mai matuqar sauqi ko kin manta ni shugabanki CE a hatsabibanci?

Dakike maganar munafurci kuwa kisaurareni kikuma bani kwanaki kadan wlh xaki tantance tsakanin Aya da tsakuwa domin Wanda yarigaka bacci dolene yarigaka tashi, kinsan Allah Lami matuqar bakibar gidannan tun ana iya gane kamanninki ba to xansa kibarshi lokacinda kika xama abun kwatance taqarashe maganar nata tareda hankadeta snn tayi cikin dakinta takuma banko qofa.

Lami kantane taji yana juyamata , da kyar tasamu takai kanta dakin Sadiya inda tasamu Nana a kwance a tsakar dakin tana baccinta amma mafarkin kuka takeyi.

Aiko kamar Allah ya aiko Lami haka tarufeta da duka, baji bagani tun Nana tana iya ihu har abun ya gagareta, shiru tayi tacigaba da karban saqonnin dukan aduk kusurwan jikinta.

Don kanta tagaji tadaina takoma gefe taxauna tana maida numfashin azaba, rasa mexatayi tayi a wnn lokacin hakan yasata cewa Nana baxata kwana tareda ita a dakin ba, don haka takoreta waje gashi lokacin sanyi ne...

Haka tafito qofar dakin ta shimfida fallen zaninta takwanta tareda duqunqunewa guri daya, amma kamar qara kado sanyin akeyi gurinta, kamar tagudu tabar gidan takeji.


Itako Lami kwanciya gagararta yayi nan tahau safa da marwa acikin dakin data gaji da hakan ne taleqo da kanta ta taga, aiko idonta yy muguwar gani, don hango Mal.Habu tayi xai wuce hannunsa dauke da ledoji niqi niqi kowanne a sheqe da kaya,  kamar tabishi ta kwace haka takeji amma inaa wani masifaffen tsoronsa da jarabarsa ce ta dirar mata lokaci daya.

Shikuwa ta gaban Nana ya wuce amma baiko luraba balle yagane mutum be a kwance, dakin Ayashe yayi wa tsinke, inda takuma ta tabude mishi qofar daga ita saiwata shedaniyar Riga, dake jikinta Wanda tsayinsa baiwuce guiwarta ba, A lalace yake kallonta duk ya shagalta, cikin kissa takarbi ledojin hannunsa, tareda jawoshi ta rungume shiko tsintan kansa yayi da biyemata saboda dokinta dayake ji.

Sunjima ahaka kapin tafara zare jikinta, fita waje tayi tahada mishi ruwan wanka, snn tasanar miqews yayi xainufi bayin tariqo hannunsa tabaya tsayawa yayi itakuma tajuyo snn tace mishi, ina xanbarka kaje kayi wanka batareda na taimaka maka ba saikace badan gata ba?

Yanda kikace hakan xa'ayi yafada ciki washe bakinsa, bata jira cewarsa ba taja hannunsa xuwa bayan gidan, bayan yacire kayansa ne ta shagala da kallon qasansa, a ranta tace ahhh lallai Lami dole kikasa barin Habu da wata macen, hummm aiko kinyi bankwana dashi har Abada.

Aibatasan lokacinda takai musu cafka ba cikin marmari da zumudi nantake yaji yafara kawo wuta don cikin salo take murza mishi su.

Cikin Axama yariqota bata mata mishi lokaci ba, donkuwa itama ta mugun yabawa da kayan aikinnasa, nan suka fara aika ma junansu saqon da yake azalzalan xuciyarsu saboda sungama zaquwa sujisu a matakin qarshe baisan lokacinda ya sabota domin yiwowa daki da itaba, yayo daidao da fitowan Lami domin sauqe sauran haushinta akan Nana acewarta ta isheta da sheshsheqar kuka.


Mutuwar tsaye tayi xata gansu, ai bata San lokacinda ta ari hauka tai Kansu ba amma kapin ta cimmusu sun shigo dakin, itako Ayashe taganta don haka suna Shiga ta diro tabanko qofar tareda sako sakata. Takoma gurin mijinta suka baje a gado, bantsaya ganin me xasuy ba don 'yan _Intelligent writers group_ sun hanani fadin komai.

Buga qofar tayi har hannunta yafara zafi, gashi banda nishinsu babu abunda take jiyowa hakan yaqara tunxurata ga kishinda take cin xuciyarta, yanda takeji matuqar tacigaba dajin wnn abubuwan to wlh tanada tabbacin xata iya rasa rayuwarta don ba shakka xuciyarta fashewa xatayi.

Dataga babu me saurarontane yasata juyowa kotakan Nana batabi ba tai shigewarta daki, itakuma Nana hakan baqaramin dadi yai mataba, takoma ta qudundune jikinta takoma bacci.

Lami yanda taga rana haka taga dare inda a fannin su Ayashe kuma suka more wnn daren (kunsan Ayashe 'yar bariki ce tasan kan harka)

Washe gari da sassafe Lami tafara kicinyar karya gadonta da sauran tarkacenta domin kaiwa gidan dillalai saboda tanada buqatar kudi sosai, akan Sadiya takeda tabbacin samun kudi a yanxu, amma rabonda tahada ido da ita yau kwana goma sha biyu knn, gashi al'amura sun cakude mata. .

Tana cikin aikinne taji cikinta ya isheta da qugin yunwa, bashiri taiyo waje domin ganin me Nana takeyi har xuwa yanxu bata kawo mata abun kari ba.

Samun NANA tayi a tsakar gida da Allon karatunta a hannun ta, ke Nana menake gani haka ina zakije, waya baki umarnin shiryawa xuwa makarnta?

Nana tana shirin yin magana Ayashe tafito cikin shiga mekyau taci uban kwalliya cikin dinkin Riga da zani, fuskarnan tasha fente fente irinnasu na 'yan bariki, tana taunar cingam kawai qas~qas kakeji.


Cikin yauqi tafara magana, Ke Nana wuce kitafi makaranta, snn inkintaso dakina xakishigo akwai aikinda xan saki, Itako kamar jira takeyi tayi waje kamar ana hankada ta.

Juyowa gurin Lami tayi tace mata dama nasan xaki fito kodon cikinki ma, to bari kiji daga rana irinta yau bakida abinci a gidannan kalli can tanuna mata gefen dakin Sadiya, kwanukan ta tagani axube kaca kaca.

Tamaido dubanta gurin Ayashen to wadannan kwanukan baxasu shiga min madafi ba, to banda abinki ma Lami mexakiyi da kwanuka tunda ba girki xakiyi ba? Aikeda girki a gidannan saidai idan nice nacemiki kifito ki karba, don haka yanxu tamkar zaman kanki kikeyi daga haka tabarta agurin.

A take xuciya ta kwasheta tai cikin dakinta mayafi kawai tadauka tayi waje...


Zainabu ce a Zaune akan Bola tadauko rubabben buredi tanaci tana kuka tana fadin menai maka?  Kwari ne sukebin jikinta Wanda yasata taketa qaiqaya jikinta babu sassauci banda datti babu abunda take kartowa, faracunta sunyi zaro zaro kamar na tsoffin arna.

Wata hadaddiyar babbar motane tai _parking_ gefen bolan dayake yamma  yayi sosai harma anshiga sallan mangariba, nacikinne yacewa direban itace wancan yanuna Zainabu A hankali xakai mata don xata iya fallawa aguje.

To Ranka ya Dade, nan yafita yanufeta a hankali amma.............

Fitowa ta. 2⃣5⃣


Yana isowa gareta itakuma aka bata umarnin guduwa daga gurin don haka tai wurgi da guntun rubabben burodin nata, sauri yayi ya isa gurin tareda taliyeta da qafansa nan taxube tafadi...

Kallonshi tafarayi tareda furta masa Menai maka? Baikulata ba yafara qoqarin janta amma taqi binshi ganinda yayi zata bata masa lokacine don gashi wasu masallatan har sun idar, yasan yanxu xa'afara fitowa, aiko cikin sauri ya sungumeta inda wani qarni da hamamin datti ya ziyarci hancinsa, dakyar ya qarasa da ita gurin motar.

Mai gidan nasa yana ganin ya iso da ita ya fito yakoma gaba, shikuma yasata agidan baya, kalle kalle takeyi tana kuka, saida yarufe snn yadawo gaba yaxauna, sannu idi yafada shikuma yace bakomai megida ka cancanci haka, amma megida gani nakeyi kamar pa wnn tanada ciki, haba dai? Yafada cikin jindadi.

Allah kuwa tanada ciki don kuwa harya dago ma, haskata yayi nan yahango tumbinta daya fara tasowa saman riganta, farinciki ne ya dabaibayeshi. Yasan xaisha garabasa, ga hauka ga ciki, dadi kan dadi. 

Iskan motane yasata bacci batareda tasani ba tana riqe da kanta data dorashi jikin glass, kalar tausayi da ita.


Alhaji Ado dai mutumin garin Kaduna ne, kuma shahararren Dan siyasa, Wanda yayi amanna da tsafi, yagama yarda cewa da tsafinsa dukkan sa'a take cimmishi.

Acikin wnn halinne siyasarshi takusa rawa, nankuwa Bokansa yabashi tabbacin duk bayan sati biyu yana kwanciya da mahaukaciya sai uku akwana uku, inko bahaka ba, yana gab da rasa kujerarsa harma da rugujewar duk wani abunda ya mallaka.

Shiko gogannaka tunda yataba yaji ai gabadaya mahaukatan ma sunpi masu hankalin dandano.

Kamar yanxu ma dawowarshi daga Maradi kenan cikin nijar, yakaiwa BOKANSA xiyara sakamakon kiran gaggawan da yamishi, shine yabiyo ta katsina domin gaisawa da abokinsa, yahadu da Zainabu a bakin kasuwa yara sunabinta.

Tundaga wnn ranar yake bibiyarta, daga sun Dana mata tarko xata wargaxa, don saika gama sakankancewa xaka kamata xata xura a guje, kapin qiftawar ido kuwa baxakaga inda tabi ba.

Hakanne yasa yau suka shirya yimata dabara, aiko sunci sa'a sun sameta. 
       _WNN KNN_


Lami xuwa tayi tasamu dillaliya domin ayi gwanjon kayanta, inda tabiyota har gidan taduba kayayyakin, tunawa datayi da kashedin Ayashe yasata tattarowa harda kwanukan a tunaninta in burinta yacika tasai sababbi daman tagaji da wadannan.

Bayan tagama dubawa tacewa Lami gabadaya kayan tamusu kudi naira dubu talatin, Talatin Lami ta fada cikin daga murya, Haba Indo bafa kayan sata bane, yaushe mai nai kayan, basu ji jiki ba kece shaida.

Ni inma badon inada buqatar kudiba a yanxu babu abunda xai sani sayarda kayayyakina don inason abuna matuqa.

Kinsan kinaso kika nemani Indo ta katseta, nan Lami ta sassauta murya tafara roqonta akan taqara tunda kayan da mutuncinsu, amma Indo qirmisisi taqi qarawa, daga qarshe ma cewa tayi Lami bata shirya sayarda kayan ba taga tafiyarta.

Saurin shan gabanta Lami tayi don tafi buqatan kudin  snn tace mata kitaimaka min koba yanxuba kimin alqawarin qaramin wani Abu akai, a'ah wlh Lami baxan qara komai akan talatin dinnan ba, kinga inafa da aiki agidana kika daukoni inason komawa, tafadi tareda nufar qofar fita

Kawo kudin to Lami tafada cikin halin narasa yanda xanyi, nan Indo dillaliya ta irgo kudin tamiqa mata tareda cewa anjima xata aiko a kwashe mata kayayyakin.

A cikin kudin Lami taxari Dari biyar tasamo almajiri yasayo mata kayan shayi a gurin masu teburin shayi tasamu takarya snn tajita dai dai.

Wanka tafito yi amma wlh haka tanaji  tana gani ya gagareta saboda Ayashe cemata tayi ai tagama yin wanka a bayin tun kapin xuwanta, saidai tajira da dare tafito tayi a tsakar gida. Inko ba hakaba ta dauwama cikin tsami babu abunda yadameta tayi wucewarta.

Itako yanda tasan wulaqanci da rashin mutuncin Ayashen ne bashiri ta wanke qafafunta tashafa musu Mai snn tadauki dubu Ashirin a hannun ta ta adana sauran canxunan nata snn tanufi gidan Maye.

Ayashe ko tasan Lami tasaida kayanta gaba daya domin tafiya gidan bokane ( kunsan ance mugu shiyasan makwancin mugu)  kuma tanada tabbacin baxata Debi dukkan kudin takai mishi ba. Shiyasa tana ganin fitarta tayi cikin dakin batasha wani wahala ba ta tattare canxunan gaba daya tayi gaba dasu tana shu'umin dariya, badon tarasa kudin bane yasata kwashewa lami nata, itadai burinta yanxu taga Lami a wulaqance.


Lami tana isa gurin maye tasanar mishi gaba daya abubuwanda suka faru bata boye masa komai ba, nan yahau surkullensa shikanshi yana mamakin meyasa yanxu indai yatashi aiki to baya ganin komai, haka yanxu ma al'amarin Lami gaba daya a kulle yake baiga komai ba. Amma haka yake dikake yake basu magunguna duk da yanada tabbacin baxasuy musu amfani ba, yaxaiyi shima tahakan yake samun abincinsa.

Saida yagama abubuwan tsafinsa snn yamiqo mata garin maganin akan ta barbada abakin qofar dakin Ayashe da rana xata barbada kuma karta kuskura a ganta. Da tsorata xa sharadinda ya gindaya mata amma tunda Neman biyan buqata takeyi saitaji ai xata iyayin komai.

Tafiya sukeyi gaba jeji baya jeji motar tayi tsit bacci Mama takeyi inka kalli fuskarta Bacin datti xakai tsammanin hankalinta  daya don bacci takeyi cikin nutsuwa.

Jifa jifa Alhajin yakanyi waya da abokanan harkansa inda shikuma direban yamaida hankalinsa gurin tuqi kadai.

Gab da shiga garin Kaduna ne Zainabu ta farka kalle kalle tafarayi tako ina bata ganin kanta tayi acikin mota nanfa fa firgita tahau buge bugen glass tanason fita hakanne ya farkar dasu halinda take ciki gani sukai harda fasa glass din hannun ta kuwa sai jini yakeyi, nan direban yatsaida motar a gefen hanya, sukai saurin fitowa bude motar keda wuya tafito ta ruga aguje kapin suyi yunqurin kamota harta yi kan titi a guje, inda atake wata mota qirar _RANGE ROVER_ tai kanta.....

Ganin haka yasa Alhaji gaggawan cewa direbansa kai yisauri kashiga mota mubar nan gurin aiko nan suka figi motarsu suka bar gurin a gaje kamar xasu tashi sama.

Cikin Sauri naga wani mutum mai cikar kamala yafiyo cikin tashin Hankali yanufo gareta, mutanene suka fara cicirindo a gurin a take yace sutaimaka masa asata acikin mota, baisha wahala ba atake isarsu asibitin aka karbeta saninshi da akayi babban mutum mai muqami..

Likitocin sun duqufa akanta sunsamu ta farfado amma sunyi bincike akanta sungano cewa mahaukaciya CE, don basuy duba da yanayin jikinta. Ba, saboda mutane da Dama masu hankali suka tsinci Kansu cikin hali na datti da musgunawar rayuwa fiye da nata  dalilin hakanne yasa saida suka binciketa sosai da sosai.

Likitanne yayi kiran Alhajin xuwa _office_ dinsa bayan sun xaunane yake sanar dashi cewar kawai buguwa tayi amma yanxu Alhmdllh komai yaxo cikin sauqi saidai fa mahaukaciya ce, Mahaukaciya kuma yatambaya, gyada mishi kai yayi alaman tabbatarwa.

Amma _doctor_ kanada tabbacin ba'a hatsarinnan tasamu matsalan nan ba, eh gaxkiya a iya bincikena tajima da haukannan snn abunda nakeson sanar maka shine dalilin hatsarinda tayi abunda yake cikinta idan ba'a luraba xai iya samun matsala.

Ciki kuma? yaqara mishi tambaya a karo na biyu, tareda nanata Kalmar innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraju'uj a xuciyarsa yana girmama wnn al'amarin ace mahaukaciya ma bata tsiraba, nanma likitan ya tabbatar mishi da abunda yafadi.

Shknn karubuta magungunan daya dace da irim masu lalurarta insha Allah xan taimaketa, haka likiyan yagama duk wani abunda ya dace snn ya sallamesu, dakyar da taimakon alluran baccinda akai mata yasamu aka samishi ita cikin mota yai gidansa da ita......

Wanene wnn mutumin?

Alhaji Abdullahi Maitama Dan Asalin garin Kaduna ne cikin garin Zaria amma yana zama a cikin kadunan, mutum ne me karamci idan akadawo fannin arziki kuwa daganinshi kasan baya kukan Naira, saidai kace Nairan CE xatai kuka saboda tasan indai tadhigo hannun to fa saita kasu.

 Dalilin dawowarshi Kaduna yasamu asaline ta sanadiyyar Abokinshi, Amininshi  kuma Dan uwansa, Alhaji Mukhtar Sabo.

Alhaji Mukhtar sabo unguwarsu daya da Alhaji Abdullahi Maitama iyayensu maqota ne, tun suka yara suka taso da abotansu , babu abunda ke rabasu sai gurin xuwa makaranta, don iyayen Abdullahi basuda halo sabanin iyayen Mukhtar.

Amma a qarshe saida mukhtar yasa baban shi yadauki nauyin karatun Abdullahin har suka gama matakin sakandiri, duk da matar shi wato maman mukhtar bataso hakam ba amma bayanda ta iya don tana tsoron mijinta.

Bayan sun gama sakandiri ne Allah yayiwa mahaifin mukhtar rasuwa, dalilin hakan yasa karatun Abdullahi yatsaya don babu yanda iyayenshi xasuyi ya cigaba, mukhtar kuwa yayi yayi da mamnshi tayarda sucigaba tare tunda akwai kudin amma qirmisisi taqi, haka ta turashi karatu qasar waje.

Haka sunaji suka gani suka rabu, randa xasuyi bankwana da juna ne Mukhtar yame tabbatar wa Abdullahi cewa insha Allahu abotarsu baxata yankeba yakuma yimishi alqawarin indai yadawo xaitaimaka mishi.

Haka rayuwar tacigaba inda Abdullahi yacigaba da xuwa kasuwa shagon dinki yana Neman kudinsa acan yana tallafawa iyayenshi shikuma mukhtar yanacan qasar waje yana karantar _law_.

Bayan shekaru bakwai Mukhtar yadawo da takardar kammala karatunshi inda yazamo cikakken _barrister_ baqaramin murna sukayiba na ganin junansu tun mahaifiyar mukhtar tanaqi harta gaji ta xuba musu idanu.

Abotansu bata tsaya anan ba har aure ma tare sukayi inda mukhtar ne yagina musu gidan da suke xaune da matayensu, snn a wnn lokacin mukhtar ya ware kudade masu yawa yabawa Abdullahi shikuma yaha jarin kansa.

Mukhtar yana xaune da matarsa me suna khadija, dansu daya aduniya *Aliyu* wanda yanxu shekararsa ashirin a duniya tundaga Kansa basu qara samun haihuwa ba hakan yasa son duniya suka Dora akansa.

Shikuma Abdyllahi Matarsa sunanta Hajara yaransu uku, babban shine sa'an Aliyu Wanda wata daya ne tsakaninsu wato Ibrahim, sai khadija takwaran maman Aliyu, sai qaramarsu habiba, yanxu haka tanada ciki.
Matannasu ma aminta ce sosae tsakininsu da mutunta juna....

Masubin novel dina da gaxke ne xasu iya tuna *KHADIJA  DA DANTA ALIYU*....

Fitowa ta.2⃣6⃣


A halinda ake ciki yanxu Aliyu Wanda yaran suka laqaba mishi suna Yaya Aliy da ibrahim Wanda shima sukece mishi Yaya IBBY sungama karatun su na _secondary_ a _Turkish_  yanxu kuma suna karatu a jami'ar  qasar _Uganda_  ajinsu biyu inda Aliy yake karantar likitanci a fannin mata wato _gynecology_ haka IBBY shima likitancin yakeyi amma fannin yara wato _pediatric_.


*Aliy* kyakykyawa ne Wanda karon farkon haduwarka dashi xaka fahimci hakan, yakasance me  cikar halitta irinta maza, ba fari bane can snn baxamu kirashi baqi kai tsaye ba, xadai mu iya kiran shi da _chocolate colour_ fuskarsa tanada tsayi dai dan gwargwado, yanada saje kwantance a fuskarsa baqi sidik datake haska fuskarsa, yanada dogon hanci, hummm mai karatu nabarka ka qarasa siffantashi amma inason ka ajiyeshi a sahun kyawawa kawai.


Misskili ne sosae da sosae, indai kaganshi yana wasa da dariya to yana cikin ahalinsa ne,bashida jijji dakai shidai maganace bata dameshi ba, shiyasa mutane da dama suke tsoron fuskantarsa don atunanisu daganinsa xaiyi wulaqanci, halinsa yanada wuyar fahimta, amma muddin ka fagimceshi kuma ka xauna tare dashi yanada sauqin mu'amala.


Sabanin Ibby Wanda shi mutum ne wara~wara faram faram da jama"a gwani a fagen barkwanci don xaiyi matuqar wuya kaxauna da IBBY na mintuna qalilan batare da kayi dariya kamar cikinka zai qulle ba, snn shima yanada kyau daidai halittarsa...

*WNN KNN*

Yana isowa maigadi ya wangale mishi _gate_ na matuqar tsorata da tsarin gidan nasu  wannan shine aljannar duniya nafadi hakan a raina, _parking space_ yanufa don yau shikadai yafita juyowa yayi ya kalleta haryanxu baccinta takeyi, don haka yafito a hankali don karya tasheta yanufi cikin gidan.


A falo ya isketa zaune habiba tana kwance a kan qafafunta suna hira da khadija tana bata labarin Kayanda mummy (Mmn Aliy knn, mamansu kuma Ummi) xata saya mata idan tadawo daga umrah kai kedai khadija bakya rabuwa da yawan magana, abunda ke hadani da ke....bata qarasa maganar nata ba sallamar Abbu ya katseta Amsa sallamar sukayi nan yaran sukahau yimishi barka da dawowa shikuma cikin sakewar fuska yayi musu Barka da gida.


Sama yanufa kai tsaye a take Ummy tarufa mishi baya suka nufi saman a tare suna hira jefi jefi.


Suna isa dakinshi yakamo hannun ta bayan yaxauna a kan Sofa hankalinta ta tattaro gaba daya tamiqa xuwa gareshi ganin hakanne yasa shi fara magana, Ummynsu da baquwa nake tafe fa, baquwa kuma? amma kai kadai naga kashigo ai, bani kadai bane yabata amsa... 


Nan yagaya mata duk abunda yake faruwa, dayake macece mai ilimi nan take tagamsu, snn suka fara shawarwarin yanda xasu shigo da ita ciki, shine yafara magana inda yacemata kinsan mexa'ayi? Saika fada tafadi tareda zama akusa dashi, bari insanar da Mukhtar baxai rasa mafita ba, yafadi hakan yana zaro wayarsa daga aljihunsa.


Ringing daya xuwa biyu yayi aka daga wayar bayan sungaisa ne tareda wasansu na barkwanci don mukhtar mutum ne me wasa da dariya, don har cewa sukeyi kowa ya haifi me halin dayan.

Kar barni kai idan aka biyemaka baxa'ai komai ba, kasan meyake faruwane, saika fada mijin me ciki,mtsww Abdullahi yy tsaki snn ya Sanar dashi komai.

Saida yayi jimm na 'yan daqiqu snn yace mishi kajirani bari inqaraso OK to shknn saikaxo.

Alhaji mukhtar fitowa yayi yasamu Mummy a falo yace mata Matar gidannan, juyowa tayi tareda cemishi Megidannan cikin salon tsokana, cemata yayi tadauko mayafi su shiga gidansu Ibby,batare da ta tambayeshi mexasuy ba, tafito tareda shiga dakinta Hijabinta tadauko har qasa suka fito tare.


Bayan sun iso gidanne akayi gaishe gaishe nan kowa yafara fadin Albarkacin bakinsa inda a qarshe mummy ta roqi abar mata ita kodon cikinda akace akwai a jikinta tunda itace batada qaramin yaro, insha Allah xata riqeta Amana har xuwa lokacinda xata samu sauqi...

Sun tattauna sosae akan maganar inda a qarshe akayi amanna da batun mummy aka dunguma domin xua gurinta bude motar Abba yayi aiko mummy na had a idanu da ita tasake wani salati me qara tareda dafe kanta tareda fadin wlh nasanta tareda fashewa da kuka me ban tausayi, mamakinsu bai tsawaita ba domin kukan mummy yatasar da Mama daga bacci tahau zazzare idanu cikin qanqanin lokaci tafara qoqarin fita ta window cikin azama.

Abunka da mahaukaci kiciniya takeyi iya qarfinta don ganin tafita, cikin hanzari batare da tunanin komai ba Mummy tayi cikin motar tana kuka, Saidai kafin ta isa gareta hartayi nasaran fasa glass din motar hannun ta yaji rauni, qaramin glass dinda ya maqale mata a hannun tane ganin mummy na nufota yasata qoqarin tureta cikin rashin sa'a tacaki mummy a fuskarta gab da idanunta.


Wani qara me hade da ihu na azaba mummy tasake tareda rude gurin da tafin hannun ta a take kuwa jini ya wanke mata hannun, cikin axama daddy yayi cikin motar yafito da ita, Ummy CE tafara matsowa snn Abba, nan daddy yace kawai awuce asibiti don shi gabadaya ya tsorata kardai ace idanun khadija ya tabu, Abba kuma jikinshi yy sanyi don yasan duk shine sila..

Mai gadi suka kira tareda mai bawa fulayi ruwa, suka CE suriqe ta don karta qara jima kanta ciwo direba ya tuqasu, snn sukuma suka shige wata motar , aka dunguma sai asibiti.....

********** Haka tanufo hangar gida bata tsaya jiran mai adaidaita ba don ganitake bata mata lokaci xaiyi kanaganin fuskarta xata ga annashuwan da yake kwance akai. 


Ranta wasai tasan yaukam xatai maganin matsalarta, har ita Ayashe xata munafurta? Humm lokaci qanqani gidanta na Neman gagararta? Ko a mafarki vata gaba tunanin Ayashe xatai mata hakaba, wlh kuma shikanshi Mal.Habun idan yadawo hannun ta saiya dandana kudarsa duk da tasan bawai yana cikin haiyacinsa bane.

Baxai yiwuba tafadi hakan a fili tareda yin kwafa, 

Sam bataji gajiya a tare da ita ba, duk da kuwa Nisan tafiyar, gab da layin da xai sadata da gidannata ne, taxo wucewa taji wasu samari dayan nacewa kamar pa wnn ce Maman sadiya, karaf sai a kunnenta, juyowa tayi tareda kafesu da idanu.

Suko ganin haka yasasu saurin fara tafiya, kamar tabisu amma domin yaji mesuke tattaunawa akai Wanda har sunanta Dana 'yarta ya gifta, amma sai taga ai  abunda ke gabanta yafi qarfin matsalar su, don haka kawai tayi wucewarta, tabarsu.

A Dan gefe da gidannasu Kadan taga cicirindon mutane kamar xasu Cin kasuwa, tayi mamakin abun harxata wuce xata shiga gidan sai wata xuciyar ta kwabeta akan ta leqa mana,aiko wnn xuciyar CE ta rinjayeta, cikin Sauri tajuya takoma gurin.

Haka taita gifta al'umma da kyar tasamu ta isa 😳😳😳 mexata gani🙉🙉🙉.............

Fitowa ta.2⃣7⃣


Sadiya tagani babu kayan arziki a jikinta, kaca kaca da jini ai batasan lokacinda ta kurma ihu ba tafadi a gurin sumammiya..


Nan kallo yadawo kanta akai saurin dagata aka shigar da ita gida tareda ciccibo Sadiya itama akaiyo ciki da ita.

Duk abunnan da akeyi a tsakar gidan Ayashe tana daki, tadai leqo ta window tunda taga meke faruwa takoma kan gadonta tai kwanciyarta, kamar batasan acikin gidan ake sha'ani ba, duk kuwa da irin doka sallamar da aka dinga kwadawa qarshe suka tabbatar da bakowa a gidan..


Ruwa aka debo aka yayyafawa lami nan tasauke numfashi maikama Dana tsabar wahala, babu salati abakinta illa wani ihun data qara kurmawa tareda miqewa har zani na faduwa a jikinta, yabarta dawani baqin siket dinta bata kulaba tayi kan Sadiya da gudu.

Jama'a kuwa ganin rashin hankalinda takeyi ne yasasu barin cikin gidan daya bayan daya.

Gurin sadiyan tanufa tadurqusa tana jijjigata amma babu motsin jijiya balle bugawar qirji a tattare da ita, haka taita ihu babu qaqqautawa tamaida gidan tamkar gidan mahaukata, don kuwa duk Wanda yaganta baxai taba yimata kallon me cikakken hankali ba.


Jin kururuwan da takeyine yafara damun kunnuwan Ayashe cikin yamutsa fuska tafito daga dakinta, dama tayi zargin hakan zaifaru, qullin magani tagane a gurin zaninda yafadi ajikin Lami Wanda tama manta da kashinsa.

Bata tsaya ga komai ba, tsugunawa tayi tadau maganin kunceshi tayi snn tazo gurin Lami din, bata damu da halin da take ciki na mutuwar diyarta ba, tana tsaye ta kunce qullin maganin ta zaxzageshi akan Lami tsaf, nan Lami ta Ankaro amma Sam batada wnn lokacin.

Yanda bata dago ta kalleta ba, haka itama bata fasa surfa mata buhun bala'i ba, a qarshe kuma tace tasan yanda zatai da gawan 'yarta don yanxu xai iya fara damunsu da wari....

Wani tunanin ne yafado mata tamiqe zumbur harta nufi qofar fita ta taka qusa, wani azaba ne ya ziyarceta har tsakar kanta, ta durqusa domin cire qusan ne a lokacin talura babu zani a jikinta juyowa tayi taganshi can jefe, tana dingisa qafa ta qarasa tadauka, wani iri haka tadaurashi tayi waje tanajan qafafunta saboda zogin da yake mata, gakuma jini.

Tsaki Ayashe tayi, duk da harga Allah a ranta bahaka taso qarshen Sadiya ba, aiko banxa taci ajikin ta, kuma snn itace duk silar lalacewarta, duk uwarkice  tafada tareda nuna gawar da hannunta, da mugun baqin halinta ta jawo muku, kekam kintafi kinbarta itaxata cigaba da dandana kudarta a hannuna kapin Dan nata yadawo shima ya jonata. 


Can kuma dai taji a ranta tanason sanin meya faru, ai antake tadauki niyyar xuwa gurin Alhaji Nuhu domin jin menene silar mutuwan Sadiya.

****************
Taimakon Allah ne kawai ya kaisu asibiti, duk da su biyu ke riqe da ita, amma hakan baihanata qoqarin fusgewa ba, kapin su isa asibitin duk sungama yin laushi, itako sai wani gurnani takeyi, idan abun yayi kaman ya lafa, to sai tafara fusgan gashin kanta tana jijjiga jikinta can kuma saikaga ta fashe da kuka tana fadin menai maka?

Suna isowa daddy yai saurin kama dayan hannun mummyn yashiga da ita A&E nan aka tarbesu tareda shiga da ita dressing room,saida yabari anshiga da ita snn yafito waje nan yasamu anata artabu da Mama don kuwa ankasa fitowa da ita daga cikin motan.

 
Can dai dabara yafado musu Abba yayi saurin dauko wani snacks dinshi daya manta a mota yanufo ta dashi, ganin da tayi abuncine ya hannunsa yasata qoqarin xuwa gurin shi, shikuma ahakan da taimakon wnn snacks dinne har ya fara janta, duk da anatafiya tana tuburewa amma ahakan dai har suka isa psychiatric, tabbas idan kaje wnn gurin sai ka rasa me yake maka dadi, likitoci suka xo anan Abba yamiqa mata snacks din amma taqi karba saima fashewa da kuka datayi tareda kafeshi da idanuwanta tana kuma nanata menai maka?


Kallon juna suka farayi da Daddy itakuwa Ummy bata iya qarasowa gurin nasu ba, don tamugun karaya tunda tahango mahaukata burjik a gurin kukanta taketa risga.

Wani doctor ne yafito acikin dabara irin tasu yasamu yajata snn yanunawa su daddy hanyar da xasubi su sami office dinshi su jirashi anan..

Gaba dayansu sunyi mamakin yanda tabishi batare da amsamu matsala ko tangarda ba, wnn shi ake kira dabara kashin kwance Wanda bai iyaba ya bata jikinsa Abba yafada yana kallon daddy muje ko yafada, kai wani muje banje naqara dubo matata ba? Kazo dai mukoma mudubo lafiyarta inyaso idan angama mudawo.

To shknn tafada suka qara juyawa suka koma A&E din, sun samu ancire glass din harkuma angama mata dressing din, abunka da tsautsayi kusa da ido, nandanan har kamanninta ya sauya, fuskarnan ta kunbura, sannu sukai mata Inda daddy kuma ya matso gurinta tareda kama duka hannayenta.


Daddyn Aliy ina take? Tanacan psychiatric, meyasa xaku kaita can? Wlh nasanta itace matar da nake baka labarinta Wanda naso ka taimaka mata, itace wacce nake baka labarin an wayi gari a asibiti ta gudu, itace wacce nasaka kabiya duka bills dinta lokacinda ake cikiyar Wanda yasan daga inda take, taqara maganar nata da kuka.

Jinjina kai yayi irin tasu ta manya, Abba ne yadafa kafadarsa tareda cewa yakamata Ku fahimtar damu abunda yake faruwa don kuwa kanmu acikin duhu yake.

 Daddy ne yafara yimusu bayanin iyakan abunda yasani gameda matar , don Mummy kam kuka yaci qarfinta, bayan yagama ne  yajuya gurin mummy yatambayeta ya sunan matar? Tsagaita kukanta tayi snn tace wlh baxan iya tunawa ba, don Allah mijina kataimakeni karka barta a gidan mahaukata ni na amince xan rayu da ita har xua lokacinda xata samu waraka. Ina da tabbacin labarinta bamai dadi bane abun tausayi ce ita.


Shidai daddy dagalo yayi yana kallon mummy yanda taketa arattako bayanai cikin salon magiya da Neman taimako, can ya nisa snn yace.

Khadija yanxu hukuncin nan ba a hanuna yakeba, baxan iya barinta a gidana tareda iyalai na, tana cikin wnn halinba, don xata iya aikata komai ciki kuwa harda kisan kai, tunda mahaukaciya ce, sai abunda likitoci sukace indai har akwai taimakon da xasu iya bamu mu rayu tare da ita to shknn, alqawari daya xan iya miki shine xan tsaya tsayin Daka akan lafiyarta.


Bahaka mummy tasoba,amma hakanma yamata dadi nan tafara qoqarin share hawayenta murmushi yayi mata tareda cewa kinga yanda kikayi kuwa wlh kincanxa harwani qaton baki kikayi, inbaki sabeba dawuri aure xan qara, dariya sukasa gaba dayansu don sanin Halin Daddy duk abunda akeyi baya hanashi taba halin nan nasa na barkwanci.


Haka suka fito suka qara nufan Psychiatric din atare , ta hanyar da likitan yanuna musu, tanan sukabi suka shiga har cikin office dinnashi........

Fitowa ta.2⃣8⃣

Lami tafiya takeyi hajaran majaran kai tsaye tayi gidan Baffan  Sadiyah Wanda baxata iya tuno rabonta da wnn gidanba, kai rabonta da ganinsu ma tun wani lokaci dayaxo mata fada akan halinda Sadiya takeyi, aiko batai qasa a guiwa gurin baxa mishi rashin mutunci kala kala ba, har ta Tara mishi mutane, kunya ta addabeshi a qarshe yabar gurin.

Sallama tayi matar baffan Sadiya mamaki ne yahanata fadin komai tun bayan amsa sallamar tata, haka tamiqe jiki ba kwari tadauko mata kujera don tasan ba lafiya ne xaisa kaga Lami cikin wnn halin ba.

Xama tayi nan itama tadauko wata kujerar ta ajiye kusa da natan, gaisar da Lami tafarayi amma bata iya amsawa ba saima kukan da tasake.

Meke faruwane Lami kisanar dani mana, amma sai kuka kikeyi, Ganin da tayi batada niyyar magana ne yasata miqewa tayi cikin dakinta da hanxari, can saigata ta fito tareda mijinta..

Cikin sassarfa ya iso gurin Lami yafara tambayanta meke faruwa, cikin kuka tafara labarta mishi abunda ke tafe da ita, baitsaya jin qarashen xancen nata ba yakoma daki yaxanxo kayan jikinsa yafito tareda cemata tashi muje.

A kan Babur dinsa suka taho basu Tarar da kowa a gidanba, gashi yanda tabar Sadiya a tsakar gidan haka tadawo ta sameta, rige rige sukayi gurin isa gareta, shine yafara tattaba jikinta can yaa dago tareda furta kalman Innalillahi wa inna'ilaihirraju'un, kalman ce taqara dagawa Lami hankali, inda batai qasa a guiwa ba tahau kururuwa tareda birgima a qasa kamar qaramar yarinya.

Baibi takanta ba, don yasan idan yace xai tsaya bata haquri ne topa tabbas baxai je suyi abunda ya dace ba, yana fitowa qofar gidan nasu yahadu da Isah Wanda shine shigowarshi garin.

Ganin baffan nashi ya matuqar bashi al'ajabi cikin Sauri yaqarasa ya durqusa da nufin gaidashi, dagoshi yayi cikin sanyin jiki, don kuwa ganin da yayi mishi yasan baisan abunda yake faruwa ba, hannu yamiqa mishi sukai musabaha da juna.

Baffan ne yafara magana, saidai yayi mishi nasiha sosae kapin daga qarshe yasanar dashi halin da ake ciki, duk qoqarin da yayi don ganin yanuna jarumtarsa saida abun ya gagareshi, baisan lokacin da ya kece da kuka ba, bakukan komai yakeyi ba, sai kukan halinda Sadiya tamutu akai don yanada masaniya akan komai, sau da dama yakanyi qoqarin nusar da ita abunda fa takeyi dinnan baida fa'ida, ita kanta takanyi kuka snn tace mishi itama tanajin takaicin rayuwarda ta tsinci kanta aciki, snn takasa dainawa koda tayi yunqurin hakan saitaji baxata iyaba.

Dakyar yasamu ya rarasheshi inda yasanar dashi cewar xaije gidane domin neman sutura, snn xai turo matarsa axo maganan wanka, xaikuma sanar da duk Wanda yadace, haka sukai sallama tareda ajiye lokacin jana'ixa.


Gidansu yawuce don shi koda yaxo baya sauqa gidan mmn nashi, saidai idan gari yawaye yaxo ya gaisheta, duk dama duk xuwanshi basa wanyewa lpy, tunda bayabin abunda take shimfida masa, donshi gaxkiya yake gaya mata, itakuma bataso.


Ayashe kuwa gidanda ta tabbatar xata samu Alhaji nuhu tanufa. Takuma ci sa'a tasameshi yana ganinta yahau xuba mata kirari kamar kullum itako ta tsume kunsan ance Tsare gida mutuncin dandi😜.

 Dakatar dashi tayi dacewa da Allah ya isheni rufemin baki mayaudarin Banxa, harni xaka yaudara kagama mantawa da uban kudaden da nake binka, kamanta halaccin da nai maka nakawo ma qananun yara kana moruwa dasu, duk da nasan sauran wa wasu kake kaiwa su mora, amma ai ba banxa kake kaimusu ba, tunda suna biyanka, xaka iya tuna yaushe rabonka daka sallameni?

Sanin da yayi mata na idan tana magana ba'a katseta, zunzurutun bala'in ta da iya tijara yasa baiyi yunqurin tsaidata ba harsanda yabari takai Aya, snn yafara bata haquri cikin dadin baki yafara cemata wlh inada niyyar cikin satinnan in hada miki kudaden ki in aika miki, tunda kinyi aure kinga haduwarmu baxai yiwu yanda mukeso ba, najima wai mijin Lami kika aura ko? Gaxkiya mutumiyata vakida kirki.


Jin labarin kudi ya gifta ne yasata sakin fuska tareda fuskantarsa dakyau tace mishi cikin murya qasa qasa, duka dai ba wnn ne kefe dani ba, amma shidinma dama inada niyyar zuwan maka, amma yanxu abunda yakawo ni shine Sadiya fa tamutu.

Tamutu😳??? Yafada bangane tamutu ba, meya faru subhanallah, xakacemin bakasan da hakan ba? Ta katseshi, wlh bansaniba rabona da Sadiya yau sati daya knn tunda narakata Kano gurin wani Alhaji kinga mani kintuna min domin bai biyani  kudina ba amma bari innemeshi inji.

 

Kiran wayarsa yayi baijima ba yadauka, gaisawa sukayi sama sama snn ya tambayeshi lbrn Sadiya kamar baisan abunda yake faruwa ba, aiko nan yacemishi Sadiya tamutu saboda na'ajiyeta a gidana muna hutawa tare don kawai natafi Abuja meeting da niyyar kwana amma kwanan bai yiwuba nadawo kawai na tarar da Sadiya da maigadin gidana! 


Abun ya girgizani snn yabani takaici, saboda meye xata hadani da maigadi na kuma a shimfidana? Jaraban Sadiya yajima daman yana qonamin rai don bantaba haduwa da macenda na kwanta da ita batai kuka ba sai Sadiya, ina haquri da itane don tasan kan harka. don inada tabbacin yana dauke da cuta.


Shiyasa nasa yarana sukai musu Dan banxan duka, cikin rashin sa'a yaran nawa sukaita amfani da ita Wanda yakai munzalin bata iya motsi, nasasu sukaita asibiti Ashe basukaita ba washe gari suka sanar dani tamutu amma tabasu lbrn gidansu akan sukaita gurin mamanta, yaqarasa maganar da Alamun rashin damuwa a tare dashi tamkar kiyashi ne yamutu a hannunsa ba Dan Adam ba.

Bayan yagama fadar hakan ne a qarshe yacemishi xan 2ro maka kudin aikinka snn xan 2ro maka kudin da xakabawa uwar dakinnata yana kai nan a maganar nashi yayi diff yakashe wayarsa.


Dayake a hands free yasa wayar shiyasa babu amfanin  tsayuw yimata bayani tunda taji komai da kunnenta, Tabdi jam Abu baiyi dadi ba wlh bari intafi gida, nasan jama'a xasu taru bawuya amin sata, amma ka tabbatar anayin uku katahomin da duk  kudadena karka bari inqara xuwa gidanka haduwarmu baxatayi kyau ba. Tana gama fadin haka tajuya ta tafi abynta.


Shikuwa baffn Sadiya 'yan uwanshi yasanarwa gaba daya, kan kace kobo gida yacika, nan aka samu masu ilimin cikin sukaiwa Sadiya wanka, lokacinda akaxo yimata wanka sun dauraye mata gabanta yafi sau a qirga, amma baidaina fitarda wani ruwa Mae yauqi hade da wari atattare dashi, suna tottoshe hanci haka dai aqarshe suka barta da katanta suka suturtata, aka fitar da ita.


Xanso Ku qiyastawa kanku irin haukan da Lami tayi, dakyar aka daddanneta abunka da jahila, babu ilimin da xataiwa 'yar tata tilo addu'a, idan sukaga kamar ta haqura akadan saketa saita deba aguje tayi qofar gida, Abunda yay ta qona ran Isah knn.

Anyiwa Sadiya sallah snn aka miqata gidanta na gaxkiya kuma madauwamiya...

Fitowa ta.2⃣9⃣


Sallama yayi musu snn yamiqawa daddy da Abba hannu suakai musabiha, suka kuma gaisa dasu ummy snn ya Samu kujerar sa yayi yakame yafara koro musu jawabinsa cikin nitsuwa kallo daya xakai mishi kasan Hutu dakuma ilimi yasamu maxauni a tattare dashi.

Amm abun da nakeson sanar daku shine a gaxkiya a halin yanxu baxan iya cewa komai akan cutar taba, don kaina ya matuqar daurewa akan ciwon natan, tunda nayi iya binciken da xanyi, bance muku shine qarshen bincike ba, domin duk wayewan gari ilimi buduwa takeyi, amma a halin yanxu gaxkiya baxance komai akan ta ba, don banga matsala a tattare da kwakwalwarta ba.


Babu matsalar komai acikin kanta, amma kuma tana hauka tuquru,Wanda yana qaruwane akan ta duk kwanan duniya, xanso kubarta anan kodon cikinda yake jikinta, insha Allah xamu bata kulawa matuqa gurin ganin abunda  ke cikinta bai illatu ba, saboda idan mu kace xamu daurata akan magungunan su ne to tabbas xai shafi lafiyar abunda ke cikinta, snn bayan ta haihu xamusan wani mataki xa'a dauka akai.

Sun gamsu sun kuma yi na'am da shawarwarinsa, duk da hakan baiyiwa mummy dadi ba, tanada burin taimakon zainab don jininsu ya hadu, tajima tana fatan haduwarsu sai gashi sun hadu bacikin dadi ba, Allah yabaki lpy tafada a fili, duka gurin aka amsa mata da Amin.

Sun nemi su ganta, shiya jagorancesu har dakinda take, samunta sukayi ta kamo qafafunta sama tana son cixan tafin qafanta, tagagara amma tanace sai tayi a qarshe kallon gefenta tayi takurma ihu tana fadin menai maka, taxube agurin jikake gibbb tafadi kannan yabugu da simintin dakin, kasa daurewa mummy tayi tafashe da kuka gami da barin gurin da gudu tanufi wajen asibitin.

Ummy ma da guntun hawayenta suka rufa mata baya, mota suka shiga suka nufi gida, tunda suka shiga cikin motar babu Wanda yasamu qarfin guiwar yin magana, sheahsheqan kukan mummy kawai kakeji, har suka isa gida kowa yanufi sashensa.

Har akayi ukun rasuwan Sadiya Mal.Habu baiyiwa Lami ta'aziyya ba abun yana damunta duk dama rabonta da ganisa tun ranar da akayi rasuwan da yamma tafito xata xaga bayan gida, don Ayashe bata tanka tana tasan kuma don tana ganin idanun jama'a ne shiyasa.

Tafito shikuma zaishiga dakin Ayashe ko kallon tsiya baiyi mata ba, balle kallon arziki, haka ta wuce bandakin, ita kanta yanxu baxata iya cewa ga halinda take ciki ba.

A bangaren Nana kuwa tayi kukan Rashin Sadiya hartaji babu dadi, don Sadiya ce kadai ke taimakonta dakuma sonta tsakani da Allah, don xuwa yanxu tafara gano manufar soyayyar da Ayashen take nunawa a gareta ammadai bata gama tabbatarwa ba.

_To ko menene_


Tunda aka watse kuwa rayuwar tagama qulleta, don kuwa Ayashe taharass tabbatar mata batada girki, bata kuma bata abinci kamar yanda tayi alqawari, abunda yataimaketa daya taci darajan 'yan canjunan da danta yabar mata bai qareba dashi take samu takecin abinci, ganinda tayi kudin xaiqare dawuri yasata yanke shawaran saro kayan miya tafara kasawa.
      _Duniya juyi juyi wai kwado yafada ruwan zafi_



Ayashe tunda ta kyallara idanunta taga kayan miyan Lami tace mata saidai taxabi daya, kodai tadaina sana'ar saboda masu saya suna damunta da sallama kuma lokacin yawanci lokacin baccinta ne, don haka kota daina kokuma takoma gidanta tacigaba da sana'ar.

Lami abun yaqona mata rai, taso nuna cewar baxatabi umarninta ba wnn karon amma ganin tana Neman kore mata kasuwa yasata  ji aranta gara ta tafin hummmm amma ita tasan meta qulla mata.

Haka tanaji tana gani tadan tarkata Dan Abunda yarage mata tafara kaiwa gidanta, saida tayi shara don gaba daya gurin yakoma kufayi, har gurin Yamma tilis snn tagama aikace aikacenta taxo deban kayan sawanta, amma me xata gani, kayanta tasamu. Abakin dakin koda taleqa dakin kaji tagani masu yawa aqalla xasukai guda Dari biyu da alama shine ankawosu don basu gama sakewa da gurin ba.


Wani munduqun baqin ciki ne da qunci yataru ya maqale mata a wuya, shibai fito ba, kuma shibai koma ciki ba, gashinan dai ya tokare mata qirji Ni Lami wann rayuwar quncin da nake ciki dame tai kama batasan a sarari tai maganan ba saida taji Ayashe A bayanta tana magana.


Ai Lami tunda kika wulaqantani kika kuma nemi ki toxartani, to Kadan ma kika gani daga sharrina dakuma kaidina, munyi zama dake bana wasa ba, amma har muka watse zaman mu baki gama Sanina ba,  in Ayashe tanuna kanta, xanxo gidanki don kinacin Abu kidaure fuska, innemi alfarmar ki ta kudi kigayamin baqar magana, a qarshe ma kicimin mutunci kinmanta nice silar shiganki duk wata duniyar dadi.


Xan qara nanata miki ni shugabanki ne a hatsabibanci, namiki nisanda baxaki kamoni ba, kinadai gani tun tafiyar batai nisa ba, ci dasha kai harma da mijin ya gagareki ko?

Kibini a sannu karkiyi kuskuren shiga gonata don haduwarmu gaba da gaba baxaiyi ma'ana ba inkunne yaji gangan jiki ya tsira.

Daga wnn maganar bata qara da komai ba tabarta agurin da sakekken baki, xuciyarta na tunxurata amma bakinta baida ikon da xai iya furta mata magana makamanciyar na ramuwa agareta, haka tabita da kallo a qarshe kuma ta tsuguna a gurin ta kwashe komatsanta ta fita dashi, gashidai babu fada balle saki amma gidan mijinta ya gagareta.

_BAYAN WATA HUDU_

Haka rayuwar taita tafiya wasu agaresu Zuma, wasuko a garesa madaci ma yafishi dandano don xuwa yanxu  Lami tagama tabbatarwa tarasa *TUDUN DAFAWA* gashi jigon rayuwar nata wato Isah yayi fushi da ita, tunda aka watse a makoki baiqara waiwayenta ba, tamkar abun asiri fitanannen jarababben son Mal.Babu ne yake azalzalan zuciyarta harbatasan yanda xatayi dashiba.


Sau da dama takan leqa qofar gida kawai don taga wucewarsa, don kwatakwata Ayashe tama hanata cigaba da shiga gidanne, don tun watarana dataje domin Neman alfarman Mijinta don batada abincin ci.

HHaka Ayashe tafito ta tsigaleta tatas tanajin duriyarshi a ciki amma dagashi har ita bamai ikon tankawa Dan uwansa, a qarshe kuma tace mata karta qara tako mata gidanta, ita xataxo kuma ta tabbatar randa ta ganta a gidanta to takaddan sakinta takawo mata tana gama fadar hakan ta hankada ta daga qofar dakin. Daga ranar bata qara yunqurin shigaba saidai leqe..


*****************
A bangaren su mummy kuwa iya kulawa suna bawa Mama don kullum Mummy saitaje itada Ummy, amma yanxu itakadai takexua don Ummy tayi nauyi sosae, dasafe direba yaje yadubota dayamma kuma mummy taje, saboda yanxu tayi nauyi sosae.

Haka kullum intaje da kuka take dawowa , saboda wani lokacin idan abun yamotsa tayita burgima qasa kokuma taita dukan cikinta, abundai babu dadin kallo.

Amma yanxu ankaita bangaren masu tsofin ciki don samun cikakken kulawa.

Allah dai auke fatan yaraba ta wnn cikin lpy.


*WATA SAFIYA*

Suntashi lpy amma banda Ummy domin wnn ranar tatashi da naquda kuma yaxoda gardama har yakaita ga matakin sai an cire mata abunda yake cikinnata.

Alhmdllh anshiga da ita lpy Ankuma fito lpy India aka Ciro mata Sambalelen yaronta mai kamada IBBY sak, amma mahaifinta ya stage har sanda akai mata dinki, tasamu matsalar da mahaifanta baxai qara samun qarfin daukan wani cikin ba..

Ansallameta da kwana biyu mummy tayanke shawaram xua duba Mama domin kwananta uku batajeba, haka tashirya ta nufi asibitin amma tanaxuwa ta samu...............

Fitowa ta. 3⃣0⃣


Mummy tana xuwa asibitin ta iske Mama acikin halin naquda, ankuma tabbatar mata tun jiya tafara, sai naqudar ta taso gadan gadan sai bakin mahaifar yarufu jin bayanansu ne yasata saurin xuwa office din likitan domin jin menene mafitan al'amarin.

Tashiga office dinshi tasamu yana ganawa da wasu aciki don dole tasauna a parlour domin jiransu, mijinta taiwa waya tasanar dashi halinda ake ciki, yace mata yana tafe bada jimawa ba.


Tajima agurin axaune har ranta yafara sosuwa, can saiga Alhaji mukhtdin, isowarshi yayi dai2 da fitowar mutanen da suke cikin office din don batareda jiran komai ba yayi mata alama da hannunsa akan tabiyoshi kamar xataci da qasa tamiqe suka shiga ciki.

Bayan yagama rubuce rubucen takaddun dayake gabansa ne yadago yadubesu, suka amsa cikin sakin fuska, saboda xua yanxu sun gama sabawa.

Wato gaxkiya mungama iya yinmu, to kunsan lamarin masu irin wnn larurar sai ahankali, dama inajiran isowarku ne, nidai anawa da'barta ta haihu dakanta gara kawai acire mata, tahuta abun cikin nata ma yasamu hutu,  kar asamu matsala gurin haihuwan, don idan baxaku manta ba a baya namuku bayanin irin cutan nata kamar gaba yakeyi.


Sun gamsu sun kuma yi na'am da bayanansa, atake aka bawa daddy takaddu yasaka hannu akai, snn yaje yagama biyan duk abunda yadace ace anyi, ba qaramin gwagwarmaya akasha ba kapin akai mata alluran bacci, nanma tadauki Rabin Awa tana birgima, inda duk Wanda yaganta, indai akwai digon imani a xuciyarka saika tausaya mata, can dai aka samu baccin yaci qarfinta, aka Shiga da ita theatre room.


*Bayan Awa Daya*


Safa da marwa take tayi agurin tanata addu'oi daddy tun yana ce mata taxauna har yagaji ya zuba mata idanu, juyowan da xatayi taga anbude dakin, cikin azama tayi gurinsu da sassarfa, Nurse ce tamiqo mata baby wacce take nannade cikin kyakykyawan towel.

Cikin hanxari takarba a hannunta, Nurse din cikin murmushi tace mata Alhmdllh munsamu baby girl, Mummy Sam bakinta yaqi rufuwa.

Sai nanatawa takeyi _FATABARAKALLAHU AHSANAL KHALIQIN_  tana juya yarinyan, shikuwa daddy sai binta yakeyi da murmushi can dai yakasa jurewa yace mata, habawa mmn Yarinya aiya kamata kiymin kawaici kibani inganta ko?

 Da kunya a kan fuskanta tanufi gurinshi tamiqa mishi yarinyan tareda bude mishi fuskanta, Kai Tabarakallah! Yafada tareda fadada fara'anshi, jaririya CE amma kallo daya xakai mata, kai tsaye zaka kirata da kyakykyawa, haka sukaita kallonta batareda gajiyawa ba.

Rufeta kar iska ya shigeta tafada mishi tareda Jan towel din tarufe mata fuska, tooo tundaga yau anfara min rowanta? Bahaka bane tafada gamida karban yarinyar a hannunsa tanamai dariya ciki ciki.

Suna zaune a gurin aka gingurota aka kaita Side room nacan ward dinsu, binta sukayi har sanda sukaga an kwantar da ita, Mummy ce tamatsa kusa da ita tareda yimata addu'oi ta tofa mata snn suka nufi office da yarinyan.


Sun sameshi da batun yaxa'a barma uwar yarinyan, ta hanyarda baxata illata ta, saida ya gyara glass din fuskarsa snn yafara magana.

A bincikena abunda nalura dashi shine shayarda ita xai iya zama illa ga yarinyan, tunda kunga halin da take ciki, though madai kunsan ko mahaukaci  yanason dansa, amma ta shayarwa ana iya shan Abu da dama, ciki kuwa harda hauka, koda batayi haukan  wani abun xai iya samunta wanda baxamuy fatan faruwan hakan ba, duk da haryanxu bamusan yanayin haukan nata ba.


Yajima yana musu bayanai akan matsalan aqarshe dai suka sanar mishi sunada Wanda xata shayarmusu da ita, kapin tawarke afara nema mata magani, da wnn sukai sallama dajuna, suka fito suka Shiga mota sukaiyo gida.


Sashin su Ummy suka fara Shiva, sunci sa'a kuwa Alhajinma yana gida, gaisawa sukai da juna  tareda sanar dasu qaruwan da aka samu,  sunyi matuqar farin ciki nan sukaita karban yarinyan suna yaba kyanta, said a surutunsu ya lafa ne snn daddy yaroqi Abba alfarman da sukaxo Nema agurinsu.

Ran Abba yabaci indai yayi magana cikin qunan xuciya, haba Mukhtar meyasa xakace kaxo Neman alfarma agurina? Atunaninka bakaji cewar kafi qarfin alfarma ba? Bakaji kamar tsakanina dakai babu alfarma ba? Banji dadin wnn abun ba kwata kwata.

Daddy ne yabashi haquri tareda jaddada mishi cewa Hakkine a roqi Ummy domin itace xatai aikin tunda shayarwa ba Abu bane me sauqi ba, don haka wajibi ne  aroqeta,.

Ummy ko qasa tai dakanta snn race wlh babu damuwa Insha Allah xata riqeta da Amana, tana fatan sutayata da addu'a Allah yataya ta. Suka Amsa da Amin gaba dayansu.


Daddy ne yakatsesu da cewa inada magana, hankalinsu suka tattaro gaba data suka miqa gareshi saida yayi gyaran murya wnn yace, Ummy banason Iran kina shayar da yarinyar nan kina kallon kwayar idonta, saboda banason tashaqu da kowa sama da matata yafada cikin barkwanci.

Gabadayansu sukasa dariya bawanda kuma ya tanka mishi don Sanin halinsa, miqewa su mummy sukayi da Ummy duka Shiga daki, Abakin gado Ummy taxauna riqe da Babyn itakuma Mummy tanufi gadon da jaririyar Ummy take kwance.


Ummy yanaga 'yar taki saiqara qiba da kyau takeyi? Kedai nononki yanada kyau wlh murmushi kawai Ummy taimata tafara gwadawa yarinyan nono. Allah yasa nima tawa hartafi takin ma qiban, to shknn Amin tunda abun son kai ne.


Ta gwada mata nono karo nabiyar kenan duka biyun, amma fafur taqi karba, tayi mamakin abun a qarshe dai ta ankarar da mummy, aiko taku daya tayi na biyu ta isosu tana zaro idanu waje.

Qara gwada mata ingani, Ummy ce tasake kaimata nonon baki a karo na shida nanma bata karba ba, kuma kallo daya xakai mata kasan tanajin yunwa don yanda taketa qoqarin kai hannunta bakinta, a qarshe ma sai kuka data sake.

Mummy cikin hanzari ta karbi yarinyar tareda cewa, Ummy taso muje musanar da su halinda ake ciki, fitowa falon sujayi jiki duk a sanyaye suka sanar dasu abunda yake faruwa, shiru sukayi gabadaya, sai xuwa can Abba yace, badamua xa'a koma asibiti asanar da likita, domin yagaya musu irin madaran da yakamata abata.


To tashi mutafi mummy tafada cikin zaquwa don har cikin zuciyarta takejin kukan diyartata. Bai bata lokaci ba yamiqe harda Abban suka fita domin xua asibitin.


*****************
Mal Habu ne zaune agaban shagonsa gaba daya abun duniya tadameshi, takuma yimishi atishawar tsaki, don yanxu shagonnasa tamkar kufayi haka takoma, kaya qalilan ne aciki.

Banda uban basussukan da mutane ke binshi, don xuwa yanxu Ayashe ce ke tsara komai na gidan,  bashida tacewa haka xataita tsara komai shidai nashi idanu kawai.


Gashi ko acikin xuciyarshi baxai iya musanta mata ba, balle fatar bakinshi tayi garajen furtawa, tadai Sanar dashi cewar duk kudaden da take karba a gurinshi tana yimishi tanadin wasu kayayyaki ne Wanda takeson yafara Sabin  kasuwanci dasu, shidai baice komai ba,  tunda ba shawaranshi ta nema ba.


Girki kuwa dama tun xuwanta gidan sau biyu tayi tareda shi, kuma takafa dokan Indai xai koma gida da dare daga kasuwa tofa xai tafi musu da abunda xasuci da dare.


A halinda yake ciki harya sallami yaron shagonsa, saboda bashida zarafin sallamarsa kullum kamar yanda akewa kowani yaron shago.

*****
Nana ce Zaune a qofar daki tanaqara mamakin rayuwar Ayashe, dukda takasa yarda da irin son da take matan ba, ammaa duk dahaka tana halacci, tunda bata barinta da yunwa snn tana barinta taje makaranta, duk dama watarana idan tafita xataje makaranta Lami takan kiranyeta ta daura mata kayan kolin da take saidawa, tareda jaddada mata karta kuskura tabari Ayashe tasani.

Kamar kullum yauma saida takirata takarbi Allonta ta ajiye a gefe, snn tadora mata tareda cewa Wlh karki kuskura kiymin ciniki kadan, kika dawomin da kaya dayawa na lahira saiyafiki jin dadi, shegiya ta dungure mata kai kamar xata kife, tafito tana hawaye, fitowarta yayi dai dai da........

Fitowa ta. 3⃣1⃣


Fitowar Nana yayi dai dai da sauqan Ayashe daga cikin adaidaita dawowarta knn daga yawon gantalinta, Xaro idanu tayi ganin Nana tafito daga gidan Lami kuma dauke da tiren kayan talla a kanta.

Itako  Nana daburcewa tayi ganin Ayashe ta ganta, tama rasa yaxatayi takoma cikin  gidan ba itaba, ta tsaya ba itaba kawai sai ta daskare a gurin.

Ayashen ne tasallami me adaidaita ta qaraso gareta, tareda ce Mata nasan me hakan yake nufi amma baxai hanani tambayarki ba, ina nabarki xaki tafi, Tallan waye, snn tun yaushe kikeyi?

 Har idanunta yaciko da hawaye bakinta yana rawa tace, Makaranta kika barni xan tafi daga haka ta fashe da kuka takasa qarasawa hakanne ya qara tunxura Ayashe, ta Hankada ta suka koma cikin gidan Lami din.

Samunta sukayi a zaune tana gyaran ganyen miya, jitayi mutum ya fado kanta dago idanu tayi taga Ayashe ce ta hankado Nana kanta, cikin zafin nama itama ta hankade Nana saida ta  FASA  bakinta a qasa  ihun azaba ta kurma.

Ayashe kuwa cikin kururuwa da azalzala tafara yiwa Lami magana, Ashe bakida hankali Lami, saboda ke jahilace bakisan dadin ilimi ba shine xaki hana 'yar mutane jin dadinsa? To wlh baki isaba, yanda na haramtamiki shiga tsabgar mijini to wlh haka 'yarsa ba kar inkuma ji ko gani.


Daga haka tajuya tafara tafiya harta kai qofa tajuyo tai mata kallon wulaqanci snn tace nasan kina magana a xuciyarki, xaki kuma so kiga kin amayar dashi hahahahaha tayi dariyan qeta, ,saida tayi me isarta snn ta tsagaita tace Lami kinyi kuskuren karawa dani amma kiyi haquri kiqaramin lokaci xanbaki mijinki alokacin da nagama tabbatar da bashida mamora a tare dashi ko kadan. Daga haka tayi waje abunta.

Kamar wucewar walqiya haka Nana tafita a gidan, tarufawa Ayashe baya, don tasani da wani baqiqqirin din aigara baqi baqi, kobanxa Ayashe bata dukanta.


Lami kuwa Kamar tabita ta shaqeta haka takeji, tarasa meke mata dadi a rayuwar ta, gaxkiya wulaqancin yayi yawa tafada a fili, miqewa tayi da nufin xuwa takawo qarshen wnn cin mutuncin da Ayashen take mata, tana fitowa kuwa tasamu isowar Mal.Habu kenan gidan, ai batasan lokacinda ta shagala da kallonsa ba, tamkar yau tafara ganinsa, wata xuciyar kuma tana raya mata Kamar takamo hannunsa tashigar dashi gidanta amma babu dama.

Tana tsaye a gurin tana saqa da warwara, jitayi muryan Ayashe yadoki dodon kunnenta takuma tabbatar da cewa fitowa waje takeda niyyar yi, ai batasan lokacinda ta antaya cikin gidan ta hartana cin tuntube😜gaba da gabanta raqumi wan doki.


***************
Bayan kwana uku Mummy tana xaune da diyarta a cinyarta daddy yashigo, dagowa tayi ta kalleshi da murmushi akan fuskarta, shi abun har mamaki yake bashi, kullum cikin fara'a take tun samuwar yarinyan nan.


Zama yayi a gefenta tareda cewa kinsan meye sunan babynki? Dasauri tadago ta kalleshi tace bansaniba nayi iya kawaici tun lokacinda na tabbatar kamata huduba amma nadaure ban tambayeka ba jnajiran kasanar dani, duk dama yaukam haquri na ya gaxa inasa ran xan sameka intambayeka.

Dariya yayi mata snn yace baxan Sanar dake kai tsaye ba, xanbaki  dai satar amsa akai idan kinci shknn idan bakici ba kuma saiki jira ranar suna in sanar dake yafada da zolaya.


Bata kulashi ba tadaiyi shiru tana sauraronshi, xuwa can yafara magana.


Sunan 'yarki sunane da babu kamarsa, sunan 'yarki babu acikin sunaye Wanda yakaishi yawan inkiya, sunan 'yarki Haske ne Wanda kowa yasan yana haskawa yayi shiru yace nabarki haka don nasan idanna cigaba da koro miki yanayin sunan babu wuya xaki ganoshi cikin sauqi, xuwa yamma inason kifadamin sunan 'yarki. Yana kaiwa nan a zabcen yatashi yamatsa kusa da ita yabude yarinyan yafara binta da idanu kamar dai yanda yasaba kullum kyanta sai qara fitowa yakeyi yafada a fili.

Murmushi mummy tayi tareda cewa daddy yakamata kafin kawuce gurin aiki mu Shiga asibiti muduba jikin Baiwar Allah nnan, Gaxkiyarta kam kitashi Ku shirya Donni a shirye nake, inajiranku a falo.



Tashi tayi xuciyarta cunkushe da tunani kala kala to meye sunan yarinyar nan? Kai subhanallah wnn miji nawa halinsa baxai sauya ba wlh, haka tayita sukur sukur basu tashi sun shirya ba, saida taji ya kwala mata kira snn tafara shirinta cikin sauri, tashirya diyarta tsaf ta nadota cikin towel me shegen kyau suka fito cikin Shiga ta alfarma dukansu suka nufi mota sai Asibiti.


Ranar suna a gidan Ummy yarinya taci suna A'isha anmata inkiya da *Humairah* agidan suna anata shagali kowa mummy tasamu acikin qawayensu saita Dan kebance dashi domin yimai tambayar sunan 'yarta cikin hikima amma bata samuba, Aqarshe tasamu kebancewa da qanwar mahaifiyar Ummy bayan ta koro mata bayani ne, itakuma tayi murmushi tace.


Kinbani mamaki khadija Saikace bakiy karatun addini ba sunan 'yarki ai yagama bayyana miki, sunan 'yarki..........

Fitowa ta. 3⃣2⃣

Sunan 'yarki Fadimatul Zahra'u mummy dajin haka ta kalleta da farinciki marar misaltuwa, tareda cewa Zahra sunan diyata? Alhmdllh Allah baxan gushe ba ina qara gode maka, inaxuwa tafada gamida miqewa bata jira cewar hajiyarba tafito a gidan dauke da 'yarta tayi side dinta da sauri xuciyarta fal da farin ciki.


A falonta tasameshi yayi dai daya yana waya da dansa yana ganinta yacemishi to Son ga Mummyn taka ta shigo yafada yana miqa mata wayar, saida ta shimfidar da 'yarta snn takarbi wayar, tafara magana My son ykk? Magana yafara yimata cikin shagwaba.

Mummy tun lokacinda kikasanar min Kinsamu yarinya kika rage nunamin kulawa yanxu a rana baifi muyi waya dake sau biyar ba, yafada yana qara shigar da muryan nashi cikin sigar shagwaba kamar Wanda xai fashe da kuka.

Dariya tayi kapin tace Haba dearest bahaka bane kodayake duk yanda xanmaka bayanin reno baxaka fahimta ba, fatana kawai Allah yakawoka watarana kaga yanda dawainiyar yake da idanunka, baiyi magana ba hakan yabata damar cigaba da magananta, Son na2ra maka hoton baby na ta EMail dinka amma har yanxu baka nunamin alamar kabude ba, balle insa ran xakai commenting akai, kana tunanin ka kyauta kenan.


Dajin hakan yafara bata haquri don bayason abunda xai Sosa ranta ko Kadan a rayuwarsa, saida ya tabbatar ta huce snn Yakoma salonsa yace mata Mummy' amsawa tayi snn yacigaba wlh yunwa nakeji, cikin Sauri tace bakaci abinciba Haidar haryanxu meya faru, meye dalilin hakan? Mummy ni kawai girkinki nakeson ci, dariya tayi don tagank yau rigimarce ta motsa.

Don haka ta shirya yimishi yanda tasaba yimishi, cikin lallabi tasashi yaje gurin cin Abinci nan yaqara kiranta tafara yimishi hira yanacin abincin yana dariya har ya kammala, aqarshe sukai bankwana,, bayan takashe wayar ne ta dafe kanta🤦🏽‍♀ oh haidar rigima, kukan 'yarta ne yadawo da ita aiko tamiqe dasauri ta qarasa gareta, tadauketa tanufi ma'ajiyar abincinta da ita.


*RANAR SUNA*
Fadin irin hidimomi da kuma barnan kudin da akayi a taron sunan _Zahra_ bata lokaci ne, baxaka taba cewa ba iyayenta bane saboda gata kam annuna mata, kuma baxaka taba fahimtar ba Nono takesha ba dalili kuwa yanda jikinta yy kyau baxaka ga rashin npnon yanuna ajikinta ba.

Idan kaganta xakai 2nanin tayi wata saboda yanda tai qiba kyanta yaqara fitowa a sarari, idan aka sanar dakai sunanta xaka tabbatar da ta cancanci wnn sunan *Zahra tauraruwa*.

**************

*Bayan shekara biyar*

Umma nagama shirin tafada tana rabe a gefen dakin, Ayashe ce tafito taganta tafa mata tafarayi snn tadora da cewa Gaxkiya Nana saiyau nasan kindaura damaran farantamin, shekara guda nawa ina fama dake saiyau kika amince, indai kikaimin yanda nake so wlh namiki alqawarin baxaki jimaba xanmiki auren gata kije gidanki ki huta ko?

Ke barima kiji ni nan nasan yanda xanyi mahaifiyarki ta dawo gareki muddin xaki kawomin kudi, kinadai ganin babanki ya tsiyace yanxu kam bashida komai abincima nike Sayan mana,
Hawayen da yake taruwa a idonta ne yasamu damar kwaranyowa kamar anbude fanfo, saboda ita Sam takasa amincewa har cikin xuciyarta xatayi abunda umman takeso, yanxun ma ta amsa mata ne sakamakon uquba da horon yunwar da take mata, gashi batada mafita.


Kai jimin sakarcin banxa Ayashe tafada cikin fada, babu dole fa yanxunma idan baki amince ba, xaki iya tafoya kiyi xamanki babu abunda yashamin kai tayi tsaki tareda qoqarin komawa cikin dakinta.

Cikin sauri da muryan kuka tace kiyi haquri Umma na Amince tafada muryanta na rawa, Komawa ciki Ayashe tayi tadauko mayafinta snn tace mata muje.

Fita sukayi suka nufi bakin hanya tamkar kurame don babu maiyiwa Dan uwansa magana har suka shiga adaidaita Nana addu'oi taketa nanatawa cikin Ranta fadi takeyi Ya Allah kaji qaina, Allah Kaine shaidana banason saba maka, Allah kaimin agaji, ka tausayamin ka kawomin dauki cikin gaggawa.

Inda itakuma Ayashe aranta tana ganin girman al'amarin da take fuskanta, kudi kamdai gashi tana samu tunda tagama tatse Mal.Habu tasaida gidansa baisani ba harma dana Lami duk batareda sunsani ba, rana kawai masushi suke jira suxo su koresu, shiyasa takeson tayi amfani da Nana tasamu kudin domin qarin dogara da kanta.

Tawan yau ko gone xa'a kori Mal.habu a shago don tajima da sayansa, ita yanxu Kano takeson tafiya acan takeson bude sabuwan rayuwarta, saidai batadan Yaya Alhajin xai karbi lamarin ba.

Haka har suka isa wani gida, a bakin gate suka tsaya don maigadi yanuna baxai barsu su Shiva batareda anbashi ixinin barinsu ba, saida yakira maigidansa yasanar dashi akabashi ixini snn yabude musu qofa suka shiga nan Ayashe tahau mishi sababi, saida tayi me isarta snn suka shige 

*******
A kasuwa kuwa mutane ne cike abakin shagon Mal.Habu, anata hataniya, wani ne acikinsu yake magana da alama shi aka tabo, Wlh yau baxan yarda ba saiya fita yabar shagon nan tunda bana gado bane, shekara uku bai biya haya ba, ko abun shagon nashi nace xan kwashe duka ai baxan fanshe kudina ba.

Haquri ake ta bashi amma yaqi haqura shiko Mal.Habu yana gefe bashida katabus, a qarshe dai aka bashi shawaran yafita yabar mishi shagon kawai, haka yanaji yana gani yamiqa makullin shagon, don ko zarafin shiga ciki yadauki koda tsinkene aciki baisamu ba.


A falo suka zauna sunajiran fitowarshi, yabata lokaci sosae kapin can saigashi yafito donsu har sunqosa da zaman, a dakile yaxauna yafara kallon Nana  sheqeqe kamar yaga kashi.

Can yafara magana cikin isa da mallaka, Ayashe kike kowa? Yatambaya cikin halin ko inkula eh Ayashe ne Yallaboi tafada cikin xumudi da kwadayi.


Yayi shiru kamar baxaiyi magana ba a can qasan maqoshinsa yafara musu bayani, To Ayashe yarinya tayimin xansan yanda xa'ayi amma yanxu kije sai nanda wata biyu kidawp don yanxu akwai abubuwa a gabana, dafatan kingane.

Jiki a sanayaye Ayashe tace bakomai Yallaboi, cikin xuciyarta kuwa tsinemishi take yi, don ba haka tasoba, baikobi takanta ba, yamiqe kudi yaxaro a aljihunsa ya wurga haryasamu idonta, maimakon taji haushi ma, a'a bugewa tayi da godiya da nuna farin ciki.

Bai amsata ba yayi shigewarsa, suko suka tashi suka nufo gida.

Kamar hadin baki atare suka iso gida tareda Mal.Habu, shima akan Babur din haya don harda Babur dinsa yabayar cikin kudin shagon.

Yayi mamakin ganinsu sundawo daga anguwa a wnn yammacin, amma bashida ikon tambayansu daga ina,  basu yi magana da junaba sakamakon wani dam Anguwansu Mal.Habu dayaxo yana haki yana kwala mishi kira tun daga nesa.

Fitowa ta. 3⃣3⃣

Dafashi yayi yana tamabayarsa lpy? Shiko cikin in ina yace kayi Sauri ana nemanka a gidanku, ai baijira saura qarashen xancen ba yabi gefensa kotakan inda Ayashe suke tsaye bai kalla ba.

Itace tai saurin tare mai kawo saqon tafara tambayarsa meke faruwa, aiko nan yace mata iyayen Mal.Habu ne suka rasu sakamakon guban da sukaci  acikin waken da yakasance da magani acikinsa.

Bataji komai a ranta ba, sabanin Nana da hankalinta yagama tashi harta rasa yanda xatayi don har tafara kuka babu shiri tajuya da nufin bin mahaifin nata.

Ayashe ce tai saurin katseta tareda cemata kekuma ina xakije? Xance gidan kaka nane tafada tana kuka gwanin ban tausayi hakanne yasa jikin Ayashen yin sanyi matuqa, tace mata muje.

Haka suka nufi gidan tundaga gefen gidan Mal.Jafaru kake hango cunkoson jama'a babu masaka tsinke ko ina al'ummar annabi ne cike maqil.

Haka sukaita rabawa har suka isa gidan, nafa suka fara cin karo da mata anata koke koke, dakin Mmnshi suka shiga suka tarar da ita kwance sambal don har ammata sutura, Nanace tai saurin qarasawa gurinta ta duqa tafara magana cikin sigar tashin hankali.

Magana tafarayi cikin kuka, tana cewa Shknn nikam naxama banida kowa yaxanyi da rayuwa ta, babu uwa babu kakanni bansan dadin kowa ba saina Mamana gashi anwayi gari anyi satittika har anfara irga shekaru duk da Inada tabbacin babu mai irgawa saini.


Nayi rashin mahaifiya tah, inaji a jikina baki mutuba, inaxan ganki, bawanda yataba yimini jajen rashinki, wanda yakamata yatausayamin yaxame mini *Tudun dafawa* baidamu da rayuwata ba, dani da babu duk daya ne agurinsa, bansan dadin rarrashi ba.

Naso ace haka naga mahaifiyata daya fiyemin kwanciyar hankali a rayuwata, rabona da bacci me dadi tun Daren da kika bar gida, kowa bayayi dani Mama kitausayamin kidawo gareni konima xansamu gata, Kakatah kin huta Allah yajiqanki.

Tafashe da matsanancin kuka gurin kowa yayi shiru ita kadai ake sauraro inda xuciyoyin mutane da dama  na gurin ya karye, tasa mutane da dama kuka duk da basuda abunda xasu gaya mata.

Yayar Mal.habu ne tasamu qarfin guiwar matsawa gurinta domin ta rarrasheta amma tana xuwa Nana taqi yarda a dagata a wajen. Haka taita kuka agurin babu qaqqautawa, har aqarshe aka fitar da ita domin asamu damar fitar da ita.

Fadar tashin hankalinda Mal.Habu yashiga ma bata lokaci ne, abunda yafi tsaya mishi arai shine rabonshi dayasa iyayen nasa a idonsa harya manta, gara ma mahaifinsa wata takwas dasuka shude yaje har shago yagaisheshi, duk da baya amsawa cikin dadin rai amma hakan baya hanashi xuwa akai akai, don rabonshi da gidan harya manta.

Mahaifiyarshi kuwa baxai taba iya tuna rabonshi da ita ba, sai yau dayaganta cikin sutura, ganinda baxai amfaneshi ba, haryamxu baxaice ga dalili kwaya daya daya gifta tsakanin sonda yake musu na iyaye ba.

Yayi kukan zuci haryadawo yanayin na fili, idan yaxauna yakanji a ransa yana buqatar yin tunani nashi na kanshi amma baya iyawa, yaxaiyi haka kanshi xaiyita ciwo, aqarshe yadau dangana.


Nana acikin kwana bakwai dinda tayi a wnn gidan taga banbanci sosae domin tasamu kulawa kwarai dagaxke bana wasa ba, saidai baida amfani a gareta domin ba'ai mata a lokacinda takeda buqatar hakan ba, abunda yake daure mata kai baiwuce yanda duk yanda taso aimata maganar mamanta hakan yagagara ba.

Koda ita tadauko maganar to sai ansamu anjanye maganar ansako wani maganar na daban, saboda irin hakan taja bakinta tai shiru tabarwa xuciyarta, tacigaba da roqon ubangiji ya bayyana mata mahaifiyarta, snn tana qara roqon Allah yadora ta akan Ayashe dakuma Alhajin nan.

Hohh _zahrah_ nah to nice nasai muku ankawo lokacinda khadija tana nan ne shiyasa nabata nata ta tafi mata dashi, badai bakyason zama guri daya ba? Kinzabi kiyta zirga zirga tsakanin parts dinnan? Sai cikin dare ki isheni da kukan ciwon gabobi.

Mummy to muje daki inga nawa kayan, kidaiyi haquri in kammala abincin xuwa asibitin saimutafi tare dagacan mushirya yaukam xaki Bini ko?

Um Umm ta maqale kafada alaman baxata jeba, nidai Mummy tsoro take bani, kai mummy ta dafe, abunnan yana matuqar damunta, Sam _zahrah_ batason xuwa gurin mahaaifiyarta, wataran da kuka da qarfi take turata a mota suje tare.

Itakuma tanayin hakanne domin tasan cewar Matar tanada muhimmanci a garesu yanda ko watarana duk Daren dadewa idan Gaxkiya tafito xasuy saurin shaquwa.

Amma itakuma taqi yarda, hade rai mummy tayi snn tace, Baby na idanpa baki Bini ba babu ruwana dake, snn next week badake xa'aje Airport ba, kai inshort ma keba qanwar Yy Aliey bane, tana gama fadin hakan tai kamar xata raba ta gefenta tawuce.

Aiko dasauri tariqota tace sorry mummy ni qanwarshi ne, xanje to tafada tana murna, datx my dotah, to kijira ingama girkin muje dakin tare don banason kije ke kadai badadi ne dake ba, yanxu xaki hargitsamin guri.


Da wannan surutun nata taita yimata harta samu ta kammala girkin duka nufi dakin, bathroom suka shiga saida mummy taimata wanka, suka fito ta shirya ta tsaf, cikin wata dakekkiyar shadda, dinkin doguwar Riga,wanda takarbi jikinta bana wasa ba.

Eh tafada tareda fita da gudu daga dakin, banda gudu Mummy tafada mata, amma inaa ai tayi nisa, ikon Allah, Allah yashiryar min dake tafada tareda shigewa bathroom domin itama tasamu tashirya.

Saida suka gama shirinsu snn  ta aika aka kirata, tafito tanayiwa humaira gwalo, mummy nipa a gaba xan xauna, daddy ne yace shigo dota na, duk inda kikeso anan xaki xauna babyna, dariya tafara yiwa Mummynnata tashige suka nufi asibiti.

Isarsu asibitin suka nufi sashinda take kamar kullum, saida suka kai abubuwan da suka taho mata dashi gurin me kula da ita,  itakuwa taimusu jagora har dakinda take, kamar kullum sun sameta a xaune tasa kanta a tsakankanin cinyoyinta, kuka takeyi kamar ranta xai fita, tsayuwa sukayi ta window suna leqenta don yanxu bata bari suje inda take, abunnata yana matuqar daure musu kai, duk wayewan gari salon haukan nata canxawa yakeyi.


Magani kuwa anayinsa kamar ba'ayi watarana idan abun yatashi ba sauqi, tofa ko ma'aikatan ma sai sunyi ahankali da ita, don wuni takeyi tana buge buge.

Tajima ahakan can a qarshe dago idanunta daxatayi tasauke akan _zahrah_...........

Saboda yau Ummanta Ayashe tasanar mata yau xasu koma gidan Alhajin don wa'adin da yabasu yacika, shiri takeyi tana kuka, duk a daburce take.

Bayan tagama shirinta ne, tahau kan darduma tayi raka'anta biyu, bayan ta idar tajima axaune a gurin tana addu'oi cikin kuka na Neman agajin ubangiji.

Fitowa tayi jikinta a sanyaye, tayiwa umman nata magana akan tagama, saida tagama uxirinta tsaf snn tafito, kallonta tayi sama xuwa qasa snn tace mata koma kicanxa dayan kayan jiyan ba wann xakisa na xuwa gidan Alhajin ba........
