Fitowa ta. 4⃣7⃣


Da humaira tafara cin karo cikin shigarta ta fita xuwa islamiya, tana ganinta ta saki murmushi tareda karban jakar hannunta tana gaisheta, amsawa tayi suka nufi cikin gidan, Zarah ce takwala mata kira tareda cewa humairah kiyi sauri munkusa yin latti pa, a tare suka juyo Gaban Maryam ne yafadi, don wani mugun kama dataga yarinyar yau tayi mata da Nana.


Ta ina ma suka hada hidi bandama sharrin xuciya tafadi hakan a ranta, cikin gidan suka nufa sai da humairah ta dangana ta da dakin ummin ta snn tawuce islamiya..


Cikata akayi da abun motsa baki, bayan sunqara gaisawa ne, Haj.Maryam tafara sanar da 'yar uwarta halinda take ciki tun daga ranar da tafara haduwa da Nana har xuwa yanxu saidai ta sirranta rayuwar Nanan, tadai nuna mata cewa marainiya ce.


Tajima tana juya abun a ranta, a qarshe tace gaxkiya Maryam kinyi tunani snn kinyi ceton rai da kuma mutunci.


Saboda da ace kin kori yarinyar nan to pa akwai yiwuwan mijinnaki ya bita su hadu acan wani gurin ya rinjayeta har ya lalata mata rayuwarta, idan kuma basu hadu abai xata iya fadawa cikin wani hali marar kyau, Wanda kinada hakki akai domin kinga alamar kintsuwa a tartare da ida amma kika kasa taimaka mata.


Munadai fatan Allah ubangiji yasa hakan da kikayi Allah yadubeki yaxamo sanadiyyar shiryuwar mijinki domin a bayanin da kikamin na gameda zumudin da yakeyi akanta ba qaramin so yake mata ba.


Maganar kishi kuma Dole nepa saikin danne xuciyarki, da zafi kam, ace kinfaro rayuwarki da mijinki tun bakusan rayuwar auren ba daga ke har shi Ku biyun Ku, amma rana tsaka kiga kinfara rayuwa da wata dukanku a qarqashin ni'imarsa, shawara ta gareki shine kiriqe addu'a, cikin riqo da addininki dakuma gaxkiya komai xaixo miki dasauqi, karki cutar da ita, domin a rayuwa da ace kai ka cuta gara acuceka, idan ita ta cutar dake ubangiji xai dubi taimakon da kikai mata da kyakykyawar niyyar ki akanta yabi miki hakkinki.


Amma idan ke ce kika cutar da ita, to ina tabbatar miki duk abunnan dakikayi yatashi a banxa, don kua kin sako ha'inci a cikin zaman naku,ubangiji kua baya xalunci bai bimata itama.


Haka taita mata nasihu masu ratda jiki, saida ta tabbatar tagama yin sanyi snn tafara janta da Hiran wasa, tace mata kuma wlh saura ranar first nyt dinsu ki kasa bacci, dariya tayi sosae harda riqe cikin ta snn tace mata.


Haba dai Allah ya sauwaqe saikace wata qaramar yarinya, ke wlh a xancen kishi babu yaro babu babba kawai haquri ke determining mutum, kedai kidaure kawai karki cika Baki domin sassafen washegarin tariyar xan shigo gidanki induba halin da kika kwana.


To Allah yakaimu Da Amin ta amasa ta, hiransu sukai tayi daga baya sukai bankwana da juna akan sai sun shigo a bikin kawai.



_Bayan wata biyu_


Cikin hikima da ixxar ubangiji andaura auren Nana da Alhaji Abbakar akan sadaki naira dubu Dari biyu, aurenda da yatara manyan mutane qusoshin gwamnati dakuma shahararrun 'yan kasuwa kowa yayi matuqar mamakin yanda yadago xancen aure lokaci guda Wanda a da can kowa yasanshi ba mutum bane me sha'awan ajiye mata biyu.

A gurin daurin aure kuwa Mal.Habu ne ya kalli Dan uwansa yace dashi kaji ikon Allah sunan Amaryan daya da 'yata, yafadi hakan cikin karyewar murya, dafashi yayi yacigaba da bashi baki har yasamu yadan sake.



Acikin gida kuwa 'yan uwan Hajiya Maryam sunyiwa NANA kara Nana Kadan ba, domin kuwa sun nuna mata gata sosai sun maye mata gurbin 'yan uwanta. _Abu daya ne basuy mata ba, domin kuwa basu shiryata yanda yadace ba I mean babu kayan harka lolx_😜 itakam batama San anayi ba, tunda batasan wace rayuwar xata Shiga ba.



Mummy ce taiyowa Ummy rakiya Inda aka barta a dakin Amarya tana yimata kwalliya, kwalliyar take mata amma hankalinta yanakan yanda take hango kamannin dayake tsakinta da zarah nta.


Humairah kirakani gurin mummy nidai wlh ban cikason taron mutane dayawa ba, shiyasa pa kikaga yanxu bancika xua gidanku ba, domin kullum gidanku cike yake a mutane.


Kedai kika sani, mutum ai rahama ne, naji muje dai kirakani, ahakan sukaita gota cicirindon mutane har suka shiga side din Amaryan.


Mummy abunda tafara fadi ne, sauran maganar da ke bakinnata ne yamaqale sakamakon toxali datayi da fuskar Nana, faduwar gaba taji wani Rass batareda tasan dalilin saba,.

Menene dota? Kasa qarasa maganar tayi sai matsowa kusa da Mummyn datayi, daidai lokacin da itama Nanan ta dago ido domin kallob wacce aka kira da dota donkuwa muryanta babu maraba Dana Mamanta.


Itakuwa Zarah shagaltuwa tayi da kallon Nana don kuwa tayi matuqar yimata kyau tareda shiga ranta lokaci qanqani, Nana ce tadaga musu gaisuwa badon komai ba saidon tanason qarajin muryan zaran.


Humairah ce kadai ta amsa domin zarah kam kafeta tayi da idanu kawai, mummy ce tace dota baxaki amsa bane? Shagwabe fuska kawai tayi batareda ta amsa ba, sunjima a hakan, har Nana ta fidda ran zarah data qara yin magan acikin dakin, a qarshe zarah tace Mummy har shi yaushe xamu tafi gida? 


Dota anjima kadan xamu tafi, harkin gama kallon Amaryan? Duk xumudinki akan amarya anan xai tsaya? Murmushi kawai tayi tace mummy inason xanyi waya da Yaya Ibby ne, yacemin xaki kirani after magrib.


To muje muy sallama da mutanen gidan mutafi, haka suka fita, xarah  kasa haquri tayi saida ta waiwayo suka hada idanu da juna aiko suka sakarma junasu murmushi kapin sukai waje.



Nana ce takwanta akan bayanta tana tunanin muryan da taji na Xarah, a take taji tunanin mahaifiyarta yadawo sabo a ranta, dakuma tunanin yanda auren ta yaxo mata, kuma be me qarfi ya kufce mata, Wanda yasa tama manta da wata kwalliya da akai mata, yitayi me isarta batai shiru ba, saida taji kanta yafara amsawa, _Niko nace ki Adana kukanki xakiy me dalili_

Fitowa ta. 4⃣8⃣



Haka akaita hidima babu kama hannun yaro, Haj.Maryam yawanci al'umma sai binta da idanu kawai sukeyi inda wasu ko suke ganin wautar ta, domin acewar su basuga abun barin miki akan lamarin kishiya ba, wasu daga cikin qawayenta tun da sukaji labarin biki suke kawomata gurbatattun shawarwari amma haka kowa ke tafiya da kayanta domin maganganun nasu basu samu muhalli agunta ba.

_yakamata a jinjina mata_


Zame jikinta tayi a cikin jama'a dakyar saboda yanda gidan yacika maqil babu masaka tsinke, dakin Nana tanufa kai tsaye, komai na dakin daya tasa suka saya da nata banbancin su kawai kala, nata komai peach ne sabanin na Nana komai purple ne.


A qasa tasameta atsugene, kanta a kan gado tanata dirzan kuka, cikin sassarfa Haj.Maryam tanufeta, dagota tayi gani tai fuskannan tayi jazur tsabar kuka gashi tayi caba~caba, domin tagama bata fuskar da powder.


Zamatayu ta Dora kanta akan cinyarta snn tafara tambayarta meke damunta kodai wani abun akai mata? Da kanta tabata amsa domin baxata iya magana ba.


Dakyar tasanar da ita abunda yake damunta, haquri tashiga bata dakuma bata baki hartasamu ta tsagaita kukan, sata tayi tashiga bathroom da wanko fuskanta ta gyarashi snn tace mata bari takoma mutane sunata jiranta amma xata aiko mata dawata sister nta taxi suxauna tare, ai batasan ita kadaice a dakin ba, don batasan tafiyar Mummyn zarah ba.


Ummyn IBBY Ashe Mummyn zarah ta tafi bansaniba tabarmin 'ya sai kuka takeyi ita Kadai a daki? Ummy ce tace wlh kua zarah ce ta takura mata akan saisun tafi gida, wash daga cikin qawayenta ne adakin tai tsaki domin ita abunna Maryam yamxu yafara bata takaici, batabi takansu ba, taci gaba da harkokinsu.


Horn akeyi babu qaqqautawa dagaji kasan meshi a matse yake mal.Habu da Dan uwanshi ne a zaune, cikin hanzari mal habu  yamiqe yabude gate din, Jada baya yayi domin kamar me motar zai bangajeshi, tinted glass ne hiyasa Sam baiga nacikij motar ba, komaws yayi yana mamakin nacikin rufe gate din yayi yadauki but a yashiga bayi.


Ko parking maishi baiyi dakyau ba, _nikaina zura idanu nayi domin ganin me fitowa daga cikin motar, aiko waxan yi tozali dashi macece nagani kuma bakowa bace face AYASHE_  lallai tabbas kudi na gyara Dan Adam, cikin shigarta ta alfarma, fuskarnan tasha kwailin an kwaile ta, tayi radau da ita.


Swiss lace ne ajikinta kalar sky blue, anmata dinkin Riga da zani, kyau kanfa tayi kyau akin qarshe barikinta yaqara fitowa dodar ajikinta, itada wata 'yar akinta ne take riqe mata da wata 'yar qaramar troley dinta, kaitsaye sukai tambaya aka kaisu sashen uwar gida.


A kitchen akaiyo kiran Haj.Maryam domin tanacan tana bada odan irin girge girgen datake shiryawa mijinta domin yasanar mata yananan shigowa gidan by 4pm.


Kai niyau yaxanyi da mutane🤦🏽‍♀ ina xua tabada amsa, saida taqarasa bada umarnin aikin snn tafita tayi dakinnata, ganin masu Neman Nata tai baqin fuska ne batasansu ba, amma hakan baihanata fadada fuskanta ta qawatashi da murmushi ba. Taqaraso garesu aka gaisa a mutunce.


Nan Ayashe tasa 'yar dakin nata tabude qaramin jakar nasu, nan sukaga wasu tulin ledoji ne a qulle kala kala, Ayashe tasauqo qasa tahau warwarewa tana fadin sunayensu dakuma amfaninsu, dagalo sukai da bakinsu suna kallonta yanda taketa bayani kallo daya xakai mata basai kaqara ba, xaka fahimci batada ruwa a idonta, a taqaice dai batada kunya.


Saida tagama bayananta gaba daya Haj Maryam tayi murmushi tace da ita, nagode da karamcin da kika nuna tunda kika taso gari ya gari kikazo domin taimakona kamar yanda kika fada, amma kiyi haquri nibana irin wannan harkan Nana cikin irinsu, daga fadin hakan taja bakinta ta tsuke.


Kowa agurin yayi yo mata caaaa kamar xasu cinyeta, itakua ko ajikinta tananan akan bakanta domin akan kishiya baxatai abunda bata saba yiba, abunda batasan ya qarshen shi xaixo mata ba.


Aiko nan mutane suka matso gareta sukaita kwasa ana cika mata jaka da kudade, saida ta kammala kwashe kudadensu tai haramar tafiya, itakam Haj.Maryam batama dakin domin tajima da fita.


Ayashe tana fita taqara Neman akaota side din Amarya, shiga sukayi suka zauna a falo, sunjima sunajiran fitowan Amaryan, amma shiru, a qarshe gajiya sukai dazaman, watace tafito tabasu haquri akan cewa baxasu samu ganin Amarya ba, domin yanxu haka tana sallah baya. ta idar kua xa'a shiryata ne domin miqata gurin dangin mijinta,  Ayashe ce tafara miqewa cikin takaici , a ranta tace saikace wata 'year gwal? Mtswww bahaka tasoba sukai tafiyarsu.


Bayan angama shiryata ne Haj.Maryam ma tafito da ita suka jera har can side dinda aka sauke 'yan uwan Alhaji, sun yaba sosae da dabi'un Nana Wanda wasu daga cikin su kyanta kedaukan idanunsu.

Sunyimusu nasiha gabadayansu tareda yabawa gwaxon dakuma jarumtar Haj.Maryam, aqarshe tariqo hannun Nana suka dawo.


Misalin qarfe Tara na dare jama'an gidan bikin suka watse bakajin qaran komai banda qira'an Ahmad Sulaiman na suratul baqarah Wanda Haj maryam ce akunnashi ganin qafa yadauke.


Shigowa yayi da leda guda biyu a hannunsa saida ya tako tsakiyan falon, snn yafara tunanin meya kamata yafarayi jin qara kamar xa'a bude dakin yara ne yasadhi saurin shigewa dakinsa haryana tuntube, baxai iya hada idanu da yaranshi ba, duk dama baida jaa akan tarbiyarsu, dakuma ilimin da uwarsu ta dorasu akai, yanada tabbacin baxai samu matsala irin Wanda ake samu a wasu gidajen ba.



Zama yayi yadafe kansa, yau rana daya amma ya tsinci kansa cikin matsanancin kunyar matarshi, baisan dawani ido xai kalleta ba, yashafe kusan rabin Awa a zaune yana tubka da warwara, qarshe dai yayi tamaza yamiqe yanufi dakinta.


A kan darduma yasameta, isa gareta yayi, itakuma ganinsa yasata tsagaitawa da karatunta bayan takai Ayah, gaisheshi tayi cikin girmamawa, tareda tambayarsa ya fama da jama'a? Aike xan tambaya, ya fama da mutane? To Alhmdllh.


Yakasa furta komai itama agareta hakan abun yake, qarshe dao itacce tafara cewa Yaya kuma kaxo gurina kaida xakaje dakin Nana? Tafadi hakan tanajin wani Abu yatsaya mata a maqoshinta, in ina yafarayi Wanda kanaji kasan yarasa mai xai fada ne.


Fahimtar hakan datayi a tattare dashi yasata miqewa tsaye takama hannunsa, shiko binta yacigaba dayi kamar qaramin yaro, saida takaici qofar dakinnata snn takoma bayanshi shikuma yaxama yana gabanta.


Magana tafarayi tace nasan xakace kanason kahadamu guri dayane, Nayi maka Alqawari mijina baxaka samu matsala daniba matuqar inacikin hayyacina, xanyi qoqarin gurin ganin daidaituwar gidanka, kaidai ka kasance me adalci tsakaninmu, ina roqon Alalh yasa wnn auren shixai kawo silan kawo farinciki madauwamiya a gidanmu Amin, inai maka fatan Alheri, Rama kaiwa nan a zancen ta tai saurin rufe qofan dakinnata tareda sa mishi key.


Yajima  tsaye yama rasa mexaiyi guda daya domin Maryam tagama daureshi da jijiyoyin jikinsa, a qarshe kama hanya yai sai dakin gimbiya Nana.


A zaune yasameta itama akan darduma tana karatun qur'ani, gabanta yaje yaxauna ya tsura mata idanu har muryanta yafara sarqewa domin ganin dirkeken namiji a kusa da ita dab da dab.


Kaiwa qarshen aya tayi an tagaisheshi cikin rawan murya, amsawa yayi cikin sakewar fuska, miqewa yayi yashiga bathroom yadauro alwala ya umarceta tamiqe suy sallah.


Jansu yayi sallah bayan sun idar nikaina nayi mamakin yanda Alhaji yaketa kwararo addu'oi banyi tsammanin yayi sani haka ba, bayan sun kammala yajuyo yafara yimata tambayoyi gameda addininta tabashi amsa yanda yadace cikin taimakon sabon malamin Islamiyar da aka samo mata.


Yaji dadin yanda tabashi amsoshi, hannu yasa a saman kanta yayi mata addu'oi kamar yanda musulinci ya tanadar.

Tazo tsakiyar dakin sai wayarta yafadi qasa, gashi babu mayafi ajikinta, aiko tana duqawa domin daukan wayarta kinsan meya faru? Nanma batace mishi komai ba, INA gayamiki my star, tsugunawar da xatai domin dago wayarta aiko nan nonuwanta suka fito daga cikin Rigan dukansu biyu, wayyo tafada daqarfi kamar yana kallonta, tai cilli dawayarta ga tausayin matar gakuma dariya dayaci qarfinta.


Dariyanta takeyi bilhaqqi shima yana tayata, sunshare tsawon mintuna suka ta dariyarsu, can dai ta tsuke fuska sakamokon..............

_ADDU'OIN KU GARENI YANA QARAMIN QARSASHI YANA SANI FARINCIKI HARMA  YANA INGIZANI HAR NAKEJIN KAMAR IN KYAUTAR MUKU DA WAYATA_😉😉😜




Fitowa ta. 5⃣1⃣


Can dai ta tsuke fuskarta sakamakon wani kifiyar kishi data soketa harma tarasa yanda xatai dashi, shiko jinta daina dariyan ne yasashi saurin tambayar ta, _star_ menene?  _My star are you dere_ yaduba wayar tashi batai hanging call din ba snn bata amsa mishi ba.


Kamar daga sama yaji ta fashe mishi da kuka, nanko IBBY ya rude yayi ta aikin rarrashi dakyar yasamu yashawo kanta tai shiru snn yatambayeta meya faru?


Cikin muryan shagwaba tafara ce mishi bakaine ka kalli jikin budurwan abokinka ba, ai baisan lokacinda dariya ta subuce mishi ba, ammai yayi saurin guntsewa saboda yasan xai qara ballo ma kanshi wani liqin.


Cikin maqale murya kamar maiyin rada yace da ita _my star_ ummm kawai tace dashi, yanda nakejin kishinki a raina haka nake tayaki kishin kaina, baxan iya kallon jikin wata ba bayan naki, ke kadai ce a cikin raina, jikinki kadai nake ji da kuma gani amatsayin jiki, idanna tuna da jikinki nakan ji inkuma kalli jikin kowacce mace bayan naki a matsayin jikin Dan uwa na namiji.


Kisa a ranki jikinki kadai nake mararin kallo zarah inamiki so Wanda nikaina bansan adadin yawansa ba, sonda Nike miki zarah _is beyond expectation_ , banida burinda yawuce inganni a tare dake, badon iyayena ba zarah Dana ajiye wnn bokon naxo gareki, duk da hausawa sunce Aski inyaxo gaban gosji yafi zafi, wata dayan nan daya ragemin jinake kamar yau naxo qasar my star.


Da irin wadannan dadadan kalaman yayi ta amfani haryaga tasaki ranta, itakua a nata bangaren tunda yafara zubamata narkakkun kalamai zaman ya gagareta nan tazame takwanta tareda janyo dayan pillown tai matashi dashi.


Bayan yagama rikitata da labarai masu narkar da xuciyar masoyi ne, snn tabuqaci yabata labarin albishir dinda xai gaya matan.


Sanar mata yayi da cewa yakusan samo mafita akan matar da mummy tace duk Wanda ya warkar da ita xata bashi auren zarah, cikin zumudi ta tambayeshi ya akayi?


Bai boye mata komai ba, yasanar da ita yanda sukayi da abokinshi snn ya tabbatar mata da cewa next month xasu dawo  insha Allah, aiko nan tabuga tsallenta akan gadon snn tahau xubamishi labarin irin tarban da xata tanadar mishi, nan shikuma yagaya mata irin abunda yakeso tai mishi a ranar ciki kua harda kalan kayanda yakeda buqatan tasa a ranar.


Sun share zamani guda suna wayar daga qarshe sukai sallama badon sungaji ba, xuciyarta fal da Annashuwa tamiqe ta qarasa shirinta ta fito, samun mummy tayi zaune a falo tana kallo sallama tai mata itakuma taimata a dawo lpy tareda gargadin kar awuce 4pm.


Gidansu humairah tashiga, ta tarar da Ummy batanan tashiga makwafta, hakan itama takeso domin tunda Ummy tafahimci akwai soyayya tsakaninta da IBBY gaba daya tadaina sakewa da ita, duk yawan surutun ta tana tsintan Kanta da nutsuwa indai a gabanta ne.


Dakin humairah tawuce tasameta tana waya da saurayinta, don itama har ta kammala shirinta ganin zarahn baisa ta tsagaita da wayar ba, saima qara narkar da muryanta tayi tanata zuba mishi shagwaba, shiko sai aikin biye mata yakeyi.


Aqarshe Xarah ce tagaji da jiranta ta fizge wayar a hannunta ta kara akunnenta tace dashi plx kayi haquri xamuje anguwa ne later, takatse kiran.

Humairah ce tabita da harara tareda yin tsaki tamiqe daga kan gadon tareda cemata banxa kinyiwa kanki wlh anjima xai fita damu shopping, amma tunda kikai mishi iskanci wlh baxaki ba, dariya zarah tayi tamatso gurin humairah ta dafa kafadar ta snn tace yarinya kin kusa kidaina min rowan da gorin saurayi, ninawa me kankat nema daura wata daya yadawo gareni, snn ina tabbatar miki idan yadawo baxan wuce watanni biyu a gaba batareda igiyarsa a kaina ba.


Da sakakken baki humaira ke binta da kallo domin bata gane manufarta ba, ganin tanayi mata kallon rashin fahimta yasata janyota suka xauna abakin gado ta fayyace mata komai dasuke ciki itada IBBY.


Humairah tayi farinciki harma tarasa yanda xata misaltashi, Xarah tamiqe tana taku daya bayan daya tana girgixa duk wani sashe na jikinta tajuya gaba da baya snn tace, kifara biyayya don wlh mijina yana dawowa bani babu wasa dake, don kuwa naxama yayarki, kekuma kinxama qanwata humairah na tausaya miki xakisha aiki a gidannan, humairah ce takawo mata duka itakuma ta goce suka hau dariya.


Direban gidansu humairah ne yakaisu har gidan Nana, shigansu suka samu Mal.Habu xaune yanata fama da ciwon qafafu sakamakon ciwon hawan Jini dakuma ciwon sugar data kamashi.


 Hakan yasa bakowani lokaci yake iya fitowa waje yaxauna ba,yanxun ma Dan uwan nasa yatafi aika wa Haj.Amarya ce data aiko me aikinta a bashi saqon aikan.

Sunyi horn yafi say biyar, dakyar yasamu yaja qafafun nashi duk da kua tarin xogin dayake azabtua dashi ta dalilin ciwon, Xarah ko a cikin Mota tagama cika tayj famm da ita saura qiris ta fashe, jira kawai takeyi abude Taiwa mai gadon kaca kaca.


Amma me yana budewa taga tsohon a gefe yana dingisa qafansa, Wanda dagani kasan qarfin hali kawai yakeyi, jitayi baxata iya yin masifar data fito da niyyar yimishin ba, saima jin wani tausayinshi tayi ya shigeta farat daya.


Humaira nayi mata magana Sam bata kulaba, domin tayi gaba isa gurinshi tayi alokacinda idanunsu ya sarqu Dana juna, gaba dayansu biyun saida tsikar jikinsu yatashi batareda sun shiryawa hakan ba.


Sannu Baba shine abunda yafito daga bakinta cikin raunananniyar murya, yauwa sannu 'Yata yafada  yaname kallonta kallon da ake kirada kallon qurilla. 


Tama rasa meya kawota mekuma takeson sanar dashi, hakan yasata fakaicewa da ce mishi Baba qafarka ne yake ciwo? 'Yata qafata ke ciwo yafada yanayi mata murmushi tareda jin wani Abu ya sokeshi dabai San daga ina abun yakeba.


Tsintar kanshi yayi da kallonta sosai idanunshi kemishi qaryan yana hango kamanninta da Nanan shi, saurin kauda tunanin yayi a ransa tareda lallashin xuciyashi.


Hankalinsu ne yadawo jikinsu itace tafara kame~kame a qarshe tace Allah yabaka lpy, shikuma ya amsa mata da Amin, tai shigewarta cikin gidan, tana tafiya tana waiwaye kuma duk juyowanda xatayi xata samu shidinma ita yake kallo.


Side din Haj.maryam  suka fara Shiga kamar kullum, sun sameta itada yasmeen axaune suna hira Yasmeen tana riqe da Muhsin tana mishi wasa.

Suna Shiga Xarah tanufi Yasmin takarbi muhsin itadai yanxu inkaga taxo gidannan to saboda mutane biyu take xua daga muhsin sai mahaifiyarshi, gaisawa tayi dasu sama sama tanufi sashen Nana da muhsin a hannunta Tanata yimishi wasa shikuma yana aikin bangare mata baki.


Samun Nana tayi a zaune a falon ta tanayiwa kanta qunshin Jan lalle, suna ganin juna suka saki murmushi tareda gaisar da junansu cikin mutunta juna.


Hira sukeyi sosai da sosai inda Xarah kebawa Nana labarin ciwon qafan megadinsu, ikon Allah kumapa ko jiyama na aikeshi banga alaman qafanshi na ciwo ba, kuma daxunnan mafa na aika larai da saqona gurinshi bata gayamin baida lpy ba, to gaxkiya kuywa Baban su Yasmin magana Asama mishi magani yaban tausayi dagani ba tambaya kasan yanashan azaba, shknn insha Allah xan sanar dashi.


Sun idar da sallan la'asar sukai haraman tafiya gida, taso tasake ganinshi amma kuma bashi tasamu a bakin aikin ba, don haka suka wuce gida kawai.


Idanta gida ta labartawa mummy labarin albishir dinda IBBY yayi mata, duk da kua ya kwabeta akan karta Dada harsai ya dawo xai yiwa mummy baxata, ai mummy kamar xatasha ruwa a qasa tasha haka takeji, itakanta tajima tana zargin akwai qullin a cikin jinyar Zainab.

Aiko yau xan sanar da daddyn Ku, mummy nikam wacece wannan matar, meye alaqar mu da ita dakowa yasamu da samun warakar ta, harkuma ake alaqanta ta da aurena?????..........

Fitowa ta. 5⃣2⃣




Har kuma ake alaqanta ta da aurena? Shiru mummy tayi tana kallonta nadan tsayin wasu mintuna a ranta bakomai take aiyanawa ba kamar irin ranar da xa'ace Xarah tabar hannunta, tagama jarabtuwa dason yarinyar bugu da qari gakuma shaquwa dake tsakaninsu.


Mummy xara tafada da Dan qarfi, na'am itama tafada ta kalleta da murmushi akan fuskarta, zarah mekike so insanar dake bayan abunda nake sanar dake kullum idan kin yimin irin tambayar nan.


Nagaya miki matar nan tanada matuqar muhimmacin a rayuwarmu musamman ma ke, mummy to ai kullum maganar a dunqule kike sanar min da ita, bakya fayyacemin, bana gane manufarki, dariya taimata tareda dafata tajuyar da kanta gefe saboda jin da tayi idanunta na qoqarin kawo ruwa. 


Snn tadan buga kafadarta tace very soon dota insha Allah lokaci yakusa isowa da xakiji komai karki damu kinji? Nikanma mummy abun bai taba damuna ba, tafadi hakan tana miqewa tanufi dakinta, da kallon tausayawq mummy tabita har tabule tadaina ganinta, snn tasauqar da ajiyar xuciya kapin tacigaba da abunda takeyi.


A bangaren Nana kuwa dawowan mijinta sashenta hakan yabata daman yimishi maganan da zarah taimata na gameda rashin lafiyar mai gadinsu, yajinjina maganar sosai a ransa tareda cemata insha Allah xaisan yanda  xa'ayi akan matsalar, da hakan sukabar xancen.


_Washe garin ranar sunfita xai kai Nana makaranta ne yayiwa maigadin magana, isowa yayi gareshi cikin ladabi yagaisa dashi, Alhajin yace mishi akace dani qafarka na ciwo?


Nisawa yayi snn yace bani bane Dan uwana ne, tare muke aikin dashi, OK ina yake? Yafada yana waige waige don ganin koxaiga Dan uwan nasa.


Alhaji ai bayanan yafita cikin unguwa domin miqe qafafunsa, Ayya yayi kyau Allah yabashi lpy, insha Allahu idan nadawo xan yi magana dashi, baxai gagara ba xa'san yanda xa'ayi, mungode Alhaji Allah yaqara girma da Arxiki, Allah yasa kafi haka, Da Amin ya amsa mishi suka wuce.


A hanya take ce mishi kasan meye Bbn Yasmin? Juyowa yayi kacokan yana kallonta dariya tayimishi snn tace a'ah tuqi fa kakeyi, shidinma dariya yamayar mata tareda kanne mata ido guda daya yace, kome nakeyi baxai hanani sauraron maganar da duk kikeda muradin sanar daniba.


Hafsat baxan iya cewa kece rayuwata ba, amma ba shakka kinacikin ta, kuma kin yamki kaso mafi girma acikinta, kinsauya ta alokacin da banyi tsammani ba, banyi tunanin hankalina xai dawo cikin gidana gabadaya lokaci qanqani ba, ke kadai kike iyawa dani, baki gajiyawa Allah yabiyaki yafada yana murxa mata yatsunta da hannun da badashi yake tuqi ba.

( _namiji Dan duniya kace maryam itace komai dinka and now Nana taxama komai naka_ 😏😏😉

Tabbas kalamansa sun ratsata fiyeda tuananin me tunani, taji dadin batunsa, cikin lankwasar da kanta tace mishi baxan iya furta komai akan alherin da kayi agareni ba, domin kamin komai bansan kalamanda xanyi amfani dashi gurin nuna godiyata agareka ba, fatana Allah yabarmu a qarqashin inuwa daya, Yakuma baka ikon kulawa dani da kuma nemamini mafita akan damuwata.

Allah yanani iko, yafada ita kuma ta amsa mishi da Amin, wlh Bbn muhsin a kwanakin nan yanda nake ji ajikina da kuma irin mafarke mafarken da nakeyi, insha Allah nakusa haduwa da ahalina, katayini da addu'an samun nasara kai dai. Addu'a a kullum knayimiki ta alheri.


Da hirarrakinsu dasukeyi cikin dadin rai harsuka isa makarantan, ajiyeta tayi tareda cemata idan Antashi xaixo, ohh baka gajiya baxaka bari  direba yaxo ya maidani ba.


Xaro idanu yayi waje snn yace mata waneni? Sokike inmutu idan naganki da direba xaune Ku biyu a cikin mota? Xariyama maganar yabata hakan yasata sakankancewa tanata zuba dariyarta shikuwa ya shagala yana kallonta, can yace mata Hafsat komai naki dabanne ina qaunarki 'yar qaramar matata, dariya tai mishi ta balle murfin motar tawuce domin idan tabiye mishi baxai barta ta tafi ba, saida yadaina kallonta snn yajuya kan motar shi yatafi.


*BAYAN WATA DAYA*


Zarah ce giji giji acikin kitchen anata aiki baji babu gani harwani xufa tahada na wahala domin tahana kowa samata hannu a cikin girkinta, acewarta baxataso yadan dani komai Wanda ba hannunta ne ya sarrafashi ba.


Sai gab da azahar ta kammala komai taraba gida biyu, dayan tajereshi a falonsu domin Yaya Aliey badon halinshi tai mishi ba, saidon mummy alfarman da mummy ta roqa mishi, nacewar tasan baqaramin girki xaran ta iyaba, domin har makarantan koyan girki aka sata Wanda yaxamo tana xua Saturday da Sunday, takuma San yau rana ce ta musamman don tun ana saura sati aka fara shirye shiryen domin xuwan wnn ranan.


Shirya komai nasu itada IBBY tayi a falon daddyn su, can take so suxauna batareda an takura musu ba, kowa dayake cikin wnn family saida suka shaida xarah tana zumudi a yau dinnan.


Wanka tashiga tayi, Wanda tashafe sama da minti talatin tana wanke duk wani saqo na jikinta da sabulai masu inganci, kanta dama can yasha gyara, faduwa yaxo mata daidai da xama kua domin bata sallah hakan yabata daman yiwa kanta gyara da gel, inka Ganshi sai sheqi yake tayi.


Bayan fitowarta a wanka towel dinta tadauka na share jiki ta tsane dukkan ruwanda ke jikinta snn taiwa kanta mazauni akan stool dinda ke gaban mirror nta.


Mai ta shafa snn taxauna zaman kwalliya Wanda shima yadauki zamani guda kapin ta kammala dashi, Waldrop dinta tabude naga taciro wani hadadden swiss lace kalan maroon, dinkin Riga da sket, pattern dinshi ne akai kwalliya dashi ajikin Rigan.


Matsowa tayi gaban mirror domin daura dankwali, wayarta taji yayi ringing alaman alarm dinda ta saita ne yabuga, aiko cikin sauri sauri na zumudi harma takasa daura daura dankwalin akan ta dakyau.


Ai dataga takasa mayafinta kawai taxara fari qal dashi, snn tasaka takalmin ta shima fari mai tsini, kamar xata dungura haka take sauqowa daga steps din, jikake tas tas tas, qarar takalminta ne kawai ke amsa kuwwa a cikin falon.


Mummy lokacin dauko su Yaya yayi pa ki tashi muwuce taqarasa maganar nata tana haki kamar Wanda tai gudun fanfalaqi.


Kallonta kawai mummy takeyi harma tarasa mexata ce da ita, mummy kinyi shiru kuma? Tafada tana bubbuga qafafunta a qasa, Doter wani irin zumudi ne haka kikeyi? Bakisan idan rawan kan yayi yawa xai rainaki ba? Kidan nitsu kikuma saka Jan aji koda kadan ne kinji? 


Kallon Maman nata tasakeyi snn tace to naji mummy mutafi to!! Allah Nagode maka ni khadija janyo hannun zaran tayi taxaunar da ita, dakyar yasamu tasahawo kanta, bata bari sunpita xua Airport dinba, saida ta tabbatar ta nitsar da ita snn sukaiwa direba magana yayi Airport dasu, motarsu a Jere Dana gidansu IBBY.



Isarsu lokacin saura minti biyar jirgin nasu yasauqa, basu wahalar da Kansu Neman gurin zama ba suka tsaya suna hirarrakinsu, Zarah da humairah labari humaira kebata amma  can ta fahimci hankalinta fa sam baya gurinta, domin idanunta wuwwurgasu kawai takeyi.

Fitowa ta. 5⃣3⃣


Aliey ne a gaba ibby yana bayanshi, suite ne baqaqe a jikinsu, ibby 'yar cikin kawai yabari ajikinshi ya rataya 'yar saman a kafadarshi, kallonshi kawai takeyi kamar taje ta rungume kayanta, badon Ummy datake gurinba babu abunda xai hanata rugawa a guje ta rungumeshi.


Isowarsu Ummy da mummy ne suka rungumesu cikin jin dadi, ibby ko gaba daya hankalinshi baya jikinshi burinshi yaga ta ina xai hango star dinshi? Ummy ce tafara fahimtar sa don haka cikin wasa ta xungureshi tace to marar haquri gatacan bayan Mummyn ta, dariya gurin yadauka, shiko ko a jikinshi yayi gurin Mummyn.


Itakua ganin ya nufota saita rufe idanunta da tafin hannunta, kowa a gurin idanunshi na Kansu, Inda Aliey shima ya qura mata ido tareda ayyanawa a ransa, yace yaushe yarinyar nan tayi irin wannan girman, wnn jikin nata ne kodai ciko ne? Kai anya farin nan natane ba mai take shafawa ba? _nidai nace oho mtswww marar kirki_😏


Ibby ko yana isa yagaida mummy cikin sosa keyansa, itakua zarah sai wani mamman newa takeyi abayan mmn nata, mummy ce tace da ita zarah bakiga yayanki ba, baki gaida ko daya daga cikin su bafa? Aiko tana juyowa da idanun Aliey tafarayin tozali Wanda yagama qure kallonshi akanta, cikin daburcewa tace ina kwana Yaya?


Shima cikin kunya amma yadan basar ya tamke fuskarshi snn ya amsa snn ya kawar da fuskanshi kamar bai taba kallon inda takeba.


Batama bi takanshi ba domin xuciya ruhi kai harma da gangan jikinta yana tartare da ibbyn ta, mummy ce ta janye jikinta takama hannun Aliey sukai gurin mota domin har angama sa kayayyakin su cikin motar.


Hakanne yabawa ibby daman matsowa kusa da ita yasa hannu yasauqar da nata hannun daga rufe fuskarta da tayi,   idanunta ya kalleshi gani tayi a rayuwa bata tabayin arba da namijin da yakai ibbyn ta kyau da cikar haiba ba.


My star kinyi kyau, kinqara girma abunda yafada kenan cikin wata dasashshiyar murya Wanda shikanshi baxaice ga abunda yajawo muryar tayi hakaba, nagode kawai ta iya cewa, ganitayi gurin duk an watse saura sune kadai basu Shiga mota ba.


Aliey kua tunda yashiga yaxauna tareda mummy a bayan motar wayarsa ya kunna kamar meyin naxarinta, amma a xahiri hankalinshi nakan ibby Wanda yaga kusancin da sukai da juna a wannan gurin yayi yawa idanunshi gixo suke mishi kamar ma ibbyn a rungume yake da zaran. Mummy yafada, na'am son ya akayi, mutafi gida mana,


Ohh bakaganin gacan wadanda muke Dan jira ai kabarsu sugaisa yaushe rabonsu, hade fuska yayi snn yace mummy a cikin jama'a basujin kunya? Wnn aiba mutuncin mace bane, ko mayafin kirki babu ajikinta fa.


Mummy kuwa zuge glass din side dinta tayi snn tace da Dan qarfi to Laila majunun kutaho mutafi kwa qarasa labarin a gida, tahpwa suka farayi suna Dan hirarrakinsu, kama ganinsu kasan masoyane wadanda xuciypyin su yake cike da qaunar juna.


Har gurin motarsu ya rakota bude mata murfin mortar yayi tashiga, snn yajuya xai wuce gurin nasu motar, yayah tafada cikin sigan shagwaba kaxo mutafi amotar mu, gabadayansu kallonta kawai sukeyi, tsakin Aliey ne yadawo dasu daga  tunanin da sukeyi,


Ibby yasan wasu yake yiwa tsakin hakan yasashi cemata sorry my star muna isa gidanku xan sauqa kinji? Ikon Allah mummy tafada tana riqe haban ta tana kallonsu, dariya yayi yawuce tasu motar akaja akao gida dasu baki daya.


Mirror ta karkato dashi tafara gyara saurin dankwalinta hankali kwance, mummy ce tace da ita zarah to ai yaga wnn kam ko? Juyowa tayi snn tace mummy ai baiga styles na dauri ba, uhmmm yayi kyau.


Yauwwa mummy idan mun isa gida kiss akarbomin sauran dinkunana, toh naji uwatah yanxukam kibarmu da surutun haka saimun isa gida, xumburo bakinta tayi gaba, juyawanda xatayi saitaga sunhada idanu da Aliey ta mirror aiko ta dalla mishi harara, Wanda yasashi zaro idanunsa waje yana mamakin yaushe ta rainashi haka harda harara? Lallai xaiyi maganin rashin kunyar nan tun kapin yayi nisa.


Isarsu gida kuwa kowa sashen shi yanufa, ibby shida zarah suka nufi inda ta Jere mishi abubuwan da tashirya domin shi, yana shigowa ya shaqi wani daddadan qamshi Wanda yasashi lumshe idanunshi tareda cemata My star inason qamshinnan, murmushi tayi tareda qarasawa kan dining din taiwa kanta maxauni.


Isowa yayi yaxauna kusa da ita, serving dinshi tafarayi, kallonta kawai takeyi yana binta da murmushi, rising star wnn duka kalolin abincin Donni kadai?


Duk kai kadai ne my paramour sonakeyi kayi qiba saboda girkina, lallai idannayi wiba ai baxaki iya daukana ba, dariya taimishi sosae snn tace mishi, to aiko yanxu mq baxan iya dagaka ba, um um nipa ba irin wnn dagawar nike nufiba.


To wani iri? Baxan fadi miki ba, saidai watarana innuna miki shi a aikace, haka sukaira hira tana tsokanarta, kowani abinci da kalan ruwan lemun da aka had a mishi dashi, shikanshi ibby ya jinjina wa Xarah yasan yayi dace.


A falofalo mummy ce xaune tama serving dinshi cin abincin yafarayi haniqan tareda yiwa mummy santi, mummy ya kikayi kalan wannan abincin? Son banibace wacce dai ka raina wa wayo itace ta sarrafa, uhmmm kawai yafada batareda yaqaracewa komai ba.


Can ko kamar an tsikareshi yace mummy naga ibby yashigo gidannan kuma banganshi ba, ai yana tare da zarah sunacan falon daddynku a can ta shirya mishi abincin sa, baisam dalili ba amma saiji yayi gabanshi ya yanke ya fadi, amma ya dake yace amma dai mummy kinsan dai Barin mace da namiji su kebance guri daya shedan ne yake kasancewa na ukun su ko? Sheqeqe tayi tana kallonshi qarshe tace mishi babu abunda xasuy insha Allah, jiyayi abincin ma yafita aranshi, kawai yamiqe yanufi dakinshi, da idanu ta rakashi itama tamiqe tai nata dakin.



&&&&&&&&&&&&&&

Nana ce suke dawowa gida itada Bbn Yasmin hira sukeyi a mota cikin nishadi, har suka iso gida tare motarsu ta iso bakin gate din da motar Haj.Maryam.



Horn sukeyi amma babu alamar xa'a bude musu gate din, ikon Allah shine abunda yafito a bakin Alhajin, snn ya balle murfin motan ya fito, qaramin qofan yabude, hango Bbn Nana yayi yanata dingiso qafansa domin yanason isowa ya bude gate din.


Ah ah Kaine da kanka, ina Dan uwan naka? Kayi haquri Alhaji inata sauri inbude maka qafan nawane  yau ya takuramin,gashi Dan uwan nawa yaje dubomin magani.


Ah bakomai bari sufito mushiga gida kawai, direba ya bude yashigo da motocin, koma can gefe kayi zamanka, in Dan uwannaka yadawo kace ina magana dashi, yaxo yasameni aciki, To shknn ya fada yana rankwafar da kanshi alamar girmamawa.


Komawa yayi, gurin motar haj.maryam yafara isa yayi mata bayani snn ya isa yacewa Nana fito mushiga da qafafunmu muy taku irinna masoya, daroya taimishi ganin haj.maryam tafito hakan ya tabbatar mata akwai dalilin aukuwan hakan........

Fitowa ta. 5⃣3⃣


Aliey ne a gaba ibby yana bayanshi, suite ne baqaqe a jikinsu, ibby 'yar cikin kawai yabari ajikinshi ya rataya 'yar saman a kafadarshi, kallonshi kawai takeyi kamar taje ta rungume kayanta, badon Ummy datake gurinba babu abunda xai hanata rugawa a guje ta rungumeshi.


Isowarsu Ummy da mummy ne suka rungumesu cikin jin dadi, ibby ko gaba daya hankalinshi baya jikinshi burinshi yaga ta ina xai hango star dinshi? Ummy ce tafara fahimtar sa don haka cikin wasa ta xungureshi tace to marar haquri gatacan bayan Mummyn ta, dariya gurin yadauka, shiko ko a jikinshi yayi gurin Mummyn.


Itakua ganin ya nufota saita rufe idanunta da tafin hannunta, kowa a gurin idanunshi na Kansu, Inda Aliey shima ya qura mata ido tareda ayyanawa a ransa, yace yaushe yarinyar nan tayi irin wannan girman, wnn jikin nata ne kodai ciko ne? Kai anya farin nan natane ba mai take shafawa ba? _nidai nace oho mtswww marar kirki_😏


Ibby ko yana isa yagaida mummy cikin sosa keyansa, itakua zarah sai wani mamman newa takeyi abayan mmn nata, mummy ce tace da ita zarah bakiga yayanki ba, baki gaida ko daya daga cikin su bafa? Aiko tana juyowa da idanun Aliey tafarayin tozali Wanda yagama qure kallonshi akanta, cikin daburcewa tace ina kwana Yaya?


Shima cikin kunya amma yadan basar ya tamke fuskarshi snn ya amsa snn ya kawar da fuskanshi kamar bai taba kallon inda takeba.


Batama bi takanshi ba domin xuciya ruhi kai harma da gangan jikinta yana tartare da ibbyn ta, mummy ce ta janye jikinta takama hannun Aliey sukai gurin mota domin har angama sa kayayyakin su cikin motar.


Hakanne yabawa ibby daman matsowa kusa da ita yasa hannu yasauqar da nata hannun daga rufe fuskarta da tayi,   idanunta ya kalleshi gani tayi a rayuwa bata tabayin arba da namijin da yakai ibbyn ta kyau da cikar haiba ba.


My star kinyi kyau, kinqara girma abunda yafada kenan cikin wata dasashshiyar murya Wanda shikanshi baxaice ga abunda yajawo muryar tayi hakaba, nagode kawai ta iya cewa, ganitayi gurin duk an watse saura sune kadai basu Shiga mota ba.


Aliey kua tunda yashiga yaxauna tareda mummy a bayan motar wayarsa ya kunna kamar meyin naxarinta, amma a xahiri hankalinshi nakan ibby Wanda yaga kusancin da sukai da juna a wannan gurin yayi yawa idanunshi gixo suke mishi kamar ma ibbyn a rungume yake da zaran. Mummy yafada, na'am son ya akayi, mutafi gida mana,


Ohh bakaganin gacan wadanda muke Dan jira ai kabarsu sugaisa yaushe rabonsu, hade fuska yayi snn yace mummy a cikin jama'a basujin kunya? Wnn aiba mutuncin mace bane, ko mayafin kirki babu ajikinta fa.


Mummy kuwa zuge glass din side dinta tayi snn tace da Dan qarfi to Laila majunun kutaho mutafi kwa qarasa labarin a gida, tahpwa suka farayi suna Dan hirarrakinsu, kama ganinsu kasan masoyane wadanda xuciypyin su yake cike da qaunar juna.

Fitowa ta. 5⃣4⃣



Don haka tana dariya tabiyoshi har suka shigo ciki, samun haj.maryam tayi suna gaisawa hartana tambayan shi ya jiki? Dalilin hakan itama ta taka gurin domin tai mishi ya jiki, amma tana isa gurin sai me?


Nuna shitafarayi da yatsanta waigowa yayi aiko nan shima yamiqe cikin rikicewa Baba kawai ta iya fadi taxube anan sumammiya, aiko anma rasa waya fara durqusawa akanta acikin su ukun.


Ga tarin mamakin dakuma tambayoyi dasuke yawo acikin xuciyoyi dakuma ruhinsu saidai rashin kwanciyarta agurin cikin halin sumewa yasasu gusar da komai domin ceto ta.


Alhaji ne yayi saurin miqewa yabude gate da kansa, yadawo da sauri ya ciccibi Nana yawuce da ita cikin mota, Haj. Maryam tarufa mishi baya, Ai Bbn Nana Neman ciwon qafan nasa yayi yarasa yabisu shima acikin motar gabadayansu suka nufi asibitin, Alhajin ne dakanshi ke jansu, haj.maryam na maxaunin gefensa yayinda Nana da mahaifinta ke baya, hulan kansa yacire yanata faman yimata firfita.


Lokaci lokaci Alhaji yakan juyo yana duba halinda take ciki, fahimtar hakan da haj.maryam tayi ne yasata Neman alfarman yayi parking a gefe itakuma takarbi tuqin, hakan kua akayi domin gaba daya yagama zama birkitacce.


Cikin nitsuwa haj.maryam ta isar dasu asibitin, aka dauki Nana aka shigar da ita emergency, nan akasamu aka bata taimakon gaggawa cikin hikimar ubangiji sai gashi tadawo hayyacinta batare da ansha wahala ba, damacan lafiyayya cikin furgici ne ya auku da ita, kamar yanda suka ambata.

Tana farfadowa idanunta tafara saukewa kan Bbn nata tace dashi baba Ashe xanqara ganinka? Hajiya. Alhaji gabadayansu suka kalleta wannan shine Babana, tafada hawaye na gangaro mata akan kuncinta 

 Matsowa gurinta mahaifinnata yayi yariqo hannunta yarungume akan qirjinsa tareda cewa Nana Ashe dasauran shan ruwa na xua randa Allah xai hadani da Ahalina?


Sudai Alhaji sun fahimci wnn shine mahaifin Nana, amma sun rasa shikuma menene sanadinsa na fadin maganganun da kunnuwansu yake jiye musu yana ambatawa.

Miqewa Nana tayi a firgice dataji yatambayeta kotasan inda mahaifuyarta take? Baba bansaniba Nima akullum addu'ata a ina xan ganta domin inaji ajikina mamana tanada rai tana numfashi a doron qasa, saidai bansan halinda take cikiba, tafashe da matsanan cin kuka me tsuma xuciya.

Alhaji ne yakatsesu tareda cemusu suyi haquri bari yaje yakarbo musu sallama snn yaxo sutafi gida ayi maganar a nutse, hakan akayi bayan anbata yan magunguna qalilan domin ciwon kan da tace yana damunta.


Isowarsu suka samu Dan uwan Bbn Nana hankalinshi a tashe baiga Dan uwanshi ba, ganin da yayi musu gabadayansu a  tare ya tabbatar mishi akwai muhimmin Abu dayake faruwa wanda shi baida masaniya akai.


Kiranshi Bbn Nana yayi suka shiga babban falo aka zauna, nan Bbn Nana yayi musu bayani akan abunda yasani har yakawo ranar da yaxo gidannan..


Itama NANA cikin kuka ta labarta wa  mahaifinta abunda ya  faru bata boyemishi komai daga ciki ba, saidai ta sirranta cewar gurin manemin mata aka kaita tadai nuna mishi cewar ita Ayashe takaita domin ayi iskanci da ita, Amma Allah ya kubutar da ita a sanadin Alhaji, domin wnn al'amarin sirrin su ne na cikin gida.


Hakan kuwa datayi baqaramin dadi yayiwa wannan ahalin ba, tare kuma daqara mata girma da daraja a idanunsu, harma dai Alhaji Wanda idanunshi kawai yatsareta dashi Wanda babu komai sai zallar qaunarta dakuma taisayinta a cikin su.


Mal.Habu yayi kuka tareda yin Allah ya isa wa Lami da Ayashe na ruguxa mishi xuri'a dasukayi, don acan qasan xuciyarshi bayajin xai iya yafemusu cutar dashi dasukayi, har abada kuwa.


Sunjima a falon suna jajanta abun tareda girmama izza ta ubangiji yanda ya qaddaro haduwar Nana da mahaifinta batare da ansha wahalar komai ba, lallai ba shakka Allah buwayi ne gagara misali.

Shikanshi Dan uwannasa yayi mamaki sosai sannan yakuma iwa Dan uwanshi murna nashigowa farin ciki kashi na daya.


Bbn Nana ne yaqara jawabinsa dacewa, Babu abunda xance daku abisa halaccin da kukai mini, domin kudin halaccinku a gareni fadinshi a fatar baki ba abune mai yiwuwa bane, fatana ubangiji yayi muku mafificin sakayya abisa riqemin yarinya dakukayi da Amana, badon sanadinku ba dabansan wani hali yanxu diyata take ciki ba, yaqarashe maganarsa acikin karyewan murya.


Babu komai ai yiwa Kaine, mai aikinsu ce tashigo dauke da muhsin takaishi ga mahaifiyarshi, itakuwa miqashi tayi gurin mahaifinta snn tace Baba ga yarona cikin jin kunya, Masha Allah shine abunda yayita nanatawa yana kallon Dan yakuma dafa kanshi tareda yimishi addu'oi.


Can Alhaji yacemishi ya shirya gobe da sassafe karyaci komai xa'aje asibiti, godiya yayi har babu iyakanta, ahaka taron ya watse Nana itada mahaifinta jisukeyi tamkar acikin mafarkin irin wannan ranar ta iskosu.



Zarah CE xaune a falo itada IBBY said hiransu sukeyi, tamkar yau ne suka fara sanin junansu,  magana suke tayi akan shirye shiryen bikinsu, dhidai biye mata kawai yakeyi tareda nuna mata duk tsarinda yayi mata shima yayi mishi.


Mummy ce takatse musu hiran ta hangar cewa to Romeo and Juliet dare yayi a tashi aje akwanta, sai kuma gobe a Dora daga inda aka tsaya, IBBY ne yafara miqewa cikin Sosa qeya yayi hanyar waje, zarah ce tafara binshi mummy tace xaki bishi Ku kwana tare ne? Dariya tayi tadawo da gudu tayi dakinta.
Kakkashe wutan falon mummy tayi snn taje takwanta.



&&&&&&&&&&&&&&
Ayashe ce kwance da daddare tana kwance akan makeken gadonta, baccin ta take shararawa hankalinta kwance, amma can tafara juye juye kamar wacce ake qoqarin shaqewa.


Zainabu ce take tsugune a gabanta tana kuka tana roqonta ta taimaka mata, tasan xata iya fiddata acikin quncin da take ciki, amma tajuya baya tanuna baxata taimaka mata ba, hakan yasa zainabun fusata ta fincikota cikin hauka, tareda shaqe mata wuya tana cewa xaki taimakeni ne inkoma gurin 'ya'yana da mijina kokuma saina kasheki? 


Aiko nan tafara kakari tana cewa xan taimakeki, xan taimakeki karki kasheni na tuba, batasan a fili take maganar ba, jin ana tabata ne yasata farkawa a firgice ta qanqamo 'yar dakinnata Wanda ihunta ne a tasota daga nata dakin.


Ruwa, ruwa ruwa taketa ambata aiko 'yar budurwan cikin Sauri tanufi Fridge dinda ke dakin, tamiqo mata goran ruwa, bata jira ta karbi cup din bama ta balle murfin Goran ta Dora abakinta, sai Goran ta yasar.


Kasa maida baccinta tayi maimakon tayi addu'a kokuma tamiqe tayi nafila? Ah ah tashi tayi tahau zarya acikin dakin tasaqa wannan ta war ware wancan.


Gari yana fara haske ta afka bayan gida ta watso ruwa tafito ta shirya a gurguje, batareda ta jira direbanta ba tafigi mota sai cikin daji gurin bokanta, tafiyarda ya kwasheta tsawon Awanni.


A gajiye ta isa cikin dajin, amma dai tayi farin ciki don tasamu ganinsa, nan ta xayyane masa abunda taji na game da mafarkin da tayi a Daren jiya.


Dariya yayi me isarsa snn can ya tsuke fuskarsa yace mata wnn aikin maye ne, shiyayi shi damacan yataba batamin aikina to faduwa yaxo dai dai da xama, yanxu xan fanshe abunda yayi min shekara da shekaru hhhahahahahhahahahahahha nan ya barke da mahaukacin dariya snn.................

Lokaci lokaci Alhaji yakan juyo yana duba halinda take ciki, fahimtar hakan da haj.maryam tayi ne yasata Neman alfarman yayi parking a gefe itakuma takarbi tuqin, hakan kua akayi domin gaba daya yagama zama birkitacce.


Cikin nitsuwa haj.maryam ta isar dasu asibitin, aka dauki Nana aka shigar da ita emergency, nan akasamu aka bata taimakon gaggawa cikin hikimar ubangiji sai gashi tadawo hayyacinta batare da ansha wahala ba, damacan lafiyayya cikin furgici ne ya auku da ita, kamar yanda suka ambata.

Tana farfadowa idanunta tafara saukewa kan Bbn nata tace dashi baba Ashe xanqara ganinka? Hajiya. Alhaji gabadayansu suka kalleta wannan shine Babana, tafada hawaye na gangaro mata akan kuncinta

Sudai Alhaji sun fahimci wnn shine mahaifin Nana, amma sun rasa shikuma menene sanadinsa na fadin maganganun da kunnuwansu yake jiye musu yana ambatawa.

Miqewa Nana tayi a firgice dataji yatambayeta kotasan inda mahaifuyarta take? Baba bansaniba Nima akullum addu'ata a ina xan ganta domin inaji ajikina mamana tanada rai tana numfashi a doron qasa, saidai bansan halinda take cikiba, tafashe da matsanan cin kuka me tsuma xuciya.

Alhaji ne yakatsesu tareda cemusu suyi haquri bari yaje yakarbo musu sallama snn yaxo sutafi gida ayi maganar a nutse, hakan akayi bayan anbata yan magunguna qalilan domin ciwon kan da tace yana damunta.

Hakan kuwa datayi baqaramin dadi yayiwa wannan ahalin ba, tare kuma daqara mata girma da daraja a idanunsu, harma dai Alhaji Wanda idanunshi kawai yatsareta dashi Wanda babu komai sai zallar qaunarta dakuma taisayinta a cikin su.


Mal.Habu yayi kuka tareda yin Allah ya isa wa Lami da Ayashe na ruguxa mishi xuri'a dasukayi, don acan qasan xuciyarshi bayajin xai iya yafemusu cutar dashi dasukayi, har abada kuwa.


Sunjima a falon suna jajanta abun tareda girmama izza ta ubangiji yanda ya qaddaro haduwar Nana da mahaifinta batare da ansha wahalar komai ba, lallai ba shakka Allah buwayi ne gagara misali.

Shikanshi Dan uwannasa yayi mamaki sosai sannan yakuma iwa Dan uwanshi murna nashigowa farin ciki kashi na daya.


Bbn Nana ne yaqara jawabinsa dacewa, Babu abunda xance daku abisa halaccin da kukai mini, domin kudin halaccinku a gareni fadinshi a fatar baki ba abune mai yiwuwa bane, fatana ubangiji yayi muku mafificin sakayya abisa riqemin yarinya dakukayi da Amana, badon sanadinku ba dabansan wani hali yanxu diyata take ciki ba, yaqarashe maganarsa acikin karyewan murya.

Fitowa ta. 5⃣5⃣

A razane ta dago ta kalleshi tareda cemishi cikin firgici, Yanxu wani taimako xaka iya yimini akan wannan al'amarin, kawar da kamshi gefe yayi yace mata babu abunda xanfada miki bayan Wanda na Sanar dake, domin iyakan abunda Nagano knn, ankuma jaddada min idan nacigaba da bincike xai iya zamowa Matsala a gareni dakuma aikina, kinsan dai akanki baxanso komai nawa ya lalace ba ko?


Don haka kitashi kikai kudin aikin ki inda kika saba ajiyewa kibar gurinnan domin inada baqin gaggawa inason ganawa dasu, aiko cikin sauri ta ciro 'yan kudaden taje gaban wani tulu taxubasu ciki snn tafita, kamar ana hankadata haka taita tafiya harta isa gurin motar ta.

Yakamata in bincikota domin nayi mata alqawarin idan nasamu biyan buqata xan aurar da ita. Da wnn tunanin ta cigaba da tuqi harta isa gida.



Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un shine abunda taketa maimaitawa tareda kallon jikinta dakuma inda take, qarshe tamiqe tsaye tana qara duba jikinnata dakyau..

Ganin qofa tayi aiko hakan yasata bin hanyar datagani Wanda take da tabbacin xai sadata da waje, mutane tagani adakunan kallo daya xakai musu kasan ba mutane ne masu hankali ke xaune acikin wannan yanayin ba.


Nurse's ne suka nufota cikin saurin su domin kamota, itako tsabar mamakin dake cinta yahanata qoqarin hanasu abunda take ganin sunada alamar aiwatarwan.


Maidata dakin data fiton sukayi, ganin sun kwantar da itane, suka dauko allura domin yimata, amma xuciyoyin su cike yakeda matuqar mamakin yanda akai, suke aiwatar da komai akanta cikin sauqi Wanda a baya komai xa'ayi sai antaru akanta samada mutane biyar, amma a yau mutane biyu suka shigo da ita daki.

Fitowa ta. 5⃣6⃣



Gaxkiya baxan boye miki ba, Maman larai abunnaki yanxu yafara isata tun banaganin kinada hannu acikin lamarin har nafara xarginki domin a halinda ake ciki yanxu abunnawa yawuce xargi xuciyata tagama ayyanomin gaxkiyan halinda ake ciki.


Hawaye ne yacika mata ido qoqari takeyi domin ganin bata basu daman fitowa ba, duk maganganun da yakeyi idanunta nakanshi har yakai aya snn tace dashi cikin muryan dakanaji kasan xuciyar meshi a karye take.



Haba Bbn larai shekarana nawa ina tare nidake? Baka taba kamani da laifin sa hannu indauka maka komai ba sai yanxu da girma yakamani, kamanta tun bansan darajar rayuwata ba nake tare dakai wannan toxarcin dame yayi kama? Tafashe da kuka mecin rai.


Shiko maimakon yaji xuciyarshi ta karye, a'a sai shedanne yaqara buga mishi ganga akan ai borin kunya kawai takeyi irinna mata masu halin beraye, inba ta raina mishi hankali ba tayaya dagashi sai ita xa'ai tamishi sata babu qaqqautawa? 


Ada yayi tsammanin ko Lami ce amma har lami tabar gidan babu abunda yasauya hakanne ya tabbatar mishi me dakin tashi ne dai ta koyo halin berayen, Wanda shikuwa baxai dauka ba xaiyi maganinta.


Abunda bai saniba shine Lami tayi iyabakacin iyawarta ta kirsa rashin mutunci, toxarta matar baffan sadiyan ,amma hakan baiyi tasiri ba, daga qarshe ma daya fahimci halin da matar tashi take ciki na tsangwaman mai dakin nashi saiya canxa tunani akan yayi saurin nema mata matsuguni duk budirin da akeyi batasani ba.


Sai watarana kawai saiga Isah yaxo, bayan yashigo gari ansanar dashi abunda yafaru shine yanemeta, bayan sungama gaisawa ne yamiqa mata kayayyakin da yataho mata dashi daga birni, suna cikin hirarrakinsu na da da uwa yake sanar da ita cewa ai yasamu matar aure 
, tayi farinciki domin ita hankalinta ma baya gurinshi, hankalinta nakan abun duniyar dataga dannata yatara



Suna zaunen saiga baffan nashi yashigo aiko Isah yatashi yabishi don damage yanason magana dashi, a xaure suka xauna, bayan sungaisa ne Isah ya koro mishi bayani daidai da yanda akai mishi bayani akan mahaifiyarshi dangane da barin gidan mijinta datayi, yaqarshe maganar nashi cikin qunar rai.


Baffan yasha mamakin jin wannan batun, sann  yatausayawa Isah na rashin dacen mahaifiya dayayi, gashi bata karban shawaran kowa, nan shima yakoro mishi matsalar data haddasa dakuma Wanda take qoqarin haddasawa a nashi gidan shima.


Isah ya jinjina al'amarin suna cikin maganar ne taji shirun Isah yayi yawa shine tamiqe tabiyoshi, aiko taimusu lampo tana saurarensu, domin duk a tunanin tabaffan nasu roqon isan xaiyi wani abu,.


_kujimin Lami qaya nutumin da yayi miki karamci, amma bakya fatan a kyautata mishi?_


Nan isah yace da baffanshi yataho da kudade yanason a nema mata matsuguni tabar nan din koda daki dayane ,baffan nashi kuwa dama kamar akanshi take xaune a take ya amince Yakuma nuna mishi maqocinsa na dab da dab, yaqaura kuma dama haya xaisa a gidan don haka kawai sukama.


Isah yayi na'am don haka suka ajiye maganar a haka, Lami taji takaicin hakan, don haka tai kwafa a hankali  tare da cewa inkunsan wata ai bakusan wata ba, don haka takoma tai zaman ta kamar dama can batabar gurin ba.


Basu wuce kwana Biyar da yin maganar ba aka sanar da'ita sunyi mamakin yanda bata nuna damuwa ko Kadan ba, matar baffan sadiyan kuwa kamar antsoma ta a aljanna, haka taita tayata harhada kayanta, xuciyarta fal da farin ciki, itakuwa Lami fada takeyi a ranta xakici ubanki ne.


Tana komawa gidan kua dayake tagama fahimtar gidan takuma karanci duk wani saqo da lungun gidan, shiyasa bai bata wahalan shigo domin yimusu sata ba yanda ranta yakeso tasan babu yanda xa'ayi a zargeta, tunda ba gida daya suke  zaune ba.


Yan xumma dasuke xaune a dakin, taga shigowarshi ne kuma tasan jiya tayi musu barna Wanda bata taba yimusu irinshi ba, shiyasa tabari bayan yashigo da kamar minti biyar tabiyo bayanshi, isowanta taji yana maganar kumacikin daga murya, hakan baqaramin dadi yayi mata ba, takoma gidanta.


Zama tayi tafara tsara yanda xatayi tunda yanxukam a qalla ta Tara kudin da Maye xai iya yimata aiki, kai yanxu kanma ta yanke hukuncin baxataje gurinshi ba domin ta fahimci kudinta kawai yakeci, yakasa yimata komai, tahana kanta cin me dadi kokuma ta kwanta a guri  me kyau, domin kuwa duk abubuwan da Isah yayi mata na gyaran daki kapin tafiyarshi kapin yabar garin saida ya tabbatar ya gyarata snn yatafi bata barshi da komai ba sai kudin mota, amma washegarin tafiyar tashin ta sayar da komai.


 
Yanxu banda tabarma babu komai acikin dakinta, Sai Dan wata tsohuwar gana mazgo Wanda tsummokin ta yake ciki. A waje kuwa daga randa sai 'yan tsirarun kwanukan abinci, komai idan xatayi amfani dashi gidan baffan sadiyan take shiga ta karbo.

Isowarshi gida yaga motar IBBY hakan ya tabbatar mishi dacewa yana tare da zarah kuma yasan gabadayansu ana falon shiyasa yabi da ita taqofar baya, dakin baqi yanuna mata, akan xaije yakira mata matar gidan.

Mummy CE tafara ganinshi tamiqe tareda cemishi daddynsu yaukuma Kaine da shigowa  taqofar baya? Dariya yayi mata snn yabata amsa, Mutum da gidansa baida ikon shigowa ta duk inda yayi niyya? Itanma dariya tayi tace ahh na is a kota sama kaga damar shigowa kashigo kawai nabaka izini domin gida dai nawane, gaba dayansu ne sukai dariya wannan karon, India kana wannan gidan topa koda kapi kashi tsumewa saikayi dariya😜

 Gaidashi sukayi gaba dayansu, banda zarah Wanda saida ta task ta iso gareshi takama hannayenshi snn tagaidashi amsawa yayi snn taimishi wani magana ahankali Wanda shikadai yasan meta gaya mishi, dariya yayi mata snn yace to naji sauna fito, yanufi hanyar dakinshi mummy ma Tamara mishi baya.


Saida suka isa dakin bayan ta tayashi garage kayak jikinshi ne ya dakatar da ita snn yace, nataho miki da baquwa tana dakin baqi, inason kuma kibi komai a hankali.


Itakuwa tsabar gandokin ganin wayene yakeda wannan matsayin da har xai biyo ta gurin mijinta, yataho tareda shi harma kuma yayi mishi masauqi dakanshi, marmarin hakan yahanata tambayarshi kawai takama hanya direct saidakin baqi.


Isarta da sallama ta shiga, amsawa tayi gamida kallon qofar domin ganin wanda yashigo, aiko miqewa tayi tareda nunata, itama mummy zaro idanu tayi tareda rufe qofar ta qaraso gareta, rungumeta tayi tareda farinciki.


Itama Mama rungumarta tayi, domin tanada tabbacin komenene yake faruwa da ita bamai muni bane, don  tasan baiwar Allahn nan bamai cutar da ita bane.



Zama sukayi suna hawaye Mummy CE taketa nanata Allah mungode maka, lallai ubangijinmu madaukakin sarki ne, me warkarwa Aduk lokacinda yaso, mai isa da buwaya, Allah muna qara godemaka take ta fada cikin kuka.


Mama kuwa sai binta takeyi da kallo snn tace inason don qaunarki da mahaliccinki inason kiy gaggawar sanar dani abunda yake faruwa dani.


Ki kwantar da hankalinki..............

Ki kwantar da hankalinki kikuma godewa Allah akan dukkan ni'imomin da yayi miki, xan Sanar dake abunda yake faruwa amma bayanxu ba sai kin Dan huta namiki alqawarin xua dare insha Allah xan sanar dake komai ina fatan kin fahimceni? Taqarasa da qaqalo murmushin qarfin hali.


Itama murmushin tamayar mata Wanda baikai maqoqoranta ba, iyakan labban bakinta ya tsaya, da hakan sukai bankwana bayan ta nuna mata bayan gida da yanda xatai amfani da komai dake ciki,  itakuma tafita domin kawo mata kayan da xata sauya.


Dawowa tayi tasamu daddy a xaune yana waya, jira tayi bayan ya kammala, matsowa tayi kusa dashi snn takama hannayenshi tace ranka ya Dade ya akayi haka tafaru? farinciki kwance akan fuskarta.


Dubanta yayi snn yace kedai mu godewa Allah kawai nan yasanar mata yanda sukayi da likitan, gabadayan su sun jinjina al'amarin qarshe suka ajiye akan bayan sallan isha'i xasu kira meeting din gaggawa.


Assalamu'alaikum da sallamarta tashiga dakin mahaifin nata a zaune bisa darduma ta sameshi da alama shine ya idar da sallan walha, Baba yau banga ka fito ba shine nace gara dai inxo indubaka kodai qafanne?


Murmushin
 yayi snn yace Nana ba qafan bane, qafa kam Alhmdllh domin tun dawowarmu daga asibitin kano naji sauqin sa, snn kinga sikarin ma yanxu dayake ana samun kiyayewa akan harkan abinci snn kuma ga magunguna toshima Alhmdllh yadaina tabani.

To masha Allah tafada, xama tayi suka Dan taba hirarraki tana kuma qara tabbatar mishi Alhaji yaqara tsananta bincike akan Neman mahaifiyarta, yaji dadi sosai duk dama jikinshi a sanyaye yake matuqa, domin akoda yaushe idan yatuna wucewarta ta gabanshi tsikar jikinsa baya daina tashi.


A halinda ake ciki daga Bbn Nana har Dan uwannashi basa harkan gadi, don tuni ansamo musu Sabon mai gadi, amma dayake al'amarin tsofi tobasuda gurin wuni sai can bakin gate din.


Tun Alhaji yana yimusu magana haryagaji yayi shiru ya kyalesu, zama sukeyi na mutuntu juna, duk da madai Bbn Nana yanuna mishi shikam baxai iya zama gida guda da diyarsa ba, domin wnn ba al'adan mal.bahaushe bane, musamman suda suke katsinawa Wanda aka sansu da kara.

_yaukam na yabaku katsinawa, amma xan Dan taboku a gaba_ 😜 lol



Bayan sallan isha'i ne daddy yashigo cikin gidan nashi tareda Bbn IBBY, suna tafiya suna dan tattaunawa akan maganar bikin su Xarah, kagane inji daddy.


Nipa nafison hada bikinnan dana Aliey amma shikuma bayan na tuntubeshi nunamin yayi wacce yakeso bafa bahaushiya bace ma, kai wace qabilace inji Abba.


Wlh wai Igala ce 'yar jihar kogi state ce, a makaranta suka hadu, ikon Allah to Allah yasa Diyar arxiki ce, amma pa taron nan banson musako yara cikinshi, meyasa kace haka?


Koda xamu sako yaran to karya wuce Aliey da Ibby, inyaso sai daga baya sauran yaran xasu sani. Ah ah wannan ba hujja bace duk dama baka fadi hujjar kaba, amma inada tabbacin batada fa'ida inason asanar da kowa kuma kowa yasan matsayinta, ciki kuwa harda diyarta, hakan xai rage mata wasu radadin dayake cin xuciyarta, though bamusan tarihin rayuwarta ba.


To shknn tunda haka kace, yanxu ni bari inkoma naiwa nawa iyalan magana, ahadu a babban falon side dinka, OK sai kun shigo,yakoma gidanshi shikuma daddy yashigo gida, dakin mummy yanufa yasanar da ita yanda sukayi da Bbn ibby nan itama tamiqe tayi dakin zarah tasanar da ita buqatar daddyn ta, snn tayi dakin Aliey kai tsaye.


Samunshi tayi yana zaune akan _sofa_ jallabiya ce a jikinsa kalar ruwan madara, hakan yaxame mishi al'adar shi ce indai yana gida to da magrib shigensa knn, waya yakeyi yana ganinta yakatse kiran tareda cewa mummy kece? 

Bakai tunanin ganina a wnn lokacin  bako? Murmushi yayi mata, zama tayi a gefenshi tace Son saiyaushe ne xaka gabatar da taka matar? Yatsina fuska yayi shikandhi baisan dalili ba tun bayan dawowarshi yaji buqatan auren Siyaman yafice mishi a rai, bayan dayana can kuma har qosawa yayi yadawo gida ya gabatar da ita, ai musu aure sabida yanda yakejinta a ranshi.



Itakanta Siyaman harta gaji dayimishi magiya akan maganar ta haqura, domin yanxu Indai tanason su samu sabani tofa tadago mishi maganar aure, yanxu sai yay mata ba dadi.


Mummy nifa maganar auren nan a barshi kawai baya,xu ba, subhanallah anya ba ruqiyya xansa ai maka ba? Dariya yafara yimata harda kyakyatawa yana kallonta, mummy aljanu ne gareni ko me? To nasani tunda gashi bakason aure? Tabashi amsa snn taqara da cewa kai ko sha'awan IBBY da zarah bakayi? Duk familyn nan waye baisan da xaman soyayyar su ba?


Ai nan ya gimtse dariyarsa kamar ba shine yagama yinta yanxu ba, itako mummy batama kula da fuskartashi ba, domin ita yanxu al'amarin nashi haushi yake bata, kawai tamiqe tasanar dashi saqon daddynsu tai wucewarta.


Jiki ba lakka yamiqe ya shirya yafito xua falon, itakua Xarah gabadaya tsammaninta maganar aurenta xa'ayi itada IBBY, hakan yasata said a ta tsara kwalliyarta tafito Ras da ita, tafito riqe da wayarta maqale a kunnenta da alama waya takeyi domin yanda ta wanxar da annashuwa akan fuskarta, hakan xai tabbatar maka komai dawaye takeyin wayar to tanajin dadin shi.


Tafiyarta takeyi tana yauqi son ranta, hango Aliey tayi shima wayan yakeyi, saitayi tsammanin ko ibbyn ta ne, hakan yasata qara yanga da sauri taqaraso tareda cewa yauwa my paramour kainaketa kira mushiga tare, baxan iya shiga nikadai batare dakai ba, banson kowa yariga ka kallon kwalliya ta, tafada cin muryan shagwaba.


Baisan ya akayi ba yaji wani Abu ya tsirgo mishi tundaga tafin qafarshi had tsakar kansa, amma ya aro dakiya yace mata ke dallah can kimaida hankalinki saiwani rawan kai kikeyi kamar ballagaxa mamakin jin zagin da yake jifanta dashi yasata zaro idanu waje daidai da isowar IBBY gurin, aiko tai maxa ta qarasa gareshi da kukan kirsa domin ko tsilin hawaye baida niyyar xubowa a idanunta.


Shiko magana yafara yimata my star waya tabaki, bayanxun nan muke waya dake ba?  Da Hanunta taimata nuni da Aliey kallon gurinyayi yaga babu kowa, kalli gurin kigani babu kowa pa.



Kallan gurin tayi taqara fashewa da kuka snn tagayamishi abunda Alieyn yayi mata, kama hannunta yayi yana murza yatsunta snn yace mata, ki kwantar da hankalinki idan kowa a duniyar munnan kallon ballagaxa yakemiki to kisa A ranki ni ibraheem kallon tauraruwa nake miki, kinmanta ke kadai nakewa kallon isashshiyar mace , cikakkiya?


Ko kinason kallon wani bayan nawa? Da murmushin tahau Murza idanunta tafara karkada mishi kai, shima murmushin yayi mata yaja hannunta yana gaya mata magana a kunne dariya tafara yimishi tareda cewa banaso.


Suna shigowa falon IBBY ya lalubo Inda Aliey yake, aiko cikin sa'a suka hada ido nan yasakar mishi dankwalo ta qasa yanda bakowane xai gani ba, dagashi sai Xarah itakuwa ganin hakan yasata yimishi gwalo, shikuwa yayi mata alaman xamu hadu..



Misalin qarfe Tara da Rabi ne falon yagama cika da iyalan cikin wannan familyn, ganin kowa yagama hallara ne daddy yakira mummy , cikin minti Biyar suka shigo tareda mamah.


Tana shigowa idanunta yasauqa akan zarah, saida taji gabanta yafadi Wanda batasan dalilin shiba, guri guda suka xauna itada mummy.


Zarah ganin mummy yasata kallon wacce tashigo tareda Mummyn, saida itama gaban nata yafadi batareda ta shiryaba, acikin ranta tace yaukuma meya shigowa da mahaukaciyar nan gidanmu? Kodai tawarke ne?

To meye hadinmu da ita?

Fitowa ta. 5⃣8⃣



Gurinne yayi tsit na Dan wani lokaci kapin Abba yadanyi gyaran murya kadan snn yabude taron da addu'a, bayan yadanyi jawabi akan godiya ga ni'iomamin da ubangiji yayi musu dakuma nasara dasuke fuskanta akullum wayewar gari cikin Ahalinsu.


To dafarko dai xan gabatar mana da baquwa kokuma ince 'yar uwa a cikinmu Wanda kadan daga cikinmu ne basu Santa ba, muna tayata murna akan ni'imar lafiyan da ubangiji yabata, Wanda ba iyawarmu bane kuma ba dabaran mu bane.


Xan Dan mana takaitaccen bayani akan yanda tashigo cikin ahalin mu badon komai ba sai don mu warware mata abunda yashige mata duhu kapin mubata damar sanar damu tarihinta itama a taqaice.


Duk abunnan da akeyi Xarah gabanta tsananta faduwa yakeyi jitakeyi a ranta kamar taron don ita akeyi, shiyasa jefi jefi takan saci kallon matar Wanda itama Mama hakanne a ranta shiyasa takasa dauke idanunta akan xaran, daxaran sun hada idanu Xarah xatai saurin sauqar da idanuwanta qasa.


Haka yadauko labarin tiryan tiryan tundaga randa ya tsinto ta haryaxuwa yanda akayi takoma hannun daddy yasanar dasu baki daya.


Nan daddy ma yadora daga inda Abba ya ajiye kawo xuwa ga yau dasuke xaune a cikin wannan dakin taron,  gaba daya jikinsu ne yayi sanyi banda Xarah datai mutuwar xaune.


Itakuwa mama banda kuka datakeyi kamar ranta zaibar gangan jikinta, dago jajayen idanun ta tayi Wanda suka gama rikidewa daga fari xuwa launin ja. Magana tafarayi cikin kuka godiya tafarayi ba adadi qarshe ta kalli Xarah Wanda itama idanunta nakanta amma a xahiri ruhinta baya tartare dasu, tagama tsunduma cikin kogin tunani.


Mummy ce takamo hannun Xarah ta sarqe shi cikin na mama Wanda ya farkar da Xarah daga halinda take ciki, hawaye masu xafi ne suka gangaro mata.


Xarah tabbas kicire shakku a cikin ranki wannan itace mahaifiyarki ta asali, kinji yanda akai kika zama 'yata, najima ina gayamiki tanada muhimmanci a rayuwar ki, najima ina sanar dake tanada alaqa babba dangane da aurenki to yau duk kinji dalili na ko? Taqarashe maganarta cikin kuka.



Kasa magana Xarah tayi, ba ita kadai ba kowa a falon jikinshi a sanyaye yake, masu raunin xuciya daga cikinsu kuwa sunjima da fara kuka, Ibby a nashi bangaren jiyakeyi kamar yatashi ya rungumota amma babu dama.


Mama ce tai saurin janye hannunta tareda kamo hannun mummy ta sarqe shi Dana Xarah, cikin kuka tafara yin magana cikin girgixa kanta.


Ah ah karkiyi min haka Xarah 'yar kice nidai nahaifa miki ne, bansan komai na dangane da rayuwarta ba, asali ma bansan ta cikin rayuwata ba, nasameta acikin hankali amma na rayu da ita cikin hauka, na haifeta a hauka, an reneta ina cikin hauka, tayaya rana daya xan karbeta daga hannun nagartattun mutane Wanda sukaimin halacci in rabata dasu?



Baxan yimuku butulci ba, koda baxan qara saka kwayar idanuna akan Ahalina ba, to ina tabbatar muku nabar muku Xarah, maganganu sunyi kadan gurin nuna godiyata a bisa tallafawa rayuwata dakukayi, fatana ubangiji ya biyaku.


Kamo hannun Xarah tayi snn tace mata, nice nahaifeki amma banice mahaifiyarki ba, wannan itace mamanki yanxu xan sanar dake ya akayi mukaxo wannan matakin.


Gyara zamanta tayi tareda share hawayen fuskanta tace Sunana Zainab..................



Bata boye musu komai ba nadaga cikin abunda tasani xuwa lokacinda taji kamar an kwala kiran sunanta a cikin dakin, takai qarshen labarin da fashewa da matsanancin kuka.


Xarah ce tai saurin rungumeta tana kuka tana fadin, lallai mama kinsha gararin rayuwa tabbas baxamu taba yafewa Ayashe da Lami ba, wayyo yanxu ina Yaya Nanah a wani hali take ciki, wayyo mahaifina a ina xanganka. Ko kanada rai? Koko sun halaka ka? Allah kadadani da 'yan uwana.


Mama tacireta daga jikinta tace babban abunda xakiymin a duniya kisakamin a visa haihuwar ki danayi shine banason har xuwa qarshen rayuwarki ki bijirewa umarnin wadannan bayin Allahn domin sunmana halacci, baxan yafe miki ba matsawar kika saba musu akan umarnin daxasu baki, indai bai sabawa addini ba.


Girgixa kai kawai takeyi kamar kadangaruwa domin magana kam baxai samu fitowa daga bakinta ba, saboda kuka yagamacin qarfinta, kowa a cikin dakin taro xuciyarshi tagama cika fal da tausayinsu.


Bayan an Dan tsagaita da jajen ne daddy yayiwa mama bayanin xuri'arsu yakuma gabatar mata da kowa a cikin falon, snn a qarshe yasanar mata da maganar auren Xarah Wanda akasa shi nan da watanni biyu masu xuwa in Allah ya yarda, domin A da abunda yasa suketa jinkiri akan maganar auren, saboda lafiyarta ne, amma tunda yanxu lafiya tasamu xa'ayishi cikin kwanciyar hankali.


Murmushin jin dadi tayi tareda da sa albarka akan maganar auren,  xuciyoyin mutane biyunnan kuwa doki ne kawai yagama cikata.


Sunjima xaune anata tattauna abunda yashafi wnn matsalar, daddy ne yayi tambaya akan akwai mai wata magana ne acikinsu?


Xarah ce tayi karaf tace daddy pls inason aje garinsu mama adubo mata 'yan uwanta, dariya sukayi dukansu snn daddy yace mata yarinyan daddy babu ko kunya? Kodai xaki gudu manane? Dariya tayi ta boye fuskanta ajikin Mummyn ta, yace mata ki kwantar da hankalinki gobe idan Allah yakaimu xamuje adubosu, kowa yaji dadin hikuncin da daddyn ya yanke, da haka aka rufe taro da addu'a kowa ya watse.



Ayashe ce tasauqa a garin katsina gidan Alhajin da takai Nana tawuce direct, tasamu ganinsa kuwa domin yanxu tawuce matsayin aita wulaqan tata kapin tasamu ganin wani, ayanxu itama hajiyar kanta ce.


Bayan sungaisa yake tambayar ta yabayan rabuwa, keko haka akeyi nariga nasanki da iya samo danyu nimapa da hakan nakesamun nawa, snn kalolin nawan ma kwana biyu baki kawomin ko guda daya ba, kinbarni inata fama da wannan niban taba haduwa da mace jarababbiya marar ruwa ba sai wnn.


Dariya Ayashe taimishi me isarta snn tace mishi duk pa kai kajawa kanka, kullum ina gaya mata kafara harka da yara, amma kaqi Sam, hummmm ni yanxu gabadaya nagama kwadaituwa dake, yafadi yana lashe leben bakinshi kamar wani tsohon maye.


Jin haka yasata yin fari da idanunta tareda juya jikinta, tace ni ba wannan ne yakawo niba nataba kawo maka wata yarinya shekaru uku dasuka wuce idan xaka iya tunawa tun kapin in bar gari.


Shiru yayi nadan wani lokaci yana nazari, to ai matanne dayawa tayaya xan iya tunowa da ita? Sunan yarinyar Nana snn nasamu labarin tajima a wannan gidan naka kapin kafitar da ita

Exactly natuno da ita, lallai Ayashe wato tun bakida kudi kepa ja'irar kanki ce, nan ya kwashe mata yanda sukayi da Alhajin da yakai Nana gidansa, snn yaqara mata da cewa yanxu bansan Yaya suka qare ba.


Dafe kanta tayi tace washh wlh yanxu alqawarin da naiwa yarinyar nan ne yatsayamin a wuya, inason insauqe mata shi, babu damuwa xan taimakamiki harki samota, amma kinsan yanayin ai bani in bakane kingane yakashe mata ido daya.


Nan suka fara maganganunsu irinna fitsara a take suka tsara yanda xasu aiwatar da komai, tareda ajiye gobe a matsayin ranar da xai kaita gurin Alhajin.


*WASHE GARI*

Washe gari dasafe suka shirya suka dau hanyar katsina ana tafiya Mama tanata kallon hanya har suka isa, da kwatance har suka isa gidan, gani tayi komai ya canxa mata a cikin garin harma da unguwar.


Kamar yanda xuwansu Nana yakasance to wannan karon xuwansu Mama abun bai canxa zani ba, domin kuwa Alhajin dayake xaune a gidan bai boye musu komai ba, nadaga abunda yasani, domin shi yanxu yafara qosawa da irin binciken da ake mishi akan wannan gidan, saikace Wanda yasayi gidan gwamnati. Yayi Neman Ayashe Neman duniya baisamu ganinta ba, koxai Dan huce takaicin abunda taimishi.


Haka suka bari garin amma xuciyam mama tagama karyewa gani takeyi tarasa mijinta dakuma tilon diyarta, a qarshe ma kukane ya kufce mata, akai ta lallashinta har aka samu aka Dan kwantar mata da hankalinta.......

Fitowa ta. 5⃣9⃣



Cikin dare bayan komai ya lafa shikanshi yasan baqaramin ruda Ayashe yayi da salonsa dayake amfani gurin lalata tunanin tsoffin mata irinta ba.


Murmushi yayi  a xuciyarsa yace tayaya ma. Xa'ayi inyarda yaushe kika fara Harkan muda muke cikinta shekara da shekaru bamuy arxikinda kika yiba, amma ke cikin qanqanin lokaci kina qoqarin ninka arxiki na.


Hade fuska yayi snn yace xanyi amfani da jarabarki tunda ke indai namiji yayi miki kaca kaca to xa'aje dashi, xansan yanda xanye inkwashe abunda yake gareki kapin insan inda dare yayi mini. Murmushin mugunta yayi yakalli inda take tareda yatsina fuskarsa kamar mace snn yace ga mace har mace amma babu dandano ko kadan mtswww.......


Washe gari saida ya tabbatar yaqara tsumata da tsuminshi snn yanemi shawaranta akan yanason suhada kai gurin daukaka sana'ar su yanda xasu gudu tare su tsira tare.


Yacigaba mata da bayanin yanda xata ke kawo masa yara shikuma yana kaisu wa Alhaxawa ana biyanshi suna raba kudin tare.


Dayake mace CE mai qaramar kwakwalwa batai dogon naxari ba, nan ta amince mishi shiko  ganin haka yayita zura ta, taita fadawa cikin ramin muguntar daya Gina mata.


Baibarta haka ba saida yayi mata alqawarin aure inda yanuna mata dadin data jiyar dashine yasashi jin baxai iya taraiya dawata mace bayan itaba. Da wadannan xantukan yagama saye xuciyarta, nan batare da tunanin komai ba tasakar mishi sirrinta dakuma ragamar rayuwarta.  _xakiy batani ae_


&&&&&&&&&&&&

Lami ce tashirya tsaf tai shirinta domin ta Dana tarko wa matar baffan Sadiya, saboda tun safe tashiga kamar yanda tasaba, cikin dabara tadan bugi ruwan cikinta, aiko tayi sa'a tasamu xataje dubiya mahaifinta bashida lfy an aiko mata da dare.


Amma abunda bata saniba shine har matar baffan sadiyan tagama shirinta, tarufe taga ta tattara tsakar gidanta harma tafito saiga baffan Sadiya aiko nan yace da ita babu inda xataje yafasa, domin a halinda yake ciki haryanxu haushinta yakeji, baida burin faranta mata rai ko qanqani.


Taji takaicin abunda ya aikata a gareta, dalilin hakan ko mayafin bata cire ba, tafada kan gadon qarfen dake dakin nata, kaxalika ko tagan bata bude ba, baxaka taba cewa akawai bil'adama acikin dakin ba.


 Kai tsaye bayan shigowanta tadanyi bincike kamar yanda tasaba, taci sa'a miyan tankwa tayi harda Dan naman ta aciki, babu kunya tajuye shi a Leda, harda jefa guda daya acikin bakinta tana tauna tanai musu xagin qare dangi.


Shigewa tayi dakin batare da tsoro kokuma tunanin komai ba, gurin data saba daukan kudin tanufa, dayake dakin da duhu batasan tayi tuntube da kwanon Silba ba, Wanda shine ya farkar da Mmn larai a Dan gajeren baccin daya saceta.


Ganin mutum a tsakiyar dakin yana hincike ne yabata tsoro kamar xatai ihu kuma dai saitahau salati acikin xuyciyarta takame bakinta tai gum domin Kuwa  tanason ganin yanda qarshen abun xai qare.



Cixon yatsa tayi ganin babu kudi a wannan gurin, kamar ta fice sainaga ta juya, sakamakon tunawa datayi maxa nabarin kudi a jikin kayansu kamar dai yanda tasaba yiwa Mal.Habu barna.


Haka taita bin aljihunayen da lalubi ana qarshen ne domin harta fidda rai taci karo da kudi Wanda a qalla idan xan iya qiyasin su, xasukai dubu sha uku.


Farinciki ne ya mamaye xuciyarta, nan tasa kudin ta qulle a bakin xaninta, tasa qafa a xatayi waje, kamar anduro da matar baffan Sadiya daga kan gadon, haka tai tsalle ta cafketa tare da hankada ta qasa tai saurin bude tagan nan haske ya bayyana a dakin.


Wazata gani😳 gaba dayansu tsoro ne qarara ya bayyana a fuskokin su amma kowa da irin nashi, cikin inda inda lami tahada hannu biyu tareda cewa kiymin rai karki sanar da kowa namiki alqawarin baxan sakeba, kiyafeni.


Bata samu xarafin magana ba sakamakon sallamar baffan Sadiya daya yunkaro bakin dakin, yana shigowa yaga Lami abun tausayi, menene yake faruwa? 


Koban sanar dakai a bakina ba kasan abunda xaikawo lami tashigo uwar dakina, kuma a ganta a durqushe tofa ba qaramin Abu bane, snn kuma bamai dadi bace , kapin in Sanar da mijina kiy gaggawan gayamishi abunda kika aikata kokuma ince kike aikatawa domin cutar  dani.


Lami ce tafashe da kyka cikin kirsa tafara fadin, yanxu saboda Allah mmn larai saboda kin kirani inbaki d
Shawara shine xakiymin sharri, maganar takima  kwata kwata bansan inda ta dosa ba, Allah sarki mutuwa nidai narasa gata, tafada tareda fashewa da kukan kirsa.



Cikin tsoro da mamaki mmn larai tasake baki tana kallon lami, maida idonta tayi kan mijinta abunda ta karanto a fuskanshi baxai xama alheri a gareta ba, domin tagama lura xai aminta da Lami fiye da ita, Wanda a qarshe tayi sanadiyyar mutuwan igiyan aurenta domin yanxu haka lilo yakeyi.



Hakane yasata yin kukan kura dama gata bakadan ba, tahaye kan Lami itama tana kuka tanakuma Kai mata duka duk a lokaci daya, Shiga tsakiyansu yasoyi amma abun yaci tura, abunda yabashi mamaki shine a iya xamanshi da mmn larai bai taba ganinta tayi fada da wani ba, amma yau itace da bala'i.



Baqaramin wahala yashaba kapin yasamu yadaga ta akan Lami, hakanne ya tabbatar mishi tanada gaxkiya domin ba mafadaciya bace, Lami kuwa gata shame shame a kwance baki duk jini, har haqori guda daya yasamu xiyartar qasa, domin da dukan qarfi ya fita.



Tambayarta yakeyi amma tsabar ta hau tama kasa bashi amsa,  lallai ya kuma tabbatar wa ankaita bamgo yanxu kam, saida yanata ruwan Randa me sanyi sannan yasamu ta Samar mishi da abunda yafaru ga a daya takuma kwanto bakin xanin Lami saiga kudade sun fado.



Salati yayi gamida runtse idanu domin takaicin abunda kunnuwansa suke jiye mishi, yajima yanabinta da kallo Wanda itakuma batamasan yanayi ba, domin ga wahalan ciwon jiki dayake damunta gakuma kunya daya gama baibayeta.



Tsawa yadaka mata, q firgice ta dago kumburarriyan fuskanta ta kalleshi, banyi tsammanin acikin munanan halayyarki akwai halin bera acikin ba, ba shakka yaya na dakuma Mal.Babu baqananan jarumai bane, sun cancanci lambar yabo, ace kinai musu wannan halin amma har kikabar hannunsu bawanda yataba jiba.


Gaxkiya Lami kinbani kunya matuqa, amma kisani a dalilin rabani da matata dakikaso Wanda muka faro tafiyar mu tun yaranta, kika tarwatsa farincikin gidana, wlh babu ni babu ke.


Damacan  banida alaqa dake, dalilin yara ne, kuma yanxu batanan, Wanda yake rayen kuma yasan abunda yakeyi domin yanada nitsuwa, don haka lami wlh babu ni babu ke, kisani ko ahanya kika nuna kinsanni wlh saina bata miki kamannin fuskan ki, domin baxan xauna damecin dunduniya ta ba.



Roqonshi tafarayi domin yanxu shine gatan ta, idan yarabu ca ita shknn tarasa *TUDUN DAFAWA* don haka ta tsananta kukanta amma yayi Burus saima qara harxuqa dayayi yajata ya wurga waje yarufe qofan gidanshi....



Ganin datayi mutane. Asu iya Ankara yasata Jan jikinta a hankali tayi cikin nata gidan,xama tayi tafara kukan takaici dakuma Dana sani.



Haka rayuwar taita tafiya, yau saura kwana takwas auren Xarah da IBBY, dokin auren dasukeyi bawa kowa mamaki yakeyi musamman ibby, wanda  xaran dakanta takance mishi ya rage domin abunnashi ita kunya da tsoro yake bata..
 


Yaune suka fara Rabon I.V tare sukeyin kayansu gwanin sha'awa xaikaita gidan su qawarta yajirata tafito sutafi, har kirari ake musu da *ga zarah ga wata* sukanyi dariya su wuce.


Suna hanya tace mishi my paramour muje gidan Auntyn Yasmin, dayake gidan ummanshi ne kai tsaye suka wuce domi shima yama buqatar yaje ya gaidata, duk dama yanajin nauyinta domin  yaqi diyarta yanace yace sai *ZARAH*


_Masu suna Fatyma farin Jini garesu_😜
