Fitowa ta. 5⃣8⃣



Gurinne yayi tsit na Dan wani lokaci kapin Abba yadanyi gyaran murya kadan snn yabude taron da addu'a, bayan yadanyi jawabi akan godiya ga ni'iomamin da ubangiji yayi musu dakuma nasara dasuke fuskanta akullum wayewar gari cikin Ahalinsu.


To dafarko dai xan gabatar mana da baquwa kokuma ince 'yar uwa a cikinmu Wanda kadan daga cikinmu ne basu Santa ba, muna tayata murna akan ni'imar lafiyan da ubangiji yabata, Wanda ba iyawarmu bane kuma ba dabaran mu bane.


Xan Dan mana takaitaccen bayani akan yanda tashigo cikin ahalin mu badon komai ba sai don mu warware mata abunda yashige mata duhu kapin mubata damar sanar damu tarihinta itama a taqaice.


Duk abunnan da akeyi Xarah gabanta tsananta faduwa yakeyi jitakeyi a ranta kamar taron don ita akeyi, shiyasa jefi jefi takan saci kallon matar Wanda itama Mama hakanne a ranta shiyasa takasa dauke idanunta akan xaran, daxaran sun hada idanu Xarah xatai saurin sauqar da idanuwanta qasa.


Haka yadauko labarin tiryan tiryan tundaga randa ya tsinto ta haryaxuwa yanda akayi takoma hannun daddy yasanar dasu baki daya.


Nan daddy ma yadora daga inda Abba ya ajiye kawo xuwa ga yau dasuke xaune a cikin wannan dakin taron,  gaba daya jikinsu ne yayi sanyi banda Xarah datai mutuwar xaune.


Itakuwa mama banda kuka datakeyi kamar ranta zaibar gangan jikinta, dago jajayen idanun ta tayi Wanda suka gama rikidewa daga fari xuwa launin ja. Magana tafarayi cikin kuka godiya tafarayi ba adadi qarshe ta kalli Xarah Wanda itama idanunta nakanta amma a xahiri ruhinta baya tartare dasu, tagama tsunduma cikin kogin tunani.


Mummy ce takamo hannun Xarah ta sarqe shi cikin na mama Wanda ya farkar da Xarah daga halinda take ciki, hawaye masu xafi ne suka gangaro mata.


Xarah tabbas kicire shakku a cikin ranki wannan itace mahaifiyarki ta asali, kinji yanda akai kika zama 'yata, najima ina gayamiki tanada muhimmanci a rayuwar ki, najima ina sanar dake tanada alaqa babba dangane da aurenki to yau duk kinji dalili na ko? Taqarashe maganarta cikin kuka.



Kasa magana Xarah tayi, ba ita kadai ba kowa a falon jikinshi a sanyaye yake, masu raunin xuciya daga cikinsu kuwa sunjima da fara kuka, Ibby a nashi bangaren jiyakeyi kamar yatashi ya rungumota amma babu dama.


Mama ce tai saurin janye hannunta tareda kamo hannun mummy ta sarqe shi Dana Xarah, cikin kuka tafara yin magana cikin girgixa kanta.


Ah ah karkiyi min haka Xarah 'yar kice nidai nahaifa miki ne, bansan komai na dangane da rayuwarta ba, asali ma bansan ta cikin rayuwata ba, nasameta acikin hankali amma na rayu da ita cikin hauka, na haifeta a hauka, an reneta ina cikin hauka, tayaya rana daya xan karbeta daga hannun nagartattun mutane Wanda sukaimin halacci in rabata dasu?



Baxan yimuku butulci ba, koda baxan qara saka kwayar idanuna akan Ahalina ba, to ina tabbatar muku nabar muku Xarah, maganganu sunyi kadan gurin nuna godiyata a bisa tallafawa rayuwata dakukayi, fatana ubangiji ya biyaku.


Kamo hannun Xarah tayi snn tace mata, nice nahaifeki amma banice mahaifiyarki ba, wannan itace mamanki yanxu xan sanar dake ya akayi mukaxo wannan matakin.


Gyara zamanta tayi tareda share hawayen fuskanta tace Sunana Zainab..................



Bata boye musu komai ba nadaga cikin abunda tasani xuwa lokacinda taji kamar an kwala kiran sunanta a cikin dakin, takai qarshen labarin da fashewa da matsanancin kuka.


Xarah ce tai saurin rungumeta tana kuka tana fadin, lallai mama kinsha gararin rayuwa tabbas baxamu taba yafewa Ayashe da Lami ba, wayyo yanxu ina Yaya Nanah a wani hali take ciki, wayyo mahaifina a ina xanganka. Ko kanada rai? Koko sun halaka ka? Allah kadadani da 'yan uwana.


Mama tacireta daga jikinta tace babban abunda xakiymin a duniya kisakamin a visa haihuwar ki danayi shine banason har xuwa qarshen rayuwarki ki bijirewa umarnin wadannan bayin Allahn domin sunmana halacci, baxan yafe miki ba matsawar kika saba musu akan umarnin daxasu baki, indai bai sabawa addini ba.


Girgixa kai kawai takeyi kamar kadangaruwa domin magana kam baxai samu fitowa daga bakinta ba, saboda kuka yagamacin qarfinta, kowa a cikin dakin taro xuciyarshi tagama cika fal da tausayinsu.


Bayan an Dan tsagaita da jajen ne daddy yayiwa mama bayanin xuri'arsu yakuma gabatar mata da kowa a cikin falon, snn a qarshe yasanar mata da maganar auren Xarah Wanda akasa shi nan da watanni biyu masu xuwa in Allah ya yarda, domin A da abunda yasa suketa jinkiri akan maganar auren, saboda lafiyarta ne, amma tunda yanxu lafiya tasamu xa'ayishi cikin kwanciyar hankali.


Murmushin jin dadi tayi tareda da sa albarka akan maganar auren,  xuciyoyin mutane biyunnan kuwa doki ne kawai yagama cikata.


Sunjima xaune anata tattauna abunda yashafi wnn matsalar, daddy ne yayi tambaya akan akwai mai wata magana ne acikinsu?


Xarah ce tayi karaf tace daddy pls inason aje garinsu mama adubo mata 'yan uwanta, dariya sukayi dukansu snn daddy yace mata yarinyan daddy babu ko kunya? Kodai xaki gudu manane? Dariya tayi ta boye fuskanta ajikin Mummyn ta, yace mata ki kwantar da hankalinki gobe idan Allah yakaimu xamuje adubosu, kowa yaji dadin hikuncin da daddyn ya yanke, da haka aka rufe taro da addu'a kowa ya watse.



Ayashe ce tasauqa a garin katsina gidan Alhajin da takai Nana tawuce direct, tasamu ganinsa kuwa domin yanxu tawuce matsayin aita wulaqan tata kapin tasamu ganin wani, ayanxu itama hajiyar kanta ce.


Bayan sungaisa yake tambayar ta yabayan rabuwa, keko haka akeyi nariga nasanki da iya samo danyu nimapa da hakan nakesamun nawa, snn kalolin nawan ma kwana biyu baki kawomin ko guda daya ba, kinbarni inata fama da wannan niban taba haduwa da mace jarababbiya marar ruwa ba sai wnn.


Dariya Ayashe taimishi me isarta snn tace mishi duk pa kai kajawa kanka, kullum ina gaya mata kafara harka da yara, amma kaqi Sam, hummmm ni yanxu gabadaya nagama kwadaituwa dake, yafadi yana lashe leben bakinshi kamar wani tsohon maye.


Jin haka yasata yin fari da idanunta tareda juya jikinta, tace ni ba wannan ne yakawo niba nataba kawo maka wata yarinya shekaru uku dasuka wuce idan xaka iya tunawa tun kapin in bar gari.


Shiru yayi nadan wani lokaci yana nazari, to ai matanne dayawa tayaya xan iya tunowa da ita? Sunan yarinyar Nana snn nasamu labarin tajima a wannan gidan naka kapin kafitar da ita

Exactly natuno da ita, lallai Ayashe wato tun bakida kudi kepa ja'irar kanki ce, nan ya kwashe mata yanda sukayi da Alhajin da yakai Nana gidansa, snn yaqara mata da cewa yanxu bansan Yaya suka qare ba.


Dafe kanta tayi tace washh wlh yanxu alqawarin da naiwa yarinyar nan ne yatsayamin a wuya, inason insauqe mata shi, babu damuwa xan taimakamiki harki samota, amma kinsan yanayin ai bani in bakane kingane yakashe mata ido daya.


Nan suka fara maganganunsu irinna fitsara a take suka tsara yanda xasu aiwatar da komai, tareda ajiye gobe a matsayin ranar da xai kaita gurin Alhajin.


*WASHE GARI*

Washe gari dasafe suka shirya suka dau hanyar katsina ana tafiya Mama tanata kallon hanya har suka isa, da kwatance har suka isa gidan, gani tayi komai ya canxa mata a cikin garin harma da unguwar.


Kamar yanda xuwansu Nana yakasance to wannan karon xuwansu Mama abun bai canxa zani ba, domin kuwa Alhajin dayake xaune a gidan bai boye musu komai ba, nadaga abunda yasani, domin shi yanxu yafara qosawa da irin binciken da ake mishi akan wannan gidan, saikace Wanda yasayi gidan gwamnati. Yayi Neman Ayashe Neman duniya baisamu ganinta ba, koxai Dan huce takaicin abunda taimishi.


Haka suka bari garin amma xuciyam mama tagama karyewa gani takeyi tarasa mijinta dakuma tilon diyarta, a qarshe ma kukane ya kufce mata, akai ta lallashinta har aka samu aka Dan kwantar mata da hankalinta.......

Amarya yau kece a gidan namu, saura qiris nai fushi nace baxani ba, domin naji labarin kunfara rabon kati amma banga nawa ba, dariya tayi tace mata kiyi haquri kina raina wlh shiyasa yau muna fitowa nace takanki xan fara biyowa.

Ayya amma dai baxai hanaku xuwa nawa ba ko? Murmushi taimata bata gajiya da kallon zarah domin tanajinta har cikin ranta, ai ana bikin magaji ake na magajiya, insha Allah babu abunda xai hanani xuwa aurenki.


Sunjima suna hira har kuwa Nana ke sanar da ita mahaifinta yana gidan yaxo, domin bata taba Sanar da ita tarihin rayuwarta ba, wannan sirrinta ne, duk da wasu lokacin takanji buqatar hakan yana taso mata.


Har ibby yagaji da jiranta yai kiranta a waya, fitowa sukayi tare da Nanan falon Haj.Maryam suka shiga cikin jin kunya Xarah ta gaidata amsawa tayi cikin sakin fuska tare da cewa Nana nikam sauda dama nakan hango kamanni tsakaninki da 'yar dakin kinnan.

Dariya sukayi gabadayansu ibby yace wlh nima nagani, Nana ce tabasu amsa da cewa shaquwarce tai yawa ina fatan baxakai min rowanta bayan karaba mu da ita ba, Insha Allah yafada yana miqewa sukai musu rakiya suka wuce.


A hanya yakecewa da ita My star ya akayi ne nikam kika saba da matar nan? Dariya taimishi snn tabashi labarin yanda suka saba da Nana, ya sunanta yajefe ta dawani tambayan, Tabdi ni wlh bansan asalin sunanta ba, Aunty amarya kawai nake ce mata. Da wannan makamantan hirarrakinsu sukaita yawo a gari, qarshe suka zarto gida.

Lami ce kwance akan Dan yadin tabarman ta, duniya taimata qullin goro, tarasa yaxatayi da rayuwarta domin ko daga yatsanta ta motsa shi ya zame mata aiki, babu mai taimaka ma, intana jin fitsari haka take Jan jikinta tayi a gefe,koko kashi dakinta kam Sam baxa'a iya shiganshi ba domin ko ina wari yagama dashi.



Tarasa meyake damunta domin bata iya dukan nan bane yaja mata wannan bala'in amma shine sanadin komai datake ji a gangan jikinta.



Yau nema tadanji dama dama, harta fito bakin qofan dakinta, jan jikinta tayi tadebi ruwa tasha aiko nan cikinta yanemi qullewa domin babu komai acikin sa, yunwa yagama cinyeta inka kalleta xakai tunanin shekara goma tayi tana fama da cuta.



Amma dayake macece marar tunani tanacin qanxo tadan ji qarfin jikinta aiko ta tattari kudaden tayi gidan sabuwar uwar dakinta datayi hatsabibiya wacce tasan kan bibiyar bokaye, agurin Maye suka hadu da ita.



Anace da ita Mama Attu taje ta tsigale Maye ciki da waje tareda yimishi  alqawarin itace ajalinsa, domin taje bokannta yasanar da ita ankarya mishi laqaninsa amma bai daina rudan mutane yana karban kudi a hannun su ba.



Hakan da Lami tagani shiyasa ta qoqarin shiga jikin Mama Attu, dakyar ta amince amma saida taiduba da irin rashin wayon Lami tasan xataci ajikinta.



A qofar gida tasameta cikin shiganta na fita anguwa domin harda jaka a hannunta, ganin Lami yasata sassauta fuskanta Wanda da yake a tamke kamar hadari.


Gaisawa sukayi tareda tambayar Lami din hatsari kikayi ne naganki bakyan gani? Dayake ita bawai ta iya duniyar bane nan ta kwashe komai tasanar da Attu, a xuciyar attu tace faduwa yaxo daidai da xama, xan ribantu dake.



A zahiri saita sako fuskar tausayi da jimami ta sanyawa fuskarta, ayya sannu amma yanxu kam anxo wajen don yanxu haka Nima ganina dakikayi gurin malami na xani akan wata matsala data kunnomini, kinga saiki miqo naki aikan in hada duka ingabatar mishi dasu.



Lami har cikin xuciyarta bata gama aminta da shawaran Attu ba, hakan yasa cikin jimami ta tambayeta baxai yiwu muje tare bane?


Ahh baxai yiwu domin ni kaina bawai ina samun ganinsa bane, Yaya tah nake baiwa saqon ta Isar gareshi nidai nawa amfani da magani ne, inkuma kinaga baki amince ba saikiyi gaba domin ni sauri nakeyi, koda taji haka tai saurin xaro kudaden.


Ganin kudin yasa Attu sakin ajiyar xuciya ahankali, Lami ta qirga kudi cass dubu saba'in da bakwai ta baiwa Attu, itakuma tasa hannu takarba sukayi bankwana akan jibi xata kawo mata magunguna.



Har ta bule Lami bata daina waiwayenta ba, haka taji a ranta kamar Attu batada gaxkiya amma saita daure tasawa xuciyarta amincewa da Attun.


Itakuwa Attu tana isa Tasha tashige motar garinsu wato yola, saida tafiya tafara nisa snn tadauko kudin taimusu kyakykyawar ma'ajiya snn, ta bushe da dariya a xuciyarta tace lallai kinyi ragon Axanci.


Har akai kwana uku Lami batani labarin Attu ba, haka yasa jiki babu qarfi ta shirya tafita domin xuwa gidanta, tana fita yayi daidai da fitowar Baffan sadiya, aiko cikin faduwar gaba haryana tuntube takoma ta labe tabayan qofa, domin bata manta kashedin dayay mata ba.


Saida ta tabbatar ya fita snn itama tarufa mishi baya,  tana tafiya tana tufka da warwara, har ta is a gidan Attu, ganin cicirindon mutane yasata jin faduwar gananta ya tsananta, da sanyin jiki ta qarasa gurin mutanen.


Samun daya saga cikin mutanen dake gurin tayi ta tambayeta meke faruwa, tana haki cikin fushi take bata labarin abunda yake faruwa akan, kudin Adashe ne suke Tarawa a hannun Attu a matsayinta na uwar Adashe, Ashe macuciya ce domin ta tattara kudin al'umma sama da dubu Dari tayi gaba, takuma tattara komai nata annemeta an rasa.


Ai lami bats qarasa jin sauran bayanan ba sakamakon jirin daya kwasheta yazubata da qasa aiko nan mutane sukaiyo kanta , ruwa aka kwaso aka fara yayyafa mata, amma tana farfadowa idan ta kallesu sai kaji tace Attu Attu, saita qara sumewa... 


Wata daga cikin gidan hayan da Attu taxauna, taimata alfarma akan ashigo da ita ciki domin tadan huta ko hankalinta xai dawo jikinta, haka akai tangare gare da Lami aka shigar da ita ciki.

   _WNN KNN_


Yaune Alhaji yagama shiryawa Ayashe gadar xare shiyasa ya amince domin yakaita gidan Alhaji, badon tasamu wani Abu ba, saidon a rufta da ita. Domin yaji komai dangane da yarinyar da tabashi yakaiwa Alhajin a bakinta.



Bayan sun isa ne, taga ai tasan gidan ma, domin ta taba xua gidan lokacin ana biki, amma saita boye mamakin ta cikin salon iya bariki tace, wato Alhajin nan ma yasan harka knn?


Dariya yayi mata snn yace yanxu kuma wa aka bari a baya? Dukansu dukai dariya, ce mata tayi tashiga yayiwa Alhajin bayaninta tace kawai tana Neman Amarya, domin agurinta yarinyar take..

Jin haka kanta tsaye ta wuce cikin gidan bata nemi akaita ba domin tasan side din Amaryan, sabida haka da sallama tashiga.

Mummyn Fadyl CE

Fitowa ta. 6⃣1⃣


Da sallama Ayashe tashiga, itakuwa Nana tanacan dakinta jin sallama yasata amsawa tare da fadin inaxua, jin an amsa yasa lami yiwa kanta mazauni a daya daga cikin kujerun  dake malale a cikin falon.


Baquwa mukayi........... Maganarta ne yamaqale a fatar bakunta domin arba datayi da fuskar Ayashe, a bangaren Ayashe kuwa motsi ma gagaranta yayi a gurinda take xaune.


A fusge tace meya kawoki nan gurin Nana, dakewa Nana tayi cikin qarfin xuciya tace keya kamata intambaya mekike min a gida, tayaya xaki sameni a cikin gidan shi kitambayeshi meya keyi?



Mutuwa ce tagifta tsakaninsu domin ko bakinsu nauyi yayi musu, sungaxa tabuka komai qarshe sai Ayashen ne tayi ta maza tamiqe xata fita, cikin xafin Nama Nana ta hanxarta ta isa ta kulle qofar falon tare da ce mata harkin isa kishigo har inda nake amma kitafi cikin sauqi.



Bansan da guxirin sharrin da kikaxo ba amma ina tabbatar miki wlh yanxu nafi qarfin jahilcin ki, idan Ada kin cutar damu kin share to kisani Allah yana juya al'amarinsa yanda yaso akuma duk lokacinda yaso.


Kallo daya xakiymin kisan nawuceki nesa ba kusa ba, kin kaini inda kike da nufin saidamin mutunci na inda xa'a keta haddina a wulaqanta ni, tana kuka tacigaba da fadin, amma dayake ban zaluntar dakeba saigashi addu'ar danayi alokacin da kika kaini gidan mala'iku suntayani miqawa gurin ubangijin rahama shikuma ya amsa min, nazamo 'yan tacciya maimakon wulaqan tarciya.


Hannu tasa akan dining  tadauko wayarta numban Haj.maryam tafara dialing cikin daqiqan da bai wuce buyu ba tadauka, kit saurin kixo sashena don Allah, abunda ta iya fadi knn ta katse kiran.


Nan take hanjin cikin Ayashe ya kada, tsoro qarara ya bayyana akan fuskarta itama wayarta ta jawo tafara dialing numban Alhaji aiko switch off taji, nan hankalinta yatashi har tarasa inda xatasa kanta taji dadi.


Bbn Yasmin takiran shidinma kiran gaggawan tai mishi inda bashiri yabaro gurin aikinshi hankalinsa a tashe domin bata taba yimishi irin wannan kiran ba, komenene yafaru sukansan dabaran da xasuyi amma basa tasoshi daga gurin aikinsa.

Haj.maryam cikin Sauri kamar meshirin tashi sama tanufo cikin side din Nanan tana xuwa tafara buga qofa babu qaqqautawa domin tajishi a rufe.


Nana ce taqarasa bakin qofan tace ki rufe qofan falon,  Haj.maryam dasauri ta rufe shi snn NANA  tabude mata qofan shigowa tayi, nan kuwa tayi arba da fuskan Ayashe sharkaf da xufa sai wuwwulla udany takeyi.


Meke faruwa? Haj maryam ta fadi cikin zaquwa taji abunda yafaru, nuna Ayashe tafarayi da hannunta snn tafara yimata bayani cikin kuka.


Zaro idanu Haj.maryam tayi waje tare da fadin yanxu baiwar Allah duk uquban da kikasa bayin Allahn nan ciki bai isheki ba, harsai kin biyota gidan aurenta? Tome kikaxo yi? Ayashe jin ance gidan auren Nana hakan yaqara hargitsa lissafinta, itakuwa Haj.maryam cewa Nana tayi mekike jira kin kira Bbn Yasmin?



Nakirashi tafada cikin sheshsgeqar kuka,  suna cikin haka sukaji ana knocking Haj.maryam ce taje  bude qofan, hanya tabashi yawuce itakuma tamaida qofan tarufe.


Yanda akaiwa Haj.bayanin Ayashe haka sukaiwa Alhaji, yasha matuqar mamakin qoqarin Ayashe da har ta iya qara kawo kanta gidan, nan shima yakirawo Bbn Nanan akaxo aka taru a falon.


Bbn Nana yana shigowa yaci karo da fuskarta baisan lokacinda bakinahi tafara bari dakyar ya iya furta Ayashe....itama qara shiga rudu tayi dalilin ganin mal.habu a wannan gidan, ga mamaki qarara akan fuskarta dakuma train tambayoyi, amma tsoron dayake cinta yadanne komai.



Ayashe meya kawoki inda muke kpdai kinxo qara tarwatsa sauran fadin cikin danake lallabawa ne? Menai miki Ayashe meyasa saini kadai, shin babu wani namiji ne a duniyar nan saini? Xuciya ce tadibeshi yayiwo kanta gadan gadan babu Wanda yayi yunqurib hanashi, domin sunbarshi kodan yarage xogin da yake cikibshi akan ta.


Saida yayi mata lilis ganin xaiyi barna yasa Alhaji yamiqe yariqeshi yafara bashi haquri kunyar Alhaji dayakeji dakuma nauyinsa da yake gani ne kawai ya ceci Ayashe a hannunsa yakoma gefe yana maida numfashi, itakuwa ko motsin kirki ba ba iyashi takeyi ba.


Alhaji ne yadaka mata wani gigitaccen tsawa yatambayeta meya kawo ta, tare da jaddada mata matuqar tai mishi qarya acikin maganar da xata gayamishi yanada hanyar da xaigano gaxkiya, matuqar yagano gaxkiya dakansa kua tasa a ranta xaisa a wulaqanta ta, kuma xata dauwama a gidan yari.


Jin an ambaci gidan yari yasata qara gigicewa nan ta xayyane mishi komai, dariyan da yasaka ne yasa kowa maida kallonshi kan Alhaji, saida yayi dariya son ransa snn yace.


Lallai kin tabka babban kuskure Wanda yaxo daidai da laipinki, Wanda ya kawokin ma ai yabar qasar, domin bayan kiran da kukaimin ina gurin aiki yakirani yasanar min yabar qasan, wayyo Ayashe tafada da qarfi tamiqe tana fadin nashiga uku yagudu da kudina.


Kowa a gurin kallonta yayi anai mata kallon wawuya,  itakuwa haukan ta taken yi tamkar wanda ta zauce,  duk kudinda ba'a tarashi ta hanyar arxiki ba to wlh baxa'a cishi da kwanciyar hankali ba. 



Alhaji ne yatambayi Bbn Nana akan wani hukunci yakeso ayi mata? Bbn Nana yace baya buqatan ai mata komai, yabarta da fitowan rana da faduwarshi yasan duk rintsi Allah baxai barta ba, xaijira har lokacin da ubangiji xai kwatar musu hakkinsu akanta, domin ganinsu cikin kwanciyar hankalinta da wadata datayi ma kadai ya isheta rashin kwanciyar hankali.


An yaba mishi sosai da irin tawakkali dakuma tsananin tausayinshi, dahaka aka sallameta, tafiya takeyi kamar xata xube qasa, mai adaidaita ta tara yakaita qofar gidanta, amma tana xuwa tasamu babu komai acikin gidan domin kuwa ansanar mata ai tun shekaran jiya aka kwashe komai na gidan ankuma saida gidan, domin yanxu me asalin gidan ne, yaturo ai mishi Dan wasu gyare gyare.



Bata San lokacinda ta tsuguna agurinba, domin kuka ma Ashe rahama ne, domin a yau nemandhi tayi tarasa, batada komai sai Dan kudaden da yake cikin jakanta saikuma gwal dinda ke jikinta na dankunne, sarqa dakuma zobe.....



A lokacin tadauki damaran bin hanyar nijar, ba ita ta isa garin maradi ba, sai misalin qarfe biyu ba dare, maimakon takwana a koda tasha ne, a'ah mummunan nufinta bai barta tarufawa kanta a siri tabari gari ya waye ba.


Haka taiwa masu mota bayanin inda takeso akaita, amma sukaqi domin daji ne meban tsoro, dakyar wani mai mota mai kwadayin tsiya ya amince sukadau hanya.



Tafiya sukayi mai tsayi kamar na yada dan shege, saida sukaxo gab da shiga qauyen, barayi suka tare su, cikin tsoro Ayashe tai aniyan neman inda xata boye gwala gwalanta, amma haqanta bai cimma ruwa ba, domin da duka suka fito dasu, cajesu sukayi sosai suka kwashe komai a hannunta, suka kora me Motan domin suna amfani da mata.


Basu jira komai ba suka haiqewa Ayashe su kusan biyar saida sukayi amfani da ita, na qarshen yace dasu matar nan oga xaiso irinta, haka suka wurga ta a mota babu alamar sassauci ko tausayi a tare dasu.


Har xasu tada motar ogansu yace karfa mukaita tunda kunga yau bamuyi ciniki dayawa ba, kawai muraba Dan abunda mukasamun inyaso muce mishi 'yan sanda ne suka biyo mu, dakyar mukasha, dariya sukayi gabadayansu suka kumayi amanna da shawaransa.



Bude motar sukayi sukaiyo wurgi da ita waje, suka bar gurin a sittin, dai sanyin asuba ne yataimaketa gurin qaramata kuxari ajikinta.


Jikinta duk jini ga masifaffen yunwa da yake cinta,tunowa datayi da halinda take ciki ne yasata kurma uban ihu, takuma manta da halin da takeciki, da gudu tayi cikin dajin taita ratsa ciyawu har ta isa gurinshi.


Xata shiga taji an wuntsulota waje, haka  taji, aiko nan taji murya kamar daga sama, ansanar dani bakyajin magana, kinje gurin mai buxu yace mikinkarki tsananta akan matunen nan, shine kikaxo ko? To kamar yanda yasanar dake baxakici nasara ba, augurin nawa ma haka abun yake, nan taji an wurgo mata wani abun kamar tumbin sa, wari ne tafara qoqarin gugitata kapin wani masifaffen qaiqayi yafara bin duk wani sassan jikinta.

Ihu tafara yi can taji hankalinta naneman gushewa, batasan ya akayi ba utadai ta tsinci kanta a bakin titi, qaiqayin ne yake qara tsananta snn a duk inda ta qaiqaya quraje ne masu ruwa tamkar bororon wuta haka yake mata, ihu takeyi tana qaiqayi kamar xautacciya.......

Fitowa ta. 6⃣2⃣



Haka taita fama da uquban wahala gata a qasan da banata ba, batada hanyar da xata koma qasarta mahaifarta, babu mahaluqin dayake mata kallon hankali balle tai tunanin xata samu mai tausaya mata, ko ina taje sai kyara da hantara saboda warin da jikinta keyi.



Lami ce kwance a kwananta na hudu a gidan Attu, matar ce ta iso gareta tace da ita, gaxkiya Lami nagaji da wannan jinyar naki Abu kamar cin kwan makauniya yaqici yaqi cinyewa? Babu dangin iya babu na Baba baxan iyaba maganar gaxkiya ki tattara kisan inda dare yayi miki.


Da kodaddun idanunta tadago ta kalleta, itakadai dakuma ubangijinta sukasan axaban da takeji a gangan jikinta, amma wannan matar batabi takan yanayinta ba taci gaba da surfa mata ruwan bala'i harsai da tasata ta dingusa dakyar tabar mata gidanta.



Dubawa tayi gabas da yamma bataga inda xata nufa domin Neman taimako ba, haka tadinga Jan jiki tanabin inuwa har tasamu dakyar ta isa matsugunin ta.



Bata samu xarafin shiga cikin gidannata ba sakamakon mai gidan data taras yaxo karban 'yan kudadensa domin shekara taxagayo samunta yayi a duqun qune a gindin bishiyar qofar gidan.


Matsowa yayi suka gaisa da dasashshiyar muryarta ta marasa lpy, nan yake sanar da ita cewa yaxo yafi sau goma baya samunta akan kudinsa, yayiwa maqpcinta magana tunda shiyayi jagora gurin kama gidan, amma meshi yace mishi yajirata duk lokacinda suka hadu suyi magana, domin shibaida hadi da ita.


A zabure tadago ta dubeshi duk da rashin jindadin da takeji a duk gabobinta hakan baihana damuwar da tashiga ta nuna ba, cemata yaqarayi Yaya kuma ina miki maganar kudi xaki tsareni da idanu?


Hawayene yafara zirarowa akan fuskarta tasjiga yimishi magiya akan danta daxai biya mata kudin bayanan snn ita batada komai domin yau sati daya kenan da aka damfare ta.



Dayake bawai yanada kirki bane, ko gexau baiji ba, yayi mata rashin mutunci tareda shiga cikin gidan dakan shi har uwar dakinta yafara watso 'yan kayayyakin ta waje babu tausayi bare kawaici, a qarshe ya wullo mata 'yar yadin tabarman ta.


Tana kuka ta tattara abubuwan nata guri daya, nadama ne yafara shiganta Wanda bata taba yin irinshi ba a rayuwarta, A yaune tayi Allah wadai da xuciyarta Wanda babu komai a cikin ta face tsantsan son kai, ta tsani mayaudariyar xuciyarta, ayaune tafara tunani dakuma Dana sanin abubuwan da ta aikata munana a baya, yau gashi an wayi gari taxama wulaqan tacciya, batada mai qaunarta babu mai tausayinta yau gashi inda xata xauna ya gagareta.


Tana durqushe a gurin tana kuka kamar ranta xai fita, a sarari take magana tana fadin " Inama ban cutar dakeba zainabu nasan duk hakkin kine dana 'yarki suke bibiyar rayuwata, da ban dagaki a Kansu ba da haryanxu ina tare dake dakuma mijinki cikin Aminci, Ayashe har inkoma ga Allah baxan yafe miki ba kin cuceni gashi yanxu nice a cikin wahala ke kinacan cikin daula, Allah kasaka min.


_Bakisan itama Ayashen halinda take ciki bane, dabaxakiy magana ba, kina maganar sakayya ai sakayyar kuke gani keda ita._

*Allah muna roqonka ka tsarkake mana xuqatan mu, kahanemu cutar da bayinka takowace siga, ya Allah karka bari a cutar damu ka tsaremu katsare mana imanin mu, Allahumma Amin* 🤲🏼



Batasan iya awannin da tadauka a wannan gurin ba, tun tanajin rana yana tafarfasa ta hartaji ranan yayi sanyi, dagowa tayi taga yamma tayi daidai dakuma dawowar baffan Sadiya, tun shigowansa Layin ya ganta amma yadauke kai gefe kamar baisan dawata halitta a gurin ba.


Ganin dagaxke yayi kamar bai ganta bane yasata miqewa dasauri kamar xata kife  a gurin sakamakon jirin da yake dibanta, qarasawa gurinshi tayi tareda kiran sunanshi.


Kamar baxai amsata ba amma haka ya juyo, fuskarnan a tamke babu alamar rahama ko sassauci a tare dashi, hakanne yasata shan ruwan jikinta guri daya, tadan tattaro nitsuwa ta axawa kanta, snn tafara sanar dashi abunda yake faruwa.


Shikuwa tamkar baitaba saninta ba yace mata to ai babu abunda xan iya yimiki akai domin kuwa ni banida kudin da xanbashi yabarki ki xauna, snn baxan qara gangancin saki ki xauna a gida ba, domin Wanda nayi a baya ma tsautsayi ne  na rashin San in ainihin ke wacece kuma gashi alhakin taimaka miki danayi kika xauna a gidana haryamxu baidaina bibiyata ba.



A don haka inamai baki shawaram kisan Inda xakisa kanki, kamar yanda nagaya miki a baya yanxuma xan nanata miki wlh xan wulaqantaki idan kika qara gigin nuna kinsanni, daga fadin haka yashige cikin gudansa yabarta dagalo da baki.


Har dare yafara yimata nan ta yanke hukuncin tafiya tasha koxata samu gurin kwana ga yunwa dayake damunta domin duk wani hanji na cikinta kokuwa da juna sukeyi suna son asaka musu wani Abu domin su warware.

Shirye shiryen biki akeyi sosai da sosai babu kama hannun yaro, yau saura kwana biyu daurin aure kuma yaune suka fara programs dinsu sakamakon daddy dayace Sudan tsagaita programs din bayason bidi'ar tai yawa.



Da yammacin ranar aka fara halarta a gurin taro, Amarya tana cikin gida itada qawayenta sai rawankai taketa yimusu, duk bayan Minti biyar saitayi waya da ibby domin tasanar mishi yanxu angama kaxa ta bangaren kwalliyarta shima kuma haka.


Qawayenta sai tsiya suke mata musamman humairah Wanda cemata takeyi sai
 kace akanta xa'a fara aure, koba akaina xa'a fara aure ba inada tabbacin nice mace tafarko da xata fara dacen nagartaccen miji a duniya, mai kyau kwarjini dakuma tarin ilimi, uwa uba sakin fuska barkwanci da sanin darajan Dan Adam.


Kwashewa dakin sukayi gaba daya 'yan matan dake dakin suka dauki tafii ana mata guda, itakuwa yanga tacigaba dayi musu, dakyar aka samu ta nitsu makeup artist dintasamu taci gaba dayi mata kwalliyar donma saida aka fitar da humairah domin itace gwanar dake tsokanarta gashi batada haquri daga humairah tayi magana xata zaburo domin bata amsa.

Gani tayi kallon nashi bamai dainawa bane yasata hura mishi ido, a firgice ya kalleta snn yasaki murmushi me hade da ajiyan xuciya, itama murmushin tamayar mishi tace kallon pa, cemata yayi My star I'm speechless narasa tayaya xan misalta kyanki, anya xan yarda ayi wannan program din? Kowa yayi ta kallenin ke?


Shagwabe fuska tayi tafara lallashinshi nan ya bingire da dariya, acan gurin taro kowa yagama halarta amma babu ango da amarya, Aliey ne axuciye yanufi gurin motar, xuwa yayi yasamu tuni Ibby kanma yashige cikin motar yakuma rufe tareda xuge glass sama................

Fitowa ta. 6⃣3⃣



Isowar IBBY cikin takaici da xaquwa ya kwankwasa musu kamar zai fasa glass din, xuge glass din yayi itakua daidai lokacin da take xabga mishi shagwaba domin batama kula da xuwan Aliey gurin ba.


Shikuma ganin hakan yasashi yin tunanin ko ganinshi ne yasata narkewa tanata xuba shagwaba wa ibbyn, abunda yasashi jin takaicin ta knn.


Cikin jin haushi tareda watsawa zarah harara snn yace kaikuma wnn wani irin salon iskanci ne anacan anata jiranku kun shanya mutane, dama kunsan bayi xakuyi ba kuka gayyato al'umma? Yaqarashe maganar haryana kunfan baki tsabar haushin su dayaji ya turniqe shi Wanda baisan dalilin sa ba.



So sorry Dan uwa wlh ganinta ne ta shagaltar dani, plx kacewa qanwar nan taka tarage wnn salon nata domin wlh xata iya zautar dani batareda na Ankara ba. Itakuwa harwani lumshe idanu takeyi cikin salon qara narkar da xuciyar Ibbyn tah..


Baibi takansu domin jiyayi yagama isowa wuya, idan kunga dama karma kuje garesu, maimakon yakoma gurin taro sai shiga motarshi yayi ya figeta a guje yabar gurin, cikin Sauri ibby yafara binshi amma kapin ya iskeshi har yabar gurin.


Dafe goshinsa yayi yace hohh Aliey maxa sarkin fushi, baiso hakan tafaru tsakaninsu a irin wadannan kwanakin ba, domin yana buqatarsa a duk cikin hidimarsa.


Yasan kuma tunda yatafi babu mahaluqin daya isa yadawo dashi, shiyasa jiki a sanyaye ya nufi jikin motar, itakanta Xarah batason hakan yafaru ba, taso ace Aliey yana gurin kodon tacigaba da shaqar dashi. Yanda taga fuskar IBBY babu walwala tasan baxaiso tai wata shirme ba, duk da tasan xai biyemata amma bacikin dadin rai ba. Shiyasa kai tsaye tamiqo mishi hannu suka fara takawa cikin gurin taron, amma a xuciyarta tana jinjinawa irin qaunar da ibby yake yiwa Alieyn.


Qawayenta ne suka taso suka tareta tareda rakata har maxauninta, bada jimawa ba aka cigaba da gudanar da taron cikin yanda aka tsara, taron ya qayatar yanda akeso saidai kawai fuskar Angon ne bakamar yanda ake buqata ba, domin Rabin taron hankalinshi nakan wayarsa yana trying numban Aliey, shidai baisan wani irin tsana Aliey yayiwa Xarah ba, koda bawai don son da yakeyiwa Xarah ba, shibaiga aibinta ba.
Haka akaita gudanarwa har aka watse...



Lami haka takoma cikin tasha daxama duk da yanda take fama da wani mahaukacin xaxxafan xaxxabi, Wanda tarasa dalili da Dan mafarinsa, gashi yanxu kanma harda xawo, don idan bata Ankara da wuri ba yanxu saita bata jikinta, haka suke yawo cikin Ahalinsu na Mabarata, idan dare yayi sunemi rumfa acikin kasuwan su kwana.



A dalilin baran ne take samu taci takuma sha, snn idan yamma tayi sundawo daga baran ta biya chemist tasayi koda paracetamol ne tasha domin jin sauqin zazzabin dayake damunta.


Tana zaune a rumfan da suke kwana ne, bayan motsin yadan lafa ta xaro 'yan kudaden da tasamo na yau, farin ciki ne ya mamaye ta domin cinikinta nayau yafi na kullum, qidayasu taqara yi dubu Bihar ne ciff, nan tamaida shi cikin lalitar ta tarufe takoma can gefe  inda makwancin ta yake, bata wuce minti biyar da kwanciya ba bacci me nauyi yayi awon gaba da ita.



Itakuwa Larabiyo tanacan akwance kamar me bacci, amma a xahiri hankalinta dakuma tunaninta gaba daya suntapi kan kudin da tagani Lami tana qidayawa.


Batun yau takeson yasheta ba, tajima da wnn burin saidai akwai wata acikinsu Wanda bata bacci wato Altine Kurma kwana takeyi sallah, baka isa kayi data wa wani a gurin ba, domin kua xata ganka, amma yau sunwayi gari 'yan uwanta sunxo sunnuna mata gata domin suma saida sukai musu kyaututtuka na ban mamaki.


Saida tabari guri yayi shiru snn tamiqe salon alin da dan tocilan ta marar haske sosai tanufi gurin kwanciyar Lami, aiko yanda tai xato hakannr ta auku domin kuwa ta baje tai dai~dai sai tashin minsharinta kakeji yana tashi a gurin.


A hankali tasa hannu ta xuge xip din lalitar takwashe kudaden cikin kaff batareda tayi tunanin halin da Lami din xata Shiga ba, tajuya abunta takoma makwancin ta kamar batai komai ba, hankalinta kwance.



Sanyin asuban daya fara bugata ne yasata miqewa dasauri damacan sallah bawai ya dameta bane, idan tafarka tanemi ruwa tawanke idonta akuma baxama cikin gari afara bara.


Da lalitar ta tafara cin karo ganinshi a wangale hannu tasa kamar wata marar rai, nan ta tarar wayam babu komai aciki, ai batasan lokacin da ta kurma wani gigitaccen ihu Wanda ya fargar da ilahirin mutanen da ke kewaye dasu.


Nan akaiyo kanta domin ba'asan meke faruwa da ita ba, tambayarta suka farayi amma bakinta yakasa furta komai inbanda lalitar ta datake nuna wa da hannunta, takasa aiwatar da komai , Larabiyo ce cikin kissa da munafurci ta taho gabanta take qara tambayarta bata bata amsa ba, amma ita tafahimci me take nupi.


Cikin siyasa tace mata kudinki aka dauke? Aiko nan tahau gyada kanta kamar kadangaruwa babu qaqqautawa gurin yadau salati wasu na tausaya mata, inda wasu kuma kemata Allah shiqara domin a cikin rumfan nasu babu Wanda baisan irin wawan baccinta ba.


A hankali aka fara watsewa akanta amma larabiyo tana wajenta tanata lallashinta, hardai tasamu ta nitsu kadan, nan suka tashi suka nufi cikin tasha domin Neman abincin kari.



Hidima akeyi sosai da sosai gida yacika maqil, sai kaiwa da kawowa akeyi a cikin gidan, daga bangaren Ango har bangaren Amarya babu Wanda yaxauna.


Nana ce a falon Haj.Maryam suna tattauna yanda xasu tsara yanda bikin xai wakana domin me aikin nasu Wanda xata aura mebawa fulayin gidan ruwa ne snn a halin yanxu yau kwananshi uku da xuwa qauyensu domin tahowa da danginsa amma annemi mahaifiyarshi anrasa sai baffansgi da aka taho dashi.


Haj.maryam ce tace to nidai kinga yanxu lokaci yafara qurewa qarfe takwas amma bamu wuce ba? Kekam ma naki abun ai yayi uku, ga Isah me aiki ga Ibby Wanda dole kiymin kara, uwa uba kuma ga 'yar dakin ki Xarah.


Murmushi Nana taimata snn tace habawa ai qafarki qafata amma insha Allah idan aka daura auren xan xo mucigaba da hidiman Isah, nifa haryau bansan shiba.


Habadai? Wlh kuwa bamu taba haduwa ba, kinsan dayake  yana xuwa makaranta da asuba yakeyin aikinshi yatafi, sallaman dasukaji ne yadadu tsagaita hirannasu ta hanyar amsa sallaman.



Iso mana Isah Haj.maryam tabashi amsa, gaban Nana ne ya yanke yafadi sakamakon hada idanu da tayi da isahn lami, shima haka xaro idanu waje yayi duk da tasauya bana wasa ba, amma hakan baxai hanashi gano fuskarta ba.


Itace tafara magana tace Isah Kaine? Nine yabata amsa jiki a sanyaye, gaisawa sukayi snn tasanar da Haj.maryam matsayin Isah a gareta, baisan lokacin da yafashe da kuka yakuma ji kunyar ta fiye da tunanin mai tunani.


Sunyi mamakin al'amarin Sosai, gabatar musu da baffanshi yayi, Alhaji suka nemo dakuma Bbn Nana sukaita jimamin abun, Aphaji dayaji xafin abun sosai ranshi Yakuma baci atake yace yayi danasanin taimakawa tsatson Lami Wanda takasance mace mai ha'inci.


Yakuma qara dacewa xai sallami Isah a gidanshi, nan baffan Sadiya yafara magiya, Bbn nanah ne yasa baki a maganar inda yaroqawa Isah alfarma, yakuma sanar dasu cewa ba halin Isah daya da mahaifiyarshi ba.


Baffan Sadiya ma yaqara musu da bayanij halinda lami take ciki da abunda aka sanar dashi dangane da ita, salati suka dauka, banda Isah Wanda yaji tamkar qasa tatsage yashige yabar doron qasa domin wata masifaffiyar kunya data lullubeshi.



Bbn Nanah yace dama shi duniya yagaji haka Indai kai cuta  to xaka hadu da daidai dakai, Allah yakyauta, Isah ne cikin kuka yafara nemawa mahaifiyarshi yafiya kapin Allah yayi haduwarsu.


Cemishi sukayi xasu yafemata duk ranar da suka hadu da zainab..............

Zarah ce a kwance a dakin tun  safiyar yau takejin wani sanyi yanabin duk wani huji na gashin gangan jikinta, ga kuma faduwar gaba data farka dashi wayarta dake ringing ne yasata jin faduwar gaban nata ya tsananta, xaro wayan tayi a qarqashin pillow nta, ganin my paramour a fuskan wayar ne yasata yin murmushi bawanda yasan tanayi domin hayaniyar da aketayi a cikin dakin...

A wannan karon cemata yayi yana son ganinta, shagwabe mishi tayi snn tafara magana kamar yana gabanta, haba Yaya nah ta ina xan ratso cikin dubban nan jama'ar nan intafi gidan Ku, Kasan dai xa'a gane gurinka xanje ko?


To saime? Yabata amsa, akwai Wanda xai hanaki xuwa amsa kiranda nai miki ne? Babu amma nidai inajin kunya babu damuwa Xarah tah baifi saura 'yan awanni bane mu mallaki juna inganki a dakina a
akan gadona a....... Wayyo nikam kadaina banaso tafada tana dariya, shima dariyan yayi yace karki ji komai xarah na I will be gentle to you kinji? Naji kawai ta iya fada, sunjima suna hira kapin daga baya sukai bankwana akan saiya kirata.

Kiy haquri kimaida wuqan ina gidansu Ango kinsan bantaba xuwa gidannaku ba, gashi jama'a suncika narasa tayamda xanfara kutsowa cikin gidan, haba dai to shknn bari insa a taho dake, to shiknn, nan suka katse kiran.

Sauran qawayensu taiwa magana nan suka mammatso kowa kuwa yabada shaidar suna kama, saidai Xarah tafi Nana haske, Xarah bata gama amincewa ba tace dasu a dakata, wayarta taxaro tadaukesu photo ta turawa ibbyn ta a watxapp snn takirashi tace yahau watxapp ta tura mishi wani Abu.



Mtswwwwww tsaki qawayenta sukayi sukace mata kedai wlh Xarah anyi jarababbiya ace komai saikin sanar dashi? Banxa taimusu tafara waqa tana fadin soyayya da dadi nayarda dakai masoyina duk don taqara basu takaici, aikuwa hakan yafaru domin himairah kasa haquri tayi ta haye kan gadon, nan suka hau damben wasa kamar dai yanda suka saba matuqar suna guri daya, dariya akai ta musu domin inda sabo sun saba da halin Xarah da humairah.



Nanah ce taita kallonsu da sha'awa tana ayyanawa a cikin ranta inama ace itama tana cikin farinciki, kuma tana cikin Ahalinta ahaknma tana godiya da Allah domin tafara tunkaran jin dadin rayuwa tunda tasamu miji takuma hadu da mahaifinta suna cikin wadata da kwanciyar hankali, amma rashin mahaifiya babban gurbi ne.



*Gurin daurin Aure*


Masallacin dake qofar gidan cike yakeda mutane maqil, babu masaka tsinke manyan masu kudi daga kasuwa har qusoshin gwamnati, motoci kuwa har titin layin saida suka kashe domin yin parking.



Angwayen ne nahango suna qoqarin Shiga cikin masallacin gaba dayansu Anko din fararen kaya ne a jinkinsu sunsha babban Riga, takalminsu baqaqe ne, hularsu  iri daya buhariyya, amma kalar na ango ya banbanta da nasu, domin cewa yayi baxaisa komai irinnasu ba don baxa'a tantance ba.



Sun matuqar yin kyau cikin shigarsu ta alfarma, idan ka kalli ibby farinciki da annashuwan dake kwance akan fuskarshi ne xai tabbatar maka dacewa shine Angon domin bakinshi yakasa rufuwa, inda shikuma Aliey kyanda yayi dakuma kwarjinin dayayi ne xai sa ka kasa tantance kodai shima auren xa'a daura masa ne. Yayi kyau harya gaji da haduwa, kowa sai binshi da idanu yakeyi duk Inda ya ratsa.


Anyi addu'oi angabatar da siga andaura aure tsakanin ibraheem da fatimatul~xarah akan sadaki naira dubu Dari lakadan ba ajalan ba.



Fitowa sukayi aka fara gaggaisawa da 'yan uwa da abokan arxiki, inda akaita daya ibby murnan aure, inda wani abokinsu mai suna mujahid yace mishi to saika dage nanda awanji Biyar ka jefa kwallonka cikin Raga, mudan cewa ka auri Xarah tauraruwa kamar yanda kake fadi.


Dariya yayi yakai mishi dukan wasa shikuma ya goce, yace mishi kallo daya xakai min Kasan ni jarumi ne ba malalaci ba, suka Dan taba barkwanci snn suka shige motocin su domin wucewa gurinda aka tanada domin gudanar da reception.

Nanah tamatso ta dafata domin a yanxu sukadai ne a cikin dakin, wawaye gaba daya suntapi wani daki domin shiryawa, kiyi haquri kinji kikuma godewa Allah domin anmiki auren gata,nan itama kuka ya kwace mata me qarfi.



Mummy ce ta tsame jikinta dakyar tanufi dakin mama, samunta tayi tana karatun qur'ani domin tanemi alfarman mummy tabarta ta kebe gurinda babu hayaniya sosai, tanason ta kebance domin Shiga hayaniyar xai iya sata jin komai yadawo mata sabo.

Fitowa ta. 6⃣5⃣



Gaba taiyo itakuma tana biye da ita a baya, taron jama'ar daketa zarya a cikin gidan yasa mummy biyowa dasu ta qofar baya,  qawayen Xarah ne suka fara gaisawa dasu, inda himairah take cewa mummy, Xarah pa mummy haryanxu batai kukan an daura mata aure ba.


Mummy ce tai dariya snn tace ai dadina da zarah itace maganin irinku 'yan sa ido, indai zarah ce sai xuciyarku tayi bindiqa da takaicinta, dariya sukayi dukansu snn suka nupi cikin dakin.


Nana ce xaune a bakin gadon, sai zarah dake hakimce a tsakiyar gadon tana waya da ibbynta, Nana ce ta amsa sallamar ganin matar dake bayan mummy yasata miqewa zumbur tareda fara goge idanunta.


Itakuwa mama kasa motsi tayi banda hannunta datake ta nunawa, Nana kuwa murxa idanunta taketayi alamar tagaxa gaxkata abunda take gani.


Xarah da mummy kuwa sai rambayarsu sukeyi cikin tashin hankali amma sungaxa samun amsa daga gurinsu duka biyun.


Abubuwa ne suka fara yawo acikin kwakwalwar Mama batasan sanda tayanke jiki tafadi ba, kanta sukaiyo gaba dayansu Nana ce tafara magana tana fadin nashiga uku naga samu naga rashi yaxanyi da rayuwata, sai yaushe rayuwa xataimin dadi?


Bawanda yakula da abunda take fadi acikinsu domin bawanda yake cikin hayyacinsa, Mummy ce tadauko ruwa tafara yayyafa mata, tadayki tsawon minti biyar kapin tasaki wani wahalallen ajiyar xuciya.


Mummy ce tace mata sannu, nan tabude idanunta tace shin abunda nagani a dakinnan gaxkiya ne kuna gani kokuwa gixo ne kamar yanda yasaba yimin akullum a farke dakuma cikin mafarki?


Subhanallah menene kika gani? Suka tambayeta a tare amma banda Nana wacce tana durqushe takasa dagowa domin jitake kamar xata rasata akaro na biyu.



'Yata nagani a dakinnan, 'yarki? Suka tambayeta a tare miqewa tayi batare da ta amsa suba, hango Nana tayi a durqushe cikin xafin nama takai hannunta kan Nana, itama Nanan dagowa tayi ta kalleta wlh itace suka juyo suna kallonsu.


Nanah ce tamiqe daga durqushen da take Nana tasaki dariya mai kama da kuka domin atare takeyinsu lokaci guda.


Rungume juna sukayi, saketa Mama tayi tajuyo tadubi mummy da zarah Wanda jikinsu yagama sakewa domin suna cikin rudu da xulumin wannan abunda yake faruwa.


Sauqowa mama tayi tai sujjada kapin tadago takallesu tace tabbas babu abun bautawa dagaxkiya sai Allah, ubangijinmu yayi gaxkiya dayasanar damu cewa dukkan tsanani tana tare da sauqi, bansan tayaya xan gode mishi ba, ubangiji yamin komai koyau na koma ga mahaliccina xan tafi inamai farincikin domin burina yacika.


Nabarku cikin mamaki, kutayani godiya ga Allah, domin yau yahadani da Ahalina, wannan itace Nana 'yata, kullum ina mafarkin inda xan ganta, Allah yaji tausayina yakawomin ita har gida, nan suka juyo suna kallonta!


Xarah ce tai saurin sauqowa ta isa gurin nanah, rungumeta tayi suka kalli juna suka sakarwa juna murmushi hawaye kwance akan fuskokinsu, Xarah ce tafara magana Ashe ke 'yar uwata ce yanda nakejinki a daina jini ne yake yawo a jikina.


Narasa yanda xan misalta tun randa nafara ganinki nake jin akwai wani Abu tsakaninmu, amma nayi tunanin tayaya haka xai kasance? Nanan ce take kallonta da mamaki akan fuskarta snn tace bangane me kike nufi ba? Tayaya naxama jininki bayan nasan nikadai mahaifiyata tahaifa?


Kallon tane Yakoma kan mummy da mama, nan mama tai mata murmushi snn tasauqo ta dafata, tace Nana labarin dayawa snn yanada tsayi dafarko kifara godewa ubangiji akan ni'imomin da yayi mana, snn inason kisa a ranki zarah qanwarkice jininku daya kamar yanda kikaji tafada miki.


Idan taro yadan tsagaita xanmiki bayani, murmushi tayi itama tacewa Maman nata, nima inada albishir dinda xanmiki amma sai taron ya tsagaita kamar yanda kika buqata, mama nah wlh nayi kewarki a rayuwa ta.



Itakuwa gwanar rahoto jiyowata sukai a bathroom tana Sanar da mijinta wani abun dadin daya samesu, Myrna tayi mata sosai  tare da cemata goron albishir dinta saisun hadu a gurin dinner. Dariya tayi tareda tabbatar mishi itama tanada surprise dinda ta adana mishi.



Fitowa tayi tasamesu suna maganar ta, nan kunya ya kamata, mamah ce tace Xarah bakida kunya ko? Harkin sanar dashi baki sanar da yayanki Aliey dakuma daddyn ki ba? Dariya tayi tace wlh namanta dasu ne, mummy ce takai mata dukan wasa itakuma ta goce snn tanufi bathroom din kuma da gudu.



Nan suka xauna suketa hiran yaushe gamo, mummy ko kiranta akaitayi a waya jama'a sunata nemanta, ta fitaa tabarsu, itakuma Nana kiran Haj.maryam tayi a waya akan taxo tanada albishir dinda xatai mata.


Bata bata lokaci ba ta iso dakin aiko ta taras da abun arxiki takuma yi murna tamakar itama mahaifiyarta ce dasuka rabu Allah yayi haduwarsu, itace nasanar da Nanah cewa ita xata yiwa Alhaji albishir, batace da ita komai ba domin tana matuqar kunyar ta akan al'amarin da ya shafi Alhaji, ko maganace Indai ta shafeshi bata iyayi a gabanta.



Amma tace mata a kunnenta cewar tacemishi  karya sanar da Bbn ta domin tana buqatar yimusu baxata amma sai xuwa dare, dariya tayi mama tace musu to tunda abun na wariya ne ai saiku cemin infita ko?


Dariya sukayi Nana ce takamo hannun Hajiya maryam tanunawa Mamanta, tace Mama duk sauyin da kika gani atare dani wannan baiwar Allahn itace sila, har Abada halaccinsu a garemu ba abune da xa'a misalta Shiba kokuma amanta, tamin komai a rayuwa ta. Bani kadai aba a cikin Ahalina.



Mama ce takalli Haj.maryam idanunta taf da hawaye tace bansanki ba amma kinmin taimakon da balallai ne Wanda yakeda hakkin hakan yayi ba, banida bakin da xan furta godiya ta a gareki saidai inai miki Fatan gamawa da duniya lpy, Allah ya albarkaci xu'arki yasa kuma kema yaranki su ji qanki, dariya tayi domin taji dadin addu'ar a qarshe tace Amin.


Hidima aka cigaba da gabatarwa cikin kwanciyar hankali, Inda aka gama shirya kai Amarya bayan sallar isha'i domin xa'aje dinner da qarfe Tara na dare.


Bayan An idar da sallan magrib ne mummy taxo domin tafiya da Xarah sashen daddy, mama taso taqi xuwa  amma mummy tanuna mata daddy ne ya buqaci hakan. Domin ta Sanar dashi komai snn shima yasanar da amininshi.


A bangaren Nana kua da yamma tasanar da Bbn ta cewa akwai inda xasuje kuma harda shi, don haka ana sallar magrib suka nufi gidan su Xarah gabadaynsu.


Kowa tana xaune a falon harsu Xarah amma ba'a fara cewa komai ba, daddy ne yaga IBBY sai kallonsu Xarah yakeyi, dariya yayi yacewa Xarah babynah tashi kidawo kuwa da mijinki ko?


Dariya sukayi a falon itakuma ta maqe kafadarta alamar baxata iya tashi tajeba, mummy ce taimata magana akunnenta tamiqe dakyar domin ba qaramin cika akayi a falon ba. Xama tayi shikuma tasaki ajiyar xuciya yacemata kokefa my star, itakadai tajishi batace mishi komai ba.

Minti biyu bayan hakan saiga sallamar su Nanah, Alhaji ne tafara sallama saikuma Bbn Nanah, abayanshi Haj.maryam ne da Nanah.



Badon dauriya dakuma taurin xuciya irinna da namiji ba, babu abunda xai hana Bbn Nanah xubewa aqasa sumamme, domin ya matuqar raxana, daganin matarshi dakuma 'yarshi Wanda shinema Yafi komai bashi mamaki, saida yaxubar da hawayen farin ciki.



Nan akaita tayasu farin ciki, daddy ne yahangi IBBY yanata duba lokaci, can ya nisa yace Ibrahim kana Cuba lokaci domin tafiya dinner ko? Sosa kai yayi yana murmushi.


Nan akai musu nasiha sosai Wanda yagama karyar da xuciyar Xarah harta fashe da kuka mai ban tausayi, IBBY ne yayi godiya ga iyayenshi, yakuma matsa gurin Bbn Nana yataya shi murnan haduwa dayayi da iyalansa lpy, Yakama qafan mahaifinshi snn yace nagode Abba. Ina roqon Ku gafara akan dukkannin laifin danai muku Wanda nasani da Wanda bansani ba, inakuma roqon kutaya ni addu'a Allah yabani ikon riqe xarah da amana.


Addu'a sukayi musu da fatan alheri daddy ne yace musu Atafi da Xarah gidanta daga can su tafi gurin dinner domin bidi'ar ta isheshi haka...........

Fitowa ta. 6⃣6⃣


Bbn Nana yayi musu godiya sosai da irin xumunci dakuma jajircewan dasukayi akan rayuwar matarshi harma da 'yarshi, haka yayita godiya har da hawaye, a qarshe dai akaita hira inda kowa a cikin xuri'ar yakejinshi kamar a sabuwar duniya. A qarshe daddy yace xa'a gyara musu aurensu da xaran taron ya watse sai su xauna inuwa daya, kowa yaji dadin wannan hukuncin nashi. Farinciki agurin su baxai misaltu ba, Bbn Nana da mama sai satar kallonsu junan su sukeyi. _tsohuwar tsumi ta motsa_


Duk irin miskilanci irinna Aliey saida ya tsinci kanshi cikin farin ciki, amma Abu qalilan yake tunawa yaji ransa ya baci, ba'a kammala taron dashiba yamiqe tayi ficewarsa.

Waro idanunta tayi waje duk da kukan da takeyi bai hanata yin mamaki tare da yin murmushi a take ba. Yanxu kam xanga murmushin ki ko? Yafada yana kallonta, godiya xata fara xuba mata amma yakatseta tahanyar cemata shhhhhhsh. Ta hanyar da xaki godemin dayane, zarah kiyiwa mijinki biyayya banida haufi akanku dukanku biyu, amma kisani Ibrahim mutunne me kawaici da haquri, inada tabbacin xakiji dadin xama dashi, kinji.


Amsa mishi tayi yace mata, Allah yayi miki Albarka 'yata Amin tace mishi janta yafarayi da labari harsaida mummy ta leqo tace kayi haquri akan garin pa xata xauna, ai xakuga juna, dariya yayi yace mata naji dai, inbadon rawan kan danaga Ibrahim nayi ba ai da sai ince abarta sai gobe, dariya sukayi ganadayan su banda Xarah data fara kukan shagwaba domin harga Allah itakam tagaji da kukan nan, sotake akaita gun mijinta yanxu kam...


Lokacin da akaxo fitowa da Xarah daga guda baqaramin turjiya tayi ba, ganin dagaxke tafiya xa'ayi da ita nan yaji tagama karaya gaba daya takuma Nuna babu inda xataje.


Dakyar aka shigar da ita tana kukan kamar wacce ranta xai bar gangan jikinta, aka kaita gidanta da qawaye kawai aka barta domin xasu shirya su wuce gurin dinner.


Shiryata akayi sosai aka fitar mata da ainihin kyanta, tayi kyau sosai idannace xan misalta muku yanayin kayanta dakuma kwalliayarta to shafinne xai qare gaba daya akanshi batare da munyi komai a cikin ta ba, kawai dai Xarah tayi kyau matuqa.


Motoci ne a fake a qofar gidan ibbyn a qalla sunkai guda Ashirin gaba daya su baqaqen, gurin mota ta shida aka nufa domin shine aka tanadar domin Amarya, kwalliyar jikin motar ma kadai abun kallo ne.


Suna isa gurin motar ta budu, hango Ibby tayi xaune a cikin motar, hannu yasa yariqo nata hannun yashigo da ita, tana shigowa kuwa motar ta koma ta rufu.


Jawota yayi jikinsa ya rungume, tasaki ajiyar xuciya ahankali ya rada mata a kunnenta, my star yanxu fa ke matata ce ko? Dariya tayi tace mishi tabbas kamar yanda kake miji a gareni.


Kallin juna sukayi, suka saki murmushi haka motacin suka fara tafiya ajere cikin tsari har aka isa hall din da aka tanadar domin yin dinner n, qara jawota yayi jikinsa dakyau tare da riqo kunkuminta, Wanda yasata jin wani shock a jikinta tadago da Sauri ta kalleshi, murmushi ya sakar mata, nan tafara qoqarin had a bakinshi da nata, xaro idanuwanta tayi waje tace mishi xaka bata kwalliyar pa.


Qara kallonta yayi snn yatuno karsu qara kwatanta abunda sukayi lokacin kamu akaita jiransu, nan tafara qoqarin akan su fito, aiko suna fitowa suka samu kowa ya fito su ake jira domin shiga dakin taron kamar yanda aka tsara.



A gurin dinner nma babu abunda IBBY yakeyi banda rawan kai domin yaqosa atashi dutafi gidansu jiyakeyi a ransa kamar yaune yafara ganinta, gakuma wata masifaffiyar sha'awsrta dayake Faman fixgarsa kamar xaiyi Yaya.


Dakyar ya lallaba yasamu har aka gama baijira suka yi tsarinsu kamar yanda suka faro taba, domin kuwa amafata yin rawa yaja Xarah suka koma mota dakanshi yaja suka nufi gidansu magana take mishi akan sujira sauran mutane mana amma baiko saurareta ba, dukaiyo gida.


Suna shigowa yarufe ko ina afalo yasauqe wani nauyayyen ajiyar xuciya domin jiyayi kamar wandayayi gudun fanfalaqi yatsira snn yasamu ruwa ya kwankwada, kama hannunta yayi suka nufi dakinsu.



Mamakin bare barensa kawai takeyi domin babu nitsuqa a tare dashi ko kadan, nan taji tsoro ya shigeta har cikinta ya duri ruwa.


Wayarshi ce tafara ringing cirota yayi a aljihunsa ganin numban Aliey yasashi dakin murmushi snn yadauka, yacemishi kai mallam Yaya haka? Yakamata kasan lokacin daya kamata kana kuran mutum mai iyali pa.


Tsaki Aliey yaja snn yace kaidao kaji kunya da bare bare karasa nitsuwarka kan wata qaramar yarinya salon ta rainaka, dariya IBBY yayi mishi yace wlh kamar kasani kai ni rainin ma nakeso, in Xarah bata rainani ba, waxata raina?



Kaika sani nikam fito kasameni ga Abu nakawo muku, OK kadan jirani in nemi ixinin matata kasan yanxu bani kadai nakeda iko da kaina ba. Dan iska idan kaga dama karka fito.


Dariya yayi snn yacewa Xarah bari infita inkarbo saqo gurin Aliey, shagwabe fuska tayi harda bubbuga qafafu akan baxaije waje yabarta a dakin ita kadai ba.


Haka dayaga darun Nata bamai qarewa bane Yakama hannunta suka fito tare, hangoshi sukayi motar abude yaciro qafarshi guda daya a waje,  waya takeyi amma tunda ya hangosu hannayensu sarqe da juna yaji ranshi ya baci, baisan lokacinda yayi hanging call dinba. Kiramshi aka sakeyi nan yayi rejecting.


Tsayawa tayi tareda riqe kunkuminta juyowa yayi yace muje manah my Star, a'ah ina jiranka anan kallonta yayi tare da kashe mata ido daya ya wuce.


Yana qarasowa yabude dayan side din yashiga, jin wayar tana ringing yasashi kallon Aliey, yadaidaita muryansa snn yace .


Dan uwa meyasa kake wulaqanta masoyanka? Meyasa Baka daukan soyayya da daraja? A yau daya inason inyi maka wani tambaya amatsayina na Dan uwanka Amininka don Allah kasaurareni.
Ina jinka kawai yafada batare da ya kalleshi ba.



Inason kasanar dani meye tsakaninka da matata, metai maka da baka sonta? Yakamata ace kodonni ka sararawa Xarah koma ace don sonda mahaifiyarka take mata, Alieyu yakamata ace Kaine mutum na farko daxaka fara gwadawa Xarah gata, nakanji ajikina kobayan raina xaka iya taimakon xarah, amma idan natuna qiyayyar da kake gwada mata jikinta yakamyi sanyi.



Yakamo hannuwansa yaqara da cewa Haidar kadubi girman Allah ka sassauta qiyayyar ka akan Xarah, xanxo qiyayyarta yadawo kaina, snn soyayyar dakakemin Yakoma kanta don girman Allah.


Kobayan raina xanyi alfahari xansan kanunamin gata, tunda kaso abunda nake matuqar qauna fiye da komai Dana mallaka. Alieyu inason Xarah sonda Kaine shaida, amma inaji ajikina baxamu Mori soyayyar mu yanda muka dayko tafiyar ba, banida tabbacin mexai faru, amma kawai daiiiiiiiii, kariqemin Xarah karka bari tayi kuka Indai Kanada ikon hanata, karka bari tayi maraici.



Juyowa yayi yace wani irin magana kakeyi ne haka? Dariya yayi sosai harda riqe cikin snn ya sassauta yace Dan iska Ashe kanasona bakason inmutu, babu inda xani insha Allah sauna mori Amarci muhaihu tareda my star, mu tsufa tare amma nudai nayi wannan roqon a karo na qarshe.


Murmushi yayi mishi Wanda yabawa IBBY mamaki, yace duk wannan magiyar akan waccar qaramar matar taka? Oho komadai jaririya ce yakaimishi duka.


To shiknn Ango xanfara Nuna mata soyayya a matsayinta na  qanwata dakuma matar Dan uwana Aminina, tafawa sukayi snn Aliey yace kar matarmu tayi fushi ka kaxa na kawo muku da madara Asha Amarci lpy, amma plx wannan rawan kan Nana karage saboda kabita ahankali ko?



To shknn ja tsamin motarka xan rufe gidana matana ta gaji da jirana kaga tsayuwa tafara gagarar ta, haka yaja mitarshi yafice sunaiwa juna bye bye......,..........

Fitowa ta. 6⃣7⃣


Dawowa yayi kusa da ita ganin ranta a bace yasashi rungumota tareda manna mata kiss a kumatunta snn Yace, my star yaukam nayi iya bakin qoqarina na shirya tsakaninki da yayanki, saura ta gurinki a matsayina na mijinki wannan umarni ne inason kiyiwa Aliey biyayya matuqar kinason farinciki na.

Dawowa falon tayi hannunta dauke da abunda mijinta yasata xuwa daukowa, xama tayi xata fara Cuba musu yace nooo my star aike yau hukumace saida rarrashi, sha xamanki ki huta gimbiya nixanyi komai, harsai randa nace kiyi.

Sorry dear basai kinyi kuka ba, bari ni inshiryaki da kaina, haka ya dagata kamar wata matacciya domin gaba daya jikinta yagama sakewa, akan gado ya direta, yafara rage mata kayan jikinta, arba yayi da halittun qirjinta nan yaji komai yana Neman kwance mishi, dakyar ya saita kansa yasamu yacire mata kayan, snn yasa mata kayan bacci.

Kinsan meye? A'ah saika fada yayanah mijina, gyara mata kwanciya yayi akan qirjinsa snn yace haihuwanki nafari inason kisa sunana, dagowa tayi ta dubeshi tace u mean sunanka xaka mayar wa danmu na fari? Bai amsata ba, yacigaba da maganarshi.



Xarah a duk halinda kika tsinci kanki inason kixama mai miqawa ubangiji al'amuranki, Xarah har abada karkiyi jayayya da hikuncin Allah akanki, kome yafaru dake a rayuwa kiss aranki cewa dama can Haka Allah ya tsarawa rayuwarki, baduk abunda mukeso Muke samu ba. 



Xarah haquri, kawaici Ibadah, wadannan Abu ukun inason kiriqesu a ranki, suxame mini makami a Duk halinda kika tsinci kanki, a qarshe kuma inadon sanar dake wani Abu Wanda baki sani ba, dab da kunnenta yafara yimata magana Wanda nikaina banji meyace mata ba, kawai naji sun bushe da dariya, wannan karon itace tafara canxa musu yanayin....

&&&&&&&&&&&&


Kamar yanda yasaba haka yauma yana cikin tafiya acikin garin nijar cikin salasainin dare, motarshi sheqe take da mahaukata kamar yanda yasaba kowacce an fesa mata hoda ta bingire da bacci.


Kai kai dakata yacewa direbansa, dasauri yataka burki yafara waiwaye kashe motar yayi yafito, sakamakom hango abunda ogan nashi yake nufi gameda dakatar dashi dayayi.



Tsallaka titi yayi hankalinshi kwance domin babu hayaniya, yanayin yanda dare ya tsala, tun kapin ya isa gareta wani wawan wari ya xuyarshi hancinsa Wanda yajishi har maqogoronsa, bashiri yayi baya kamar xai fadi, komawa yayi gurin motarsu yasanar da ogan shi.



Fuskarnan a murtuqe yace mishi kasan dai dolene adadin mutanen nan yacika domin wannan umarnin boka ne, snn kana ganin Idan mukace baxamu tafi da ita ba, gari xai iya waye mana batareda adadin mahaukatam yacika ba, wannan kuwa daidai yake da tonuwan dukkan asiri na. 


Cikin xafin nama yajuya gurin Ayashe yanufa yana toshe hanci, harya isa dab da ita baiga alamar xata gudu ba kamar yanda sauran mahaukatan sukeyi idan yaje garesu, saidai shine ya qarasa har gareta.



Itakuwa ganinshi datayi tadauka aljanine batayi motsi ba domi a halinda takeciki yanxu babu abunda tafi qauna fiye da mutuwa a rayuwarta, aganinta shuxaifi mata komai sauqi, dayaxo xai tanata kuwa jiyayi jikinta ya tamkar auduga, don yafi jikin tsoffin da sikai shekara Dari a duniya xagwanyewa, shikanshi ya tsorata matuqa domin yayi tunanin koba mutum bace, jiyayi kamar ya juya amma yatuna da kashedin  da boka yayi musu a game da hakan.



Aiko nan yafara janta har gurin motar tasu yabude gurinda suka xuba, sauran mahaukatan, Yakoma cikin ya finciki motar suka cigaba da tafiya, suna cikin tafiya saida yafesawa motar turare bila adadin amma warin Ahashe daban qamshin turaren ma daban.



Suna shiga garin jibia kua barayi suka taresu, kapin ace yayi abunda yasaba yiwa barayi idan irin hakan tafaru, saiyaga wadannan sunfishi laqani domin nasu na musamman ne, ya murxa layarsa yayi surkullensa duk a banxa, gashi babu kudi a tattare dashi balle ya sallamesu suwuce damuwarshi kar gari yafara haske asirinsa ya tonu.


Basu saurara mishi ba suka fito dasu gaba dayansu, Wanda acikinsu yasan kan irin motar yanufi Inda aka Tara mahaukatan nan yabudesu nan aka gandu burjik kamar basuda rai a jikinsu.



Xaro idanu sukayi waje shikuwa Alhaji hankalinshi ne yakai qololuwa gurin tashi ganin anata fitowa dasu, Ayashe ce taqarshe wanda dakanta tafito duk dayanda takejin axaban naman jikinta, amma ganin barayin yasata jin dadi a ranta burinta akasheta tahuta...
_nace duk abunda ya koro bera daga rami ya afka wuta to abunda yakecikin Ramin yafi wutan xafi_



Babban cikinsu ne yamatso yace da Alhaji Bala haryanxu baka daina safaran mahaukata kana kaiwa bokanka kuna lalata suba? Jiki na kyarma jin an anbaci sirrinsa yasashi saurin bashi haquri akan kome yakeso xai iya bashi, amma yayi haquri yabarshi su wuce.


Ogan bai tsaya saurarenshi ba yasaita kan bindigarsa akan Alhaji Bala, yace bakada arxikin da xaka bani domin a yanxu natara iya abunda nakeso, saboda wani dalili nawa make fashi. Kawai yau kayi bankwana da duniya nan yafasa kanshi da alburushi sai jini kake gani yanayin sama.



Bai tsaya wata wataba yaqara Harbin direban a qirji dakuma qafafu nan shima yaxube matacce, motarsu suka shige dayansu yaja na Alhaji bala suka qarawa kansu iska sukabar dajin.



A gigice Ayashe take takawa tana tafiya batasan Inda takesa qafafunta ba, tsabar rudewa, domin yau taga iyayenta a tijara, tayi tafiya mai nisa kapin ta fahimci acikin garinta take...



&&&&&&&&&&


Koda asuba tayi Xarah ce tafara farkawa ganinta tayi a rungume ajikin ibby yanata baccinsa hankali kwance, wayarta ta xare a dayan hannunsa ta kunna gani tayi fuskar wayan akan hoton ta yake da alama ita yake ta aikin kalla, murmushi tayi kapin...........





😍😍🤝🏼Anatare
Mummyn Fadyl CE

      *TEAM IBBY🤲🏼*


Fitowa ta. 6⃣8⃣



Da hanxarinta ta miqe tanufi bayi saida tadan watsa ruwa ajikinta kapin tadauro alwala ta fito sauri tayi akan tarigashi yin sallah domin tun suna gida yana yi mata gorin bata sallah dawuri.


Bayan ta idar ko addu'a bata tsaya yiba, taqarasa gurin gadon domin tadashi taimishi gorin tarigashi yin sallah yaukam...



Qarasawa tayi ta haye kan gadon tamatsa kusa dashi, rungumo hannunshi tayi a jikinta nan taji  hannun nashi kamar a sake, hankalinta bai kai gurin ba tafara mishi magana a hankali ganin bai kulata ba yasata matsawa jikin kunnenshi, hura mishi cikin kunnuwansa tafarayi nanma taji shiru.


Tun tana lallabinshi har haqurinta ya gaxa ta fashe mishi da kukan shagwaba domin tasan lagonshi matsawar yafara yi mata wasa, to indai tafara kuka xai daina, fadi takeyi wlh Kuma kokatashi kamin magana baxan kulaka ba harsai munyi sati daya, babu ruwana dakai, niba zaran ka bane.


Haka taita sunbatu amma bai kulata ba, hayewa jikinshi tayi tafara jijjigashi amma babu alamun motsi a tare dashi, cikin firgici takai kunnenta kan qirjinshi, jin bataji alamar numfashi a tare dashi ba, yasata saurin dirowa daga kan gadon tayi falo ruwa ta dauko acikin gora ta yayyafa mishi domin tunaninta suma yayi.


Nanma babu alamar motsi baya baya tafara tafiya tana girgixa kai, kamar an hankadata taqara komawa jikinshi tafada tana fadin wlh ba gaxkiya bane, yayah nasan wasa kakemin katashi karkayi irin wannan baccin.


Tadauki a qalla minti arba'in tana kwance a jikinshi ta jijjigashi taimishi magana, tunaninta yafara xarewa kamar an mintsineta aka sanar da ita kodai mutuwa yayi.


Daqarfi tace wlh baxaka mutu ba, yayah karka tafi kabarni nima mutuwa xanyi, wayyo mijina yaxanyi, gani tayi kamar yana mata murmushi dasauri ta isa gabanshi nan taga zahiri ba murmushi yakeyi mata ba amma fuskarshi a sake take.


Hannayenshi ta daga dama tasake nan taga sun sake yaraf, gigitaccen qara tasaki taxube a gurin sumammiya, gashi babu kowa a gurin.


Tun bayan dawowan Aliey daga masallaci yaji gabanshi na fadi, wayarshi yajawo domin tsokanar Ibby, domin da tunaninsu ya kwana a Daren jiya, yasan baqaramin tsiya xai mishi ba, shiyasa yafasa kirannashi, ganin envelope a fuskar wayarshi alamar shigowar saqo yasashi budewa IBBY ne. Murmushi yayi yafara duba saqon.


_Dan uwa babu abunda yafi aure da masoyi dadi a rayuwa, bansan tayaya xan sanar dakai dadin aure ba, kaidai kadage ka fitar da mata ayi maka aure, plx Dan uwa don Allah ga Amanar Xarah, kaimin gata gurin nuna mata qauna ka tausayawa maraicinta, nasan Xarah xata jigata, Xarah tanada sauqin kai, katausaya mata karka manta da alqawarin da kaimin inason duk soyayyar da kakemin ka dunqula guri daya kamayar dashi kan Xarah, nabarka lpy gwauro_


Dariya yayi gameda yin qaramar tsaki yace a fili, saikace mai bayar da wasiyya, wannan yarinyar kamar ita kadai ya mallaka a rayuwarshi, bashida buri saina a riqe mishi ita, Xarah, xarah, Xarah komai Xarah his Star..


Komawa kan gadonshi yayi yafara karatun alqur'ani acikin wayarsa kamar kullum, al'adarshi ne wannan.


Mummy kuwa a dakinta, haka kawai ta tsinci kanta tanacikin wani yanayi harwani sanyi sanyi takeji yana ratsa qofofin gashin jikinta, haka yake a bangaren Ummy domin itakan harkasa haqura tayi taje  ta sanar da Abba.


Murmushi dabai kai qasan ranshi ba, domin shima hakan take a gareshi, amma dakiya irinta maxa ya kwantar mata da hankalin ta hanyar cemata idan kika jiki a irin wannan yanayin addu'a akeyi kinji? Dakyar ta amsa mishi ta tashi tanufi kitchen.


Abincin breakfast tahada me rai da lpy tajere acikin basket snn tanufi dakin humairah, ke tashi ki kai wasu yayanki abincin kari, mutditstsike idanunta tayi snn tace Ummy wlh xakisa Xarah ta qara rainani ne, idan nakai musu nasan tsiya xatai ta min, kinsan Yayah kuma kome tayi biye mata yakeyi.


To kome xatamiki yanxukam ai dai2 ne domin wanki take aure, nikam tashi kikai musu, karkuma kiymata shirme kinsan da dayanxu ba daya bane, atoh, haka humairah tamiqe tana tura baki tashirya tadauki basket din..


Mummy ma haka ta shirya abincin nata, dakin daddy tanufa tana xolayarshi dukda babu qarfi jikknta ko kadan, tace Daddy ga abincin gidan diyarka ko xaka kai, dariya yayi mata yace yaukam baxai yiwuba domin baxanso ganinta a yanayin da take ciki yauba, yanda IBBY yake rawan kan nan, nasan baxai barmin 'yata da yanayinta dai2 ba.


Dariya taimishi tajuya tana fadin bari inje gurin wannan dannaka mai sheqen ra'ayin riqau inga koxai miqa musu abincin nan, domin babu Wanda yasan gidan acikin 'yan aikin mu, shikadai yasani don haka shixaije.


Samunshi tayi xaune yanata tilawarsa, qarasowa tayi taxauna akan kujeran dake cikin dakin, saida yakai ayah snn ya kalleta da murmushi a kwance akan fuskarsa.


Alaramma barka dai Allah yabada lada, Amin yace Yakuma qara da cewa Mummnyn Aliey kintashi lpy? Natashi lpyr dai ba sosai ba.


Dasauri ya qaraso ga durqusa a gabanta tareda dafa guiwowinta da hannuwansa duka biyu, ya kalleta yace meke faruwa dake mummy? Numfasawa tayi snn tace wlh son jikinane  gaba daya babu kwari banajin qarfi, gakuma faduwar gaba danake ji.



Gabanshi ne yafadi, snn yatafi tunanin menene yake shirin tunkarosu a wannan lokacin, dagashi har mahaifiyarsh? Amma a fili yayi murmusa yace babu komai da ixinin Allah mummy.



To Allah yasa, yanxu dai tashi plx kuma banason musu ka kai abincin nan gidan qanwarka, baisan dalili saiya kasa yi mata musu domin babu qarfin yin hakan a jikinsa, snn kuma yaji shikanshi yana buqatan xuwan To bari inshirya.



OK saika fito tajuya tanufi falo, bai bata lokaci ba, saigashi yafito mummy saina dawo, kadai dage kaima kayi auren inbahakaba wlh kai xaka dinga kai abinci gidan Xarah, dariya yayimata kawai yacigaba da tafiyarshi.



Isarshi yasamu humairah a qofar gidan IBBY sai faman buga qofar gidan takeyi,  nan ya qaraso gurinta tare da tambayarta meke faruwa, saida ta gaisheshi snn tasanar dashi abunda ke faruwa


Ciro wayarsa yayi a aljihu, yafara trying layin amma ba'ai responding ba, mummy yakira yace ta turo mishi numban Xarah, acikin sakan biyar numban tashigo wayarsa.



Nan ya kira amma still ba'ai picking ba, yakirasu a qalla yakai sau ashirin amma duka a banxa, nan yahau buga qofar cikin jin takaicinsu, yagama ayyana irin rashin mutuncin da xai musu idan duka fito.


Hatta humairah itama tagama cika, ta tsaya sai surutu takeyi acan gefe, fadi takeyi saikace akansu aka fara aure, ita dama tasani yanda suka jarabtu da junansun nan wlh tasan xasu iyayin abunda yafi haka ma.



Kiran mummy yayi a take a daidai wnn lokacin humairah ma, takira Ummy tasanar da ita tana tare da yayamsu Aliey amma sunqi budewa, shikuma cewa mummy yayi cikin xafin rai yace mummy gashinan kinsa naxo ba irin kira da kuma bugun qofar da banyiba amma basuy responding dinmu ba, ga humairah nanma da alama abinci aka bata daga gidansu.



No Haidar kasan ko IBBY xai maka haka Xarah baxatai maka ba domin kuwa tana tsoronka, snn ibbyn ma wasan baxai kai matakin haka ba, plx karka bar gurin ka bincika bana tunanin sunjika Kodai ma bacci sukeyi.



Mummy saikace masu baccin mutuwa nidai gida xandawo I'm tired nakira nema domin karkice nadawo batareda kinsani ba.



A'ah karka dawo wannan umarnina ne, karka kuskura kabar gurin saika miqa musu abincin nan, tana kaiwa nan takatse kiran, ohhh dafe kanshi yayi tsabar takaici yama rasa mexaiyi next.



A qarshe dabara tafado mishi jallabiyarsa ya nannade yahaye tasama, yabyde gate din wa humairah tashigo itama, luckily yana taba qofar falon tabudu, aranshi yace tsabar xalama ko qofar basu samu daman rufewa ba.


A falon nanma sallama yayi ta kwadawa amma shiru a qarshe yacewa humairah kishiga kidubosu domin yanxu kam jikinshi yafara sanyi.



Hakane itama gareta tafara taraddadin abunda xata tarar, a qofar ta tsaya kamar karta shiga candai tayi shahada tashige abunta kai tsaye, abunda tagani ne yaraxanata, domin IBBY ne kwance akan gadon Xarah kuma a sheme aqasa dagani dai kasan balafiya ba.



Kwalla qara tayi Wanda yasan Aliey cikin takun da baifi Biyar ba ya cimmata a dakin, abunda yagani shima ya matuqar raxani shi, gurin Xarah yafara xuwa, nan yaga bata cikin hayyacinta, yanufi gurin ibby a matsayinshi na likita bai sha wahala ba gurin fahimtar babu rai a jikin ibby.


Dakyar kamar xai fadi ya jingina da kujera humairah kuwa tuni ta barke da kuka, ganin halinda Aliey yashiga shiya qara gigitata, hannu tasa a ka tanata kurma ihu domin Tarasa mexatayi guda daya.



Binsu kawai yakeyi da kallo wata hadaddiyar ciwon kai ne yabugeshi Wanda ya tabbatar mishi B.p dinshi ne yahau a take, wayarshi datake vibrating ne yajawota a hankali domin babu kuxari a tare dashi.


Mummy CE yana dagawa yace mummy kuxo gaba dayanku harda su Abba, kawai ya kashe wayar ma gaba daya, mummy batasan lokacin da tai dakin daddy a gigice ba, nan tasanar dashi abunda Aliey yagaya mata a waya bata saurari mexaice ba tayi gidansu IBBY da Ummy tafaracin karo, tin kapin tasanar da ita abunda ke tafe da ita, ta fashe da kuka domin tasamu amsar abunda ke damunta kenan bamai dadi bane.




Mummy kuwa batama kula da kukan da Ummy keyi ba, tsanar da ita saqon Aliey akan tasanar da Abba, bata saurareta ba tajuya kamar mai shirin kifewa a qasa, a waje suka hadu gaba dayansu suka nufi gidan Ibrahim.



Suna isa suka fita gabadayansu Abba ne kawai yadan fisu dauriya,  humairah ce tajiyo shigowarsu, nan tafito tana kuka basu saurari kukan taba suka wuceta xuwa dakin, ganin Aliey sukayi kamar gunki sai hawaye ne sirara kebin kuncinsa.


Mummy CE taqarasa kan Xarah dagudu harda fadi a qasa, daddy ma haka haryafi mummy rudewa domin shi dago Xarah yayi cikin gigita, Abba da Ummy sukayi kan IBBY a wannan lokacin ne Aliey yasamu kuka ta kufce mishi.



Abba ne yafara dagowa idanuns taff da kwalla, yace saidai muyi haquri domin ibrahemm yarigamu gidan gaxkiya dai dai lokacin Ummy fadi a gurin sumammiya.



Iyakan rudani ahalin wannan xuri'ar sun shigeta a wannan ranar, nan aka dungumesu sai asibi gaba dayansu sunkasa tuqa motar, direbansu suka kira daga gida sukaxo suka kaisu asibitin, banda daddy Wanda dashine aka wuce da IBBY gida.



A daki guda aka kwantar dasu, Aliey jininshine yahau shida Abba yayinda Ummy kuma doguwar suma ne, banda Xarah Wanda akace tahadu da abunda yafi qarfin tunaninta ne Wanda yayi sanadiyyar bugawan xuciyarta a take, ....




Sundauki awanni biyar kapin suna farfado da taimakon ruwan da aka daura musu wanda yasha allurai acikinsa, Lahaula wala quwwata illah billah, innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraju'un shine abunda Abba yaketa nanatawa.



Bayan farfadowansu Abba yanemi a sallamesu domin sunada uxiri a gida, banda Xarah wacce aka barta itada mummynta a asibiti, haka jiki babu qarfi aka kwashesu a mota, isansu guda suka samu gida yacika maqil da al'umma.



Ba'a bata lokaciba gurin suturtashi aka fitar dashi domin yimishi sallah, Ai Aliey kasa tsayuwa yayi a cikin sahun sallan domin sumewa yayi a gurin, jiyakeyi kalaman IBBY na jiya dadare dakuma text dinda yai mishi suna mishi amsa kuwwa a cikin kunnuwansa.


Shigar dashi cikin gida akayi, snn aka tafi dashi gidanshi na gaxkiya, anguwarce tayi tsit kamar babu halitta acikinta.
*Allahu Akbar ,Allahu Akbar* saidai muce Allah yasa kyawawan aikinshi suy mishi jagora gurin dacewa agurin ubangijinsa.



Bayan dawowansu ne  Aliey yasan shknn IBBY yatafi yabarshi kuka yakeyi kaman qaramin yaro abokanshi sunata bashi bako amma a banxa domin bayajinsu ko kadan yakasa gaxgata yarasa abokinshi Dan uwansa.



Ummy kuwa ko magana ma kasayinshi takeyi sai girgixa kanta kawai take, Labari ya iske su Nanah suna isowa gidan bayan tagaisa da mutane ta sanar da mamanta xata tafi gurin Xarah a asibiti, domin Maman tace basai tajeba tunda mummy tanacan.........

*Ina miqa saqon ta'axiyata ga team IBBY inakuma qara Baku haquri akan rashinda akai muku, Allah yajiqanmu baki daya*
                _Nikaina banso ibbyn nan yamutu ba, amma yaxanyi labarinne yaxo a haka_ lol nikaina nayi kuka shame~shame.


Fitowa ta. 6⃣9⃣



Lokacinda ta isa asibitin itakanta ba acikin hankalinta take taka qafafunta ba, domin iya firgici tagani dajin mutuwar mutumin da jiya aka rabu dashi lpy batare da yana fama da wata lalura ba, gashi kuma alokacinda yafi kowani lokaci muhimmanci acikin rayuwarsa.


Batasha wahalan Neman dakinba domin itada Bbn ta dakuma direban gidansu Xarah nsuke tafe, isarsu dakin tasamu mummy yana xaune hannunta dauke da carbi, tanaja tanakuma addu'oi, ganinsu yasa xuciyarta qara karyewa.


Nana CE ta taho da gudu tafada jikinta, dagota tayi itanma tana hawaye tace ankai Ibrahim gidansa ko? Dakyar maganar yake fitowa a bakinta domin yanda tasha kuka, Ankaishi mummy ya jikin Xarah? Girgixa mata kai kawai take faman yi, sai Bbn Nanah ne yamatso yafara basu baki kapin sukadam tsagaita da kukan.


Mummy tasanar dashi abunda likitoci suka fada gameda ciwon dake damun Xarah. Nana qara kecewa da kuka tayi ta matsa gurin gadon ta riqe dayan hannun xaran Wanda babu ruwa a jiki ta manna shi a jikinta, tana fadin sannu 'yar uwa ba shakka mukam munga jarabawa a rayuwarmu.


Ke kadaice a tsakaninmu kikai rayuwa mai sauqi tun haihuwarki, kika taso cikin gata, amma saigashi kinga jarabawarda take baraxana da rayuwarki, mutuwar miji babu dadi, mijinma na kwana daya, Wanda kuka gama tsara yanayin rayuwar da xakuyi tare.



Ya Allah idan wani laipi muka aikata kake hukuntamu akansa Allah muntuba, Allah ga qanwata Allah ka tausaya mata kabata lpy, badon halinmu da aiyukan mu ba, Ya Allah Xarah tayi qanqanta.


Kulll taji muryan Bbn ta abayanta, Nanah Yaya kike shirin kauce hanya kuma? Kinmanta Allah shiyake tsarawa bawansa rayuwar da ta dace dashi? Kidaina fadin haka godiya xamuyi ga mahaliccin mu domin yana kawo mana mafita aduk lokacin da muka rasa *TUDUN DAFAWA* juyawa yayi yabar dakin sakamakon xuciyar shi data gama karyewa baya kuma buqatar Nanan taga hakan, idan shi yayi kuka dawani baki xai rarrasheta.



Komawa qasa tayi ta xube tana kuka mai tsuma xuciya tare kuma dayin duk addu'an dayaxo bakinta, mummy ma haka kukan takeyi kashi biyu, tana tausayin halinda su Ummy xasu shiga dajin wannan mutuwar data afko musu lokaci guda, A dayan bangaren kuma tana tausayin diyarta domin ita shaidace akan qaunar da take yiwa ibby, bata hadashi da kowa acikin xuciyarta ba.



Tana cikin wannan tunanin, xuciyarta ta tunano  mata Aliey, cikin Azama ta xaro wayarta layinshi takira, yana cikin kuka abokinshi yamiqo mishi wayar, ganin _my pride_ a fuskar wayar yasashi jin faduwar gaba, domin bai San mexata sanar dashi ba, yana dagawa yace mummy Xarah ma tamutu ko?



Cikin kuka tace mishi ka kwantar da hankalinka Xarah tananan da ranta, mummy narasa IBBY antafi dashi mummy yaxanyi dawa xanyi wasa da dariya a gidannan waye xai tunkareni ya bugi qirjina yasanar dani gaxkiya koda banaso, mummy meyasa IBBY yatafi? Wayyo mummy Ibrahim yatafi ya fashe da kuka kamar qaramin yaro.


Kalamanshi qara jikkata ta sukayi, tarasa mexatace mishi, tarasa yanda xata lallasheshi, kawai saita kashe wayar domin baxata iya cewa komai ba, kuka takeyi kamar ranta xai bar gangan jikinta.



Ana xaune gurin karban xuciya, Abba ne yataso yacewa daddy, yakamata adubo matar marigayi kawai tadawo gida, inyaso ayita dubata a gida, inaga hankalinmu xaifi xama guri daya ko?



Daddy ne yadago jajayen idanunsa, domin shima kuka yayi bana kadanba, Abba yafishi dauriya nesa na kusa ba, wasu sabbin hawayen ne ke neman gangaro mishi, Abba yayi qoqarin maida nashi snn yadafashi yace haba Yallaboi meyasa kake hakane saikace yaro please kukan nan ya isa haka, ibraheem addu'arka yake buqata a yanxu shine kadai gatan da xaka nuna mishi Yakuma isa gareshi.


Miqewa yagi yana share hawayenshi da handkerchief din dake hannunsa, domin bayajin xai iyayin dogon magana, matuqar yace xai takura kanshi akan saiyayi kuwa ba shakka kuka ne xai kwace mishi me qarfi Wanda xai janyo hankalin mutane kanshi.



A mota Abba yacigaba da bashi baki, tsananin dauriya da tawakkali irinna Abba kowa saida ya jinjina masa, tafiyarsu asibitin ma dakanshi yajasu dashi da daddy dakuma wasu abokansu guda biyu.


Sun tarar da mummy da Nanah babu mai rarrashin Dan uwansa, Abba yafara qoqarin basu haquri, daddy ya katseshi cikin kuka yace mishi, kabarsu suyi kuka domin anyi rashin nagartaccen namiji, mai tausayi haquri kawaici dakuma jinqan iyaye, dole suyi kuka rashin mutum irin Ibrahim abun ayi kuka ne.


Hummm kawai Abba yake fadi, domin shikansji kukan yakeso yayi koxaiji sauqin yanda xuciyarshi dakuma qirjinsa kemishi xafi kamar xasu hudo Riga sufito waje haka yakeji, sun sanar da mummy akan xa'a tafi da Xarah gida, batace komai ba, nan suka karbo sallama daddy ne yasa hannu ya ciccibeta kamar qaramar yarinya yasakata a mota, suka shiga sukayi gida da ita.



Dakin mummy aka ajiyeta snn kowa yawuce maxauninsa, Nanah ce ke xaune a gefenta, mummy taje dubo su Ummy dakuma Aliey.



Kuka sukayi sosai itada ummy, tareda yiwa junansu ta'axiya nan take tambayarta labarin Xarah,nan tasanar da ita komai nagameda halinda ake ciki. Ikon Allah kawai tace.



Bayan mummy tadan gaggaisa da mutane, ta isa gurin Maman Nana tasanar da ita halinda ake ciki, snn tace mata Xarah tana dakinta ta gashi suje taganta, bata musa ba ta bita a baya, suka nugi dakin Mummyn.


&&&&&&&&&&&&&&



A wahalce Ayashe tayi tafiya mai nisa Wanda wahalanshi da nisanshi ya haddasa mata masifaffen qishin ruwa, ga ciwon qafafu Wanda dakyar take dagasu tsantsan axaba, itakanta tanajin warin jikinta tadai rasa yanda xatayi da yanayin ne itakanta.


Batasan ya akai ba taganta a cikin tasha, xama tayi a wani gindin mota tana maida numfashin wahala, ruwa ta hango a buta aiko a guje tanufeshi ta dauka ta kwankwada bata gama shaba saiga wani mutum ya hangota, Sam baxakai mata kallon mai hankali ba, domin gaba daya kamanninta sun sauya, koda wasa baxakace Ayashe bace tun tana xaman qauye balle dataje burni, acikin 'yan watanni tasauya gaba daya, shiyasa yana xuwa da sandansa baiyi wata wataba ya xula mata a bayanta tasake butar daqarfi.


Aiko yafara korarta irin koranda akewa mahaukata, fadi yakeyi wadannan sabbin mahaukatan narasa mesuke nema da wannan rumfan nawa, basuda gurin shan ruwa sai nan.



Mamakine yakamata domin kuwa tasanshi maqocinta ne, nan tahau kalle kalle bashakka garinsu taxo, bataji wani dadi acikin ranta ba, domin baxata iya tinkaran kowa a garinba, don kuwa batai wa kowa abun arxiki ba, tsakaninta da bayin Allah wulaqanci cin Amana dakuma rashin mutunci.



Cikin sauri tabar mishi rumfar tanajan qafanta tana kuka, tana kuma danasanin rayuwarta, tana tunanin tayaya xata gyara laifukan da ta aikata, a ina xataga al'ummar data xalunta tanemi yafiyarsu koxata samu sauqin axaban datake fuskanta, gashi a kullum kamar ninka axaban da ake yi mata akeyi.



Xuwa tayi can gurin da aka tanada domin mabarata, taxauna domin tadan huta kapin ta fita tadanyi bara koxata samu abunda xatasamu abunda xatasa abakin salati, Mabaratan dasuke gurinne sukaiyo kanta domin wani warin dayake tasowa a jikinta kamar danyen mushe..........

Mummyn Fadyl CE
